### [1] [Network Edge Inference for Large Language Models: Principles, Techniques, and Opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22906)
*Zhixiong Chen,Bingjie Zhu,Jiangzhou Wang,Hyundong Shin,Arumugam Nallanathan,Dusit Niyato*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于大语言模型（LLM）边缘推理的综述，系统总结了由于内存和计算需求巨大而在边缘端部署LLM面临的挑战，并从系统架构、模型优化部署及资源管理调度等方面梳理了最新研究进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在语言理解、生成和推理方面表现卓越，但因其巨大的内存与计算开销，在资源受限的网络边缘进行推理部署仍极具挑战，亟需系统性总结该领域现状。

Method: 该综述对LLM边缘推理特有的挑战进行了界定，并从三个核心维度展开全面调研：系统架构设计、模型优化与部署策略、以及资源管理与调度机制，综合分析了最前沿的技术方案。

Result: 本文提供了针对LLM边缘推理的全面技术图景，涵盖了当前最先进的系统架构、模型压缩与优化技术，以及高效的资源调度方法，揭示了各项技术的核心思路与关联。

Conclusion: 通过整合现有技术与展望未来研究方向，本综述旨在解锁大语言模型在资源受限边缘环境中的部署潜力，为后续研究与实践提供明确指引。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have advanced rapidly, emerging as versatile tools across fields thanks to their exceptional language understanding, generation, and reasoning capabilities. However, performing LLM inference at the network edge remains challenging due to their large memory and compute demands. This survey outlines the challenges specific to LLM edge inference and provides a comprehensive overview of recent progress, covering system architectures, model optimization and deployment, and resource management and scheduling. By synthesizing state-of-the-art techniques and mapping future directions, this survey aims to unlock the potential of LLMs in resource-constrained edge environments.

</details>


### [2] [GreenDyGNN: Runtime-Adaptive Energy-Efficient Communication for Distributed GNN Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23139)
*Arefin Niam,Tevfik Kosar,M. S. Q. Zulkar Nine*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: GreenDyGNN通过强化学习动态调整分布式GNN训练中的缓存策略，在网络拥塞情况下比现有方法节省高达43%的能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式GNN训练中远程特征获取成本高昂。传统静态缓存策略无法应对运行时网络拥塞变化，导致能耗最高增加45%。

Method: 将缓存窗口管理建模为序列决策问题，采用Double-DQN智能体在仿真环境中训练，动态调整缓存重建窗口大小和分配策略；配合异步双缓冲流水线实现零开销适应。

Result: 在拥塞环境下，GreenDyGNN相比Default DGL节省高达43%总能耗，比最佳静态策略节省4-24%；在无拥塞时性能接近最优。

Conclusion: 基于强化学习的动态缓存管理能有效适应网络条件变化，显著降低分布式GNN训练能耗。

Abstract: Distributed GNN training is dominated by remote feature fetching, which can be very costly. Multi-hop neighborhood sampling crosses partition boundaries and triggers fine-grained RPCs whose fixed initiation cost and GPU-stall latency waste energy. Prior systems try to reduce this overhead with presampling and static caching, but cache policies cannot react to runtime network variation. We show that under time-varying congestion, static caching can increase energy by up to 45% because a fixed rebuild schedule is insufficient. We present GreenDyGNN, which formulates cache window management as a sequential decision problem. GreenDyGNN performs intra-epoch cache rebuilds and uses a Double-DQN agent, trained in a calibrated simulator with domain-randomized congestion, to adapt rebuild window size and per-owner cache allocation at each boundary. An asynchronous double-buffered pipeline makes adaptation effectively free. Under congestion, GreenDyGNN cuts total energy by up to 43% over Default DGL and 4-24% over the best static policy, while closely matching the optimum under clean conditions.

</details>


### [3] [The Blockchain Execution Dilemma: Optimizing Revenue XOR Fair Ordering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23266)
*Artjom Pugatsov,Can Umut Ileri,Jérémie Decouchant*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种动态优化框架和遗传算法来优化区块链交易排序，在提升验证者收益约15%的同时加速拥堵缓解达58%，并发现严格的公平排序约束会使验证者收入减少50-60%，实质上抵消了高级排序的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着共识算法的发展，区块链的性能瓶颈已转移到执行层。现有并行化执行方案忽视了交易排序的关键作用。传统排序方式要么将排序权交给验证者（易受MEV攻击），要么采用僵化的公平排序协议（限制验证者收入），两者之间存在张力。

Method: 提出了一个独立于区块链的连续时间交易排序模型，允许区块执行重叠。在该框架下，设计了一种随时遗传算法，利用Gas价格、对象集和预测执行时间来优化调度方案。使用Sui和以太坊的真实数据集进行评估。

Result: 所提算法将验证者利润提高约15%，将拥堵缓解速度提升高达58%。量化分析显示，公平排序约束在高拥堵期间会使验证者收入减少50%至60%。首次证明严格的公平排序实际上会抵消高级排序带来的优势。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了验证者收益与排序公平性之间的内在矛盾，证明了严格公平排序会显著降低验证者收入并抵消排序优化的益处，为区块链交易排序机制的设计提供了重要启示。

Abstract: The successive generations of consensus algorithms have progressively shifted the performance bottleneck of blockchains to the execution layer. While recent works address this by parallelizing transaction execution, they often overlook the critical role of transaction sequencing. Historically, transaction ordering was left to validator discretion, a practice prone to Maximal Extractable Value (MEV) attacks, or rigid fair-ordering protocols that limit validator revenue. In this work, we address the tension between validator revenue and order fairness using a dynamic optimization framework. We introduce a blockchain-independent model for transaction sequencing in a continuous setting where block executions can overlap. Within this framework, we propose an anytime genetic algorithm that utilizes gas prices, object sets, and predicted execution times to optimize schedules. We evaluate our approach with real-world datasets from Sui and Ethereum, and demonstrate that our algorithm increases validator profit by approximately 15% and accelerates congestion relief by up to 58%. Furthermore, we quantify the impact of fair-ordering constraints, showing they can reduce validator revenue by 50% to 60% during periods of high congestion. We provide the first evidence that enforcing strict fair ordering effectively nullifies the advantages of advanced sequencing.

</details>


### [4] [A Taxonomy and Resolution Strategy for Client-Level Disagreements in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23386)
*Daan Rosendal,Ana Oprescu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对联邦学习中的“客户端分歧”问题（即客户端因策略、法规或竞争原因需相互排斥），提出了多轨道解决策略，通过隔离模型更新路径实现严格的客户端排除，在保证可扩展性的同时解决了跨污染和不公平问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习通常假设客户端无条件协作，忽略了现实多利益相关者环境中因策略、法规或竞争原因导致客户端需相互排斥的复杂情况（即“客户端分歧”），这限制了联邦学习在实际合规与战略控制场景中的应用。

Method: 提出一种多轨道解决策略，通过创建和管理隔离的模型更新路径（轨道）来保证严格的客户端排除，防止朴素策略中的跨污染和不公平问题；并引入子模型重用策略以缓解客户端参与多轨道训练的计算负载。

Result: 在MNIST和N-CMAPSS数据集上的34个场景实验验证了该方法能有效处理永久性、时序性和重叠性分歧模式；可扩展性分析显示服务端解决算法开销可忽略不计（每轮<1ms），主要约束是客户端多轨道训练负载，但可通过子模型重用策略有效缓解。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种可扩展且架构稳健的客户端分歧管理方法，增强了联邦学习在策略合规与战略控制不可妥协场景中的实际适用性。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) typically assumes unconditional collaboration, a premise that overlooks the complexities of real-world, multi-stakeholder environments in which clients may need to exclude one another for strategic, regulatory, or competitive reasons. This paper addresses this gap, which we term 'client-level disagreements,' by first introducing a taxonomy of such scenarios. We then propose a robust, multi-track resolution strategy that guarantees strict client exclusion by creating and managing isolated model update paths ('tracks'), thereby preventing the cross-contamination and unfairness issues present in naive strategies. Through an empirical evaluation of our custom simulation system across 34 scenarios using the MNIST and N-CMAPSS datasets, we validate that our approach correctly handles permanent, temporal, and overlapping disagreement patterns. Our scalability analysis reveals the server-side resolution algorithm's overhead is negligible (<1 ms per round) even under heavy load. The primary scalability constraint is the client-side training load from participating in multiple tracks, a cost that we show can be effectively mitigated by a submodel reuse strategy. This work presents a scalable and architecturally sound method for managing client-level disagreements, and enhances the practical applicability of FL in settings where policy compliance and strategic control are non-negotiable.

</details>


### [5] [ClusterFusion++: Expanding Cluster-Level Fusion to Full Transformer-Block Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23553)
*ChiHeng Jin,Hongche Yu,Xihui Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ClusterFusion++通过CUDA级扩展实现Transformer解码器全块融合，在RTX 5090上将Pythia模型吞吐量提升1.34倍，同时保持高输出保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型解码过程对延迟敏感，主要受限于碎片化的算子执行和中间张量的重复片外物化。现有工作仅能融合部分注意力侧算子，无法覆盖完整的Transformer解码器块，导致性能优化空间受限。

Method: 开发ClusterFusion++，一种CUDA级扩展，将融合范围扩展到完整的Transformer解码器块（包括LayerNorm、QKV、RoPE、解码注意力、输出投影、Post-LN、MLP和残差连接）；设计兼容CUDA-Graph的执行模式，使用持久化张量内存加速器(TMA)描述符来减少每步开销。

Result: 在NVIDIA RTX 5090级别GPU上，ClusterFusion++使Pythia-2.8B的吞吐量提升1.34倍，Pythia-6.9B获得类似增益；同时保持高输出保真度（生成结果几乎token级一致，仅因FP16原子操作存在微小非确定性）。

Conclusion: ClusterFusion++成功实现了Transformer解码器块的全面融合，显著提升了解码吞吐量，证明了在CUDA级别扩展融合范围的有效性，为LLM推理优化提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) decoding is latency-sensitive and often bottlenecked by fragmented operator execution and repeated off-chip materialization of intermediate tensors. Prior work expands fusion scope by leveraging thread-block clusters and on-chip inter-block collectives to fuse attention-side operators such as QKV projection, attention, and output projection.
  We develop ClusterFusion++, a CUDA-level extension that broadens fusion to the full Transformer decoder block for GPT-NeoX/Pythia models: LayerNorm -> QKV -> RoPE -> decode attention -> output projection -> Post-LN -> MLP -> residual. We additionally engineer a CUDA-Graph-compatible execution mode with persistent Tensor Memory Accelerator (TMA) descriptors to reduce per-step overhead.
  On an NVIDIA RTX 5090-class GPU, ClusterFusion++ improves throughput by 1.34x for Pythia-2.8B and yields similar gains for Pythia-6.9B, while maintaining high output fidelity (near-token-identical generation, with minor non-determinism from FP16 atomics).

</details>


### [6] [Towards System-Oriented Formal Verification of Local-First Access Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23560)
*Florian Jacob,Johanna Stuber,Hannes Hartenstein*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文使用Rust语言和Verus验证框架，针对支持拜占庭容错的本地优先系统（如Matrix和Keyhive），构建了形式化验证的访问控制算法，并贡献了一个管理协作组的复制数据类型的形式化语义与不变式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无冲突复制数据类型（CRDTs）和本地优先概念正被广泛应用于大规模系统（如Matrix和Keyhive）。由于参与者之间不再相互信任，系统需要拜占庭容错和细粒度访问控制。然而，目前的Matrix和Keyhive仅有非形式化规范和未经验证的参考实现，缺乏形式化保证。

Method: 采用自底向上的方法进行形式化验证。使用Rust编程语言进行形式化规范、验证和实现，利用Verus框架和Z3定理证明器实现零运行时开销。提出了一个用于管理简化协作组的复制数据类型，基于能力（capabilities）进行访问控制，并使用哈希编年史（hash chronicles）进行复制。

Result: 贡献了用于管理简化协作组的复制数据类型的形式化语义和不变式，作为形式化验证的中间目标。报告了使用系统导向形式化验证方法的经验和初步结果，验证了该方法的潜力。

Conclusion: 虽然将该方法扩展到现实世界本地优先访问控制系统的复杂性仍是未来工作，但研究结果证明了使用Verus进行系统导向形式化验证的潜力，并为未来提高访问控制表达能力的扩展奠定了基础。

Abstract: Conflict-free replicated data types (CRDTs) and the local-first concept are increasingly employed not only in small-scale collaboration systems among few users who trust each other, but also in large-scale systems, like Matrix for instant messaging and Keyhive for collaborative documents. Since mutual trust is no longer warranted, these systems require Byzantine fault tolerance and fine-grained access control. As of today, Matrix and Keyhive pair an informal specification with an unverified reference implementation. In this work, we follow a bottom-up approach towards constructing formally verified authorization algorithms for Byzantine fault-tolerant local-first systems. As intermediate target for formal verification, we contribute semantics and invariants of a replicated data type for managing simplified collaboration groups, based on capabilities for access control and hash chronicles for replication. To enable future integration into local-first systems like Matrix and Keyhive, we strive for accessibility to system engineers by using the Rust programming language for formal specification, verification, and implementation, enabled by the Verus framework using the Z3 theorem prover at zero runtime cost. We report on our experience and preliminary results following this approach, and discuss next steps towards scaling up access control expressiveness. Whether this approach can be scaled up to the complexity of real-world local-first access control systems like Matrix or Keyhive remains future work, but our findings demonstrate the potential of system-oriented formal verification with Verus.

</details>


### [7] [SDSL-Solver: Scalable Distributed Sparse Linear Solvers for Large-Scale Interior Point Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23979)
*Shaofeng Yang,Yunting Wang,Yingying Cheng,Fan Zhang,Xin He,Guangming Tan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了SDSL-Solver，一个面向内点法的可扩展分布式稀疏线性求解器框架，在多节点集群上相比PETSc和PARDISO实现了显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏线性系统的求解是内点法的主要计算瓶颈，常占用超过70%的总求解时间。随着优化问题规模扩展到数百万变量，直接求解器面临填充增加、内存消耗过大和并行可扩展性受限等问题。

Method: SDSL-Solver采用Krylov子空间方法，结合基于数值的稀疏过滤和对角校正技术构建高质量预条件子。针对不同问题特性，提供两种互补的分布式并行方法：适用于良态、对角占优系统的块Jacobi方法，以及适用于病态问题、通过Schur补技术实现全局耦合预条件的带状块对角（BBD）方法。此外，采用预条件子重用策略以分摊连续IPM迭代中的构建成本。

Result: 在矩阵维度从数万到超过五百万的基准问题上，四节点配置下的SDSL-Solver相比同节点数的PETSc，块Jacobi和BBD方法分别实现了6.23倍和7.77倍的平均加速；相比单节点PARDISO，平均加速分别达到97.54倍和5.85倍。

Conclusion: SDSL-Solver通过结合高效的预条件技术和分布式并行策略，有效解决了大规模IPM中稀疏线性系统求解的计算瓶颈，在多种问题规模和特性下均展现出优越的求解性能和可扩展性。

Abstract: The solution of sparse linear systems constitutes the dominant computational bottleneck in interior point methods (IPMs), frequently consuming over 70\% of the total solution time. As optimization problems scale to millions of variables, direct solvers encounter prohibitive fill-in, excessive memory consumption, and limited parallel scalability. We present SDSL-Solver, a scalable distributed sparse linear solver framework designed for IPMs. SDSL-Solver employs Krylov subspace methods, combined with numerics-based sparse filtering and diagonal correction techniques that produce high-quality preconditioners. To accommodate diverse problem characteristics, SDSL-Solver offers two complementary distributed parallel methods: Block Jacobi for well-conditioned, diagonally dominant systems, and Bordered Block Diagonal (BBD) for ill-conditioned problems requiring globally coupled preconditioning via Schur complement techniques. A preconditioner reuse strategy further amortizes construction costs across consecutive IPMs iterations. We evaluate SDSL-Solver on benchmark problems with matrix dimensions ranging from tens of thousands to over five million on multi-node clusters equipped with X86 processors. The experimental results show that under the Block Jacobi and BBD distributed methods, SDSL-Solver on a four-node configuration achieves average speedups of $6.23\times$ and $7.77\times$, respectively, compared to PETSc running on the same number of nodes. Relative to the single-node PARDISO, the average speedups reach $97.54\times$ and $5.85\times$, respectively.

</details>


### [8] [KubePACS: Kubernetes Cluster Using Performant, Highly Available, and Cost Efficient Spot Instances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24027)
*Taeyoon Kim,Kyumin Kim,Enrique Molina-Giménez,Pedro García-López,Kyungyong Lee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出KubePACS系统，通过多目标优化实现了云竞价实例的成本、性能和可用性联合优化，相比现有方案性能/价格比平均提升55.09%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有竞价实例供应系统主要关注成本和可用性的权衡，忽略了不同实例类型间的硬件性能差异，仅基于最小资源需求选择节点，导致无法充分利用硬件性能优势来优化工作负载执行效率。

Method: 提出KubePACS系统，将节点选择建模为多目标优化问题，整合竞价价格、性能基准和可用性评分（包括多节点Spot Placement Score）等实时数据。采用整数线性规划(ILP)结合黄金分割搜索(GSS)算法高效求解最优配置。与Karpenter节点自动扩缩器集成，联合优化实例类型选择和节点扩缩决策；引入新颖的启发式方法，通过缩放专用实例的性能指标来支持特定工作负载的偏好。

Result: 在合成和真实工作负载上的广泛评估表明，KubePACS相比Karpenter、SpotVerse和SpotKube等最先进方案，性能/价格比平均提升55.09%，最高提升81.06%。

Conclusion: 通过同时考虑成本、性能和可用性三个维度，KubePACS成功克服了现有系统仅关注竞价实例价格和有限可用性数据的局限性，为云用户提供了更优的竞价实例供应解决方案。

Abstract: Cloud users aim to minimize cost while maximizing performance by selecting the most suitable instance types for their workloads. To reduce expenses, spot instances have been widely adopted due to their steep discounts compared to on-demand pricing. However, their use introduces reliability risks due to potential interruptions, and existing research has primarily focused on mitigating this trade-off from a cost or availability perspective alone. Despite the diversity in hardware capabilities among instance types, current provisioning systems tend to ignore performance variation, selecting nodes solely based on minimum resource requirements.
  In this paper, we present KubePACS, a Kubernetes-native spot instance provisioning system that constructs node pools optimized for both cost and performance while guaranteeing high availability. KubePACS formulates the node selection process as a multi-objective optimization problem, incorporating real-time data such as spot prices, performance benchmarks, and availability scores, including the multi-node Spot Placement Score (SPS). It solves this problem efficiently using an Integer Linear Programming (ILP) approach guided by the Golden Section Search (GSS) algorithm to find the optimal configuration. By integrating with the Karpenter node autoscaler, KubePACS jointly optimizes instance-type selection and node scaling decisions within a standard provisioning workflow. KubePACS also adopts a novel heuristic to support workload-specific preferences by scaling performance metrics for specialized instances. Through extensive evaluation across synthetic and real-world workloads, KubePACS demonstrates on average 55.09% and up to 81.06% higher performance per dollar over state-of-the-art solutions such as Karpenter, SpotVerse, and SpotKube, which only reference the spot instance prices and limited availability data.

</details>


### [9] [TACO: Efficient Communication Compression of Intermediate Tensors for Scalable Tensor-Parallel LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24088)
*Man Liu,Xingchen Liu,Xingjian Tian,Bing Lu,Shengkay Lyu,Shengquan Yin,Wenjing Huang,Zheng Wei,Hairui Zhao,Guangming Tan,Dingwen Tao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出TACO框架，通过FP8量化和算子融合优化大规模张量并行训练的通信开销，实现1.87倍吞吐提升且精度无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模张量并行训练中的通信开销是一个关键挑战，因为中间张量呈密集且接近零的分布，在频繁通信下会加剧误差，且压缩过程会引入显著的计算开销。

Method: 提出TACO框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 数据驱动的重塑策略结合自适应尺度Hadamard变换，实现高保真FP8量化；2) 双尺度量化机制确保数值稳定性；3) 设计高度融合的压缩算子减少内存流量和内核启动开销，实现与通信的高效重叠。最终将其与数据和流水线并行方法集成，构建支持压缩的3D并行训练框架。

Result: 在GPT和Qwen模型上的实验表明，TACO实现了高达1.87倍的端到端吞吐提升，同时保持近乎无损的精度。

Conclusion: TACO有效解决了大规模张量并行训练中的通信开销问题，在保持训练精度的同时显著提升了训练效率，验证了其在大规模训练中的有效性和高效性。

Abstract: Handling communication overhead in large-scale tensor-parallel training remains a critical challenge due to the dense, near-zero distributions of intermediate tensors, which exacerbate errors under frequent communication and introduce significant computational overhead during compression. To this end, we propose TACO (Tensor-parallel Adaptive COmmunication compression), a robust FP8-based framework for compressing TP intermediate tensors. First, we employ a data-driven reshaping strategy combined with an Adaptive Scale-Hadamard Transform to enable high-fidelity FP8 quantization, while its Dual-Scale Quantization mechanism ensures numerical stability throughout training. Second, we design a highly fused compression operator to reduce memory traffic and kernel launch overhead, allowing efficient overlap with communication. Finally, we integrate TACO with existing state-of-the-art methods for Data and Pipeline Parallelism to develop a compression-enabled 3D-parallel training framework. Detailed experiments on GPT models and Qwen model demonstrate up to 1.87X end-to-end throughput improvement while maintaining near-lossless accuracy, validating the effectiveness and efficiency of TACO in large-scale training.

</details>


### [10] [Unfolding an Atomistic World: Atomistic Simulation of Reactor Pressure Vessel Steel Across Year-and-Meter Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24091)
*Haozhi Han,Ruge Zhang,Haoquan Chen,Yifeng Chen,Haipeng Jia,Liang Yuan,Yunquan Zhang,Ting Cao,Yunxin Liu,Ya-Qin Zhang,Kun Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出AtomWorld框架，首次实现反应堆压力容器钢从原子尺度到工程尺度（年-米级）的直接模拟，峰值性能达1.27 EFLOP/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反应堆压力容器(RPV)钢寿命预测需要跨越原子尺度退化机制与服务尺度(从埃和皮秒到米和数十年)之间的鸿沟。现有工程尺度模型依赖拟合的退化定律，而原子动力学蒙特卡洛(AKMC)方法无法达到年-米尺度覆盖。

Method: 提出AtomWorld原子世界建模框架，包含三层协同设计：(1)算法层：将经典AKMC重构为原子世界模型，在从头算能量景观上学习后果感知的状态转移；(2)高性能计算层：与现代超级计算机协同设计，构建计算密集、同步轻量、通信高效的执行流水线；(3)应用层：通过物理基础的体素并行框架将原子世界建模扩展到工程尺度模拟。

Result: 首次实现RPV钢在年-米尺度的原子模拟，扩展到十万亿亿原子系统，模拟一个服务年的求解时间为1.71天；在五台领导级超算上实现92-97%的扩展效率，峰值性能达1.27 EFLOP/s（占Lineshine峰值FP64性能的48%）。

Conclusion: AtomWorld实现了原子模拟的范式转变，成功跨越了原子尺度与工程尺度之间的时空鸿沟，为RPV钢寿命预测提供了直接原子建模的新途径。

Abstract: Lifetime prediction of reactor pressure vessel (RPV) steel requires bridging atomistic degradation mechanisms with service-scale spatial and temporal regimes, from Angstroms and picoseconds to meters and decades. Existing engineering-scale models provide long-range reach but rely on fitted degradation laws, while recent atomistic kinetic Monte Carlo (AKMC) advances still fail to achieve year-and-meter-scale coverage. We present AtomWorld, an atomistic world-modeling framework for RPV steel lifetime simulation co-designed with leadership-scale supercomputing through three tightly coupled layers: (1) algorithm: AtomWorld recasts classical AKMC as an atomistic world model that learns consequence-aware state transitions over the ab initio energy landscape; (2) HPC: it co-designs this formulation with modern supercomputers, yielding a compute-dense, synchronization-light, and communication-efficient execution pipeline; and (3) application: it extends atomistic world modeling to engineering-scale simulation through a physically grounded voxel-parallel framework, offering a scalable pathway from local atomistic dynamics to engineering-scale degradation evolution. We demonstrate a paradigm shift in atomistic simulation: AtomWorld enables atomistic simulation of RPV steel across year-and-meter scales for the first time, extending direct atomistic modeling to ten-quintillion-atom systems and achieving a time-to-solution of 1.71 days for one simulated service year. These capabilities are sustained across five leadership supercomputers with 92-97% scaling efficiency and peak performance up to 1.27 EFLOP/s, corresponding to 48% of the Lineshine peak FP64 performance.

</details>


### [11] [Exact, Efficient, and Reliable Multi-Objective and Multi-Constrained IoT Workflow Scheduling in Edge-Hub-Cloud Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24340)
*Andreas Kouloumpris,Georgios L. Stavrinides,Maria K. Michael,Theocharis Theocharides*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对边缘-中心-云端物联网应用，提出一种基于混合整数线性规划的精确工作流调度方法，联合优化时延、能耗和可靠性，实验显示相较启发式方法性能提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有调度方法（精确或启发式）均无法联合满足低时延、高能效、高可靠性等多目标以及严苛的时序与资源约束，无法为关键性物联网应用提供最优调度方案。

Method: 提出一种基于连续时间混合整数线性规划（MILP）的多目标多约束工作流调度方法。该方法联合优化时延、能耗与可靠性，统一处理时序和资源约束，并通过选择性任务复制在避免不必要开销的同时增强可靠性。

Result: 在真实物联网工作流和不同规模的合成任务图上，与经过公平扩展的广泛使用的启发式方法相比，所提方法在时延、能耗、可靠性上分别实现了最高29.83%、33.96%、28.49%的平均改进，运行时间具有实用性，并展示出针对目标应用规模的任务图的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 所提精确调度方法在各种系统配置和目标权衡下均表现出优越性和实用性，能够有效解决边缘-中心-云环境下的多目标多约束工作流调度问题，适用于相关应用场景。

Abstract: Emerging IoT-enabled cyber-physical applications demand low-latency, energy-efficient, and reliable execution across resource-constrained edge devices with heterogeneous multicore processors and diverse sensing and actuating capabilities, in collaboration with a hub device and a cloud server. These workflow-based applications comprise interdependent tasks that must be executed under stringent deadline, reliability, capability, memory, storage, and energy constraints. Given their critical nature, exact optimization is necessary to obtain optimal schedules that ensure dependable operation. Existing scheduling approaches, both exact and heuristic, fail to jointly address all these objectives and constraints. To this end, we propose an exact multi-objective and multi-constrained workflow scheduling approach for edge-hub-cloud cyber-physical systems, based on continuous-time mixed integer linear programming. The proposed formulation jointly optimizes latency, energy, and reliability, while holistically addressing timing and resource constraints. To enhance reliability while avoiding the overhead of unnecessary task replicas, it selectively employs task duplication. We evaluate our approach against a widely used heuristic, which we extend to ensure a fair and meaningful comparison, using a real-world IoT workflow and synthetic task graphs of varying sizes, across different system configurations and objective trade-offs. The proposed method consistently outperforms the heuristic, achieving up to 29.83%, 33.96%, and 28.49% average improvements in latency, energy, and reliability, respectively, while attaining practical runtimes. Overall, the experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach under various system configurations and objective trade-offs, and show its practical scalability to task graphs of sizes relevant to the targeted applications and system architecture.

</details>


### [12] [Incisor: Ex Ante Cloud Instance Selection for HPC Jobs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24464)
*Michael A. Laurenzano,Shihan Cheng,David A. B. Hyde*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Incisor是一个云HPC作业提交系统，利用程序分析工具和大语言模型（LLM）推理，在仅拥有可执行文件、输入和调用命令的情况下自动推断硬件需求并选择云实例。实验显示，它能100%为C/C++/Fortran/Python应用选择正确的AWS实例，相比专家基线方案，运行时间减少54%，成本降低44%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究针对云高性能计算（HPC）中的“事前实例选择问题”：用户在提交作业时通常只有可执行文件、输入和调用命令，缺乏对工作负载性能特征的先验知识。现有方法依赖人工经验，需要用户结合对云服务的有限认知、工作负载直觉、静态分析和系统推理来推断硬件约束，这一过程不仅耗时且高度依赖专业知识。

Method: Incisor系统将广泛可用的程序分析工具与前沿代码大语言模型（LLM）相结合。仅利用提交时的工件（可执行文件、输入、命令），系统通过LLM引导的推理过程自动推断硬件需求，并据此选择合适的云实例类型，实现了端到端的自动化实例选择。

Result: 在AWS EC2平台上，Incisor对源码编译的C、C++、Fortran以及Python应用程序首次运行实例选择的成功率达到100%。与结合专家约束和SkyPilot实例选择的强基线相比，Incisor将作业运行时间缩短了54%，实例成本降低了44%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过结合程序分析工具与LLM推理能力，可以有效自动化云HPC的实例选择过程，摆脱对专家知识和手动分析的依赖，显著提升作业执行效率并降低云资源成本。

Abstract: We present Incisor, a cloud HPC job submission system for the ex ante instance selection problem: choosing suitable hardware in the challenging but common setting where only the executable, inputs, and invocation commands are available at submission time. In practice, this task is manual and expertise-intensive, requiring users to combine incomplete knowledge of rapidly evolving cloud offerings with workload-specific intuition, static analysis, and systems reasoning to infer hardware constraints and select an instance type for each job. Incisor automates this process by pairing widely available program analysis tools with LLM-guided reasoning to infer hardware requirements and choose cloud instances. Using submission artifacts alone, Incisor atop frontier coding LLMs selects working AWS EC2 instances ex ante for 100% of first-time runs of source-compiled (C, C++, Fortran) and Python applications. Against a strong baseline combining expert-derived constraints with SkyPilot's instance selection, Incisor cuts job runtime by 54% and instance costs by 44%.

</details>


### [13] [SpotVista: Availability-Aware Recommendation System for Reliable and Cost-Efficient Multi-Node Spot Instances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24548)
*Taeyoon Kim,Kyumin Kim,Kyunghwan Kim,Hayoung Kim,Seungwoo Jeong,Moohyun Song,Kyungyong Lee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了SpotVista系统，利用公开数据集推荐可靠且高性价比的多节点竞价型实例资源池，在多区域场景下相比现有工作SpotVerse实现了81.28%的可用性提升和2.84%的成本节约，相比AWS SpotFleet则实现了21.6%的稳定性提升和26.3%的成本节约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云厂商提供的竞价型实例存在突然中断风险，传统的定价数据集预测效果因政策变化而减弱。现有研究主要关注单节点实例，忽略了现代云工作负载广泛采用的多节点环境的稳定性需求。云厂商提供的实时可用性数据集虽有潜力，但缺乏针对多节点场景的有效利用方法。

Method: 提出SpotVista系统，通过克服查询限制收集大规模多节点可用性数据集，深入分析多节点竞价型实例的可用性行为，建立一套推荐高性价比且可靠的多节点配置的方法论。系统利用多种公开可用数据集来推荐可靠且高性价比的多节点竞价型实例资源池。

Result: 在多区域设置下，SpotVista相比最先进的SpotVerse实现了81.28%的更高可用性和2.84%的更多成本节约；相比AWS公开服务SpotFleet，SpotVista提供了21.6%的更高稳定性和26.3%的更大成本节约。通过大量真实世界中断实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: SpotVista成功解决了多节点竞价型实例推荐问题，证明了利用公开可用数据集构建多节点配置推荐系统的可行性，为用户在保证稳定性的前提下有效利用竞价型实例提供了切实可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Cloud vendors offer discounted spot instances to maximize surplus resource utilization, but these instances are subject to the risk of sudden interruption. Traditional pricing datasets have been employed to predict this risk, yet recent policy changes by cloud vendors have diminished their effectiveness. To promote spot instance usage, public cloud vendors provide instant availability datasets to help users mitigate interruption risks. While existing research utilizing this data has proposed methods to reduce interruptions, these studies have primarily focused on single-node instances, overlooking the stability of multi-node environments widely adopted for modern cloud workloads.
  This paper proposes SpotVista, a system that recommends a resource pool of reliable and cost-efficient multi-node spot instances by leveraging various publicly available datasets. To achieve this, SpotVista collects a large-scale multi-node availability dataset while overcoming significant query limitations. Through a thorough analysis of multi-node spot instance availability behavior, SpotVista establishes a methodology for recommending cost-efficient and reliable multi-node configurations.
  To evaluate how effectively the proposed methodology reflects multi-node availability and cost efficiency, extensive real-world interruption experiments were conducted. The results demonstrate that SpotVista outperforms the state-of-the-art work, SpotVerse, achieving 81.28% greater availability and 2.84\% more cost savings in a multi-region setup. When compared to a publicly available service, AWS SpotFleet, SpotVista provides 21.6\% higher stability and 26.3% greater cost savings.

</details>


### [14] [Cloudless-Training: A Framework to Improve Efficiency of Geo-Distributed ML Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2303.05330)
*Wenting Tan,Xiao Shi1,Cunchi Lv,Xiaofang Zhao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Cloudless-Training框架，通过无服务器架构、弹性调度策略和新型同步策略，解决了地理分布式机器学习训练中的资源调度效率低和广域网通信开销大两大挑战，实现了训练成本降低9.2%-24.0%和最高1.7倍的训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理分布式机器学习训练（如大模型训练、联邦学习）能够利用多区域云资源和广域网，但面临两大效率瓶颈：一是缺乏针对多区域云资源的高效弹性调度机制，影响资源利用率和训练性能；二是广域网（WAN）上的通信仍是主要开销，受限于低带宽和高波动性。

Method: 提出Cloudless-Training框架，包含三个核心设计：（1）采用控制平面与物理训练平面分离的双层架构，以无服务器方式支持多区域云的弹性调度与通信；（2）设计弹性调度策略，根据云资源异构性和数据集分布自适应部署训练工作流；（3）提出两种新的同步策略：带梯度累积的异步SGD（ASGD-GA）和跨参数服务器模型平均（MA）。基于OpenFaaS实现并在腾讯云上进行评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，Cloudless-Training能够支持通用的地理分布式机器学习训练，显著提升资源利用率（训练成本降低9.2%-24.0%）和同步效率（相比基线最高实现1.7倍训练加速），同时保证模型正确性。

Conclusion: Cloudless-Training框架有效解决了地理分布式机器学习训练的关键效率问题，证明了无服务器架构与优化同步策略在该场景下的优越性，为多区域云环境下的分布式训练提供了可行的高效解决方案。

Abstract: Geo-distributed ML training can benefit many emerging ML scenarios (e.g., large model training, federated learning) with multi-regional cloud resources and wide area network. However, its efficiency is limited due to 2 challenges. First, efficient elastic scheduling of multi-regional cloud resources is usually missing, affecting resource utilization and performance of training. Second, training communication on WAN is still the main overhead, easily subjected to low bandwidth and high fluctuations of WAN. In this paper, we propose a framework, Cloudless-Training, to realize efficient PS-based geo-distributed ML training in 3 aspects. First, it uses a two-layer architecture with control and physical training planes to support elastic scheduling and communication for multi-regional clouds in a serverless maner.Second, it provides an elastic scheduling strategy that can deploy training workflows adaptively according to the heterogeneity of available cloud resources and distribution of pre-existing training datasets. Third, it provides 2 new synchronization strategies for training partitions among clouds, including asynchronous SGD with gradient accumulation (ASGD-GA) and inter-PS model averaging (MA). It is implemented with OpenFaaS and evaluated on Tencent Cloud. Experiments show that Cloudless-Training can support general ML training in a geo-distributed way, greatly improve resource utilization (e.g., 9.2%-24.0% training cost reduction) and synchronization efficiency (e.g., 1.7x training speedup over baseline at most) with model correctness guarantees.

</details>

### [15] [Hardware-Efficient FPGA Implementation of Sigmoid Function Using Mixed-Radix Hyperbolic Rotation CORDIC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23547)
*Chintan Panchal,Ankur Changela,Mohendra Roy*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于混合基数CORDIC的高效FPGA sigmoid激活函数硬件实现，通过优化算法和架构，在保证高精度的同时显著降低了硬件资源消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的边缘设备（如FPGA）上部署人工神经网络时，非线性激活函数的高效硬件实现至关重要。Sigmoid函数广泛用于概率输出、二分类和循环神经网络的门控机制，但其依赖指数运算，硬件实现开销较大，因此需要一种精度高且资源消耗低的硬件实现方案。

Method: 提出了一种基于混合基数CORDIC的FPGA sigmoid函数硬件架构。首先利用sigmoid与双曲正切函数的数学关系，将输入范围归一化至1，使tanh计算在0.5的缩减范围内进行，改善收敛性。引入改进的混合基数双曲旋转CORDIC（MR-HRC）算法，结合基-2和基-4迭代：初始基-2阶段确保稳定收敛，后续基-4阶段加速收敛且无需比例因子补偿。最后阶段采用基-2线性向量CORDIC（R2-LVC）计算双曲正切。整个架构采用全流水线设计，在Xilinx Virtex-7 FPGA上使用16位定点数实现。

Result: 实验结果表明，该设计仅需835个逻辑切片，零DSP使用，硬件资源消耗显著降低。平均绝对误差为4.23×10⁻⁴，精度优于多种近期的sigmoid实现方案。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于混合基数CORDIC的sigmoid函数FPGA实现在硬件效率和计算精度之间取得了良好平衡，非常适合资源受限的边缘设备和神经网络加速应用。

Abstract: Efficient hardware implementation of nonlinear activation functions is a crucial task in deploying artificial neural networks on resource-constrained and edge devices such as Field-Programmable Gate Arrays (FPGAs). The sigmoid activation function is widely used for probabilistic output, binary classification, and gating mechanisms in recurrent neural networks, despite its reliance on exponential computations.
  This paper presents a hardware-efficient FPGA implementation of the sigmoid activation function using a mixed-radix CORDIC-based architecture. The proposed approach leverages the mathematical relationship between the sigmoid and hyperbolic tangent functions. The input range is normalized to 1, enabling the corresponding tanh computation to operate within a reduced range of 0.5, which significantly improves convergence behavior.
  To achieve high accuracy with minimal hardware overhead, a modified mixed-radix hyperbolic rotation CORDIC (MR-HRC) algorithm combining radix-2 and radix-4 iterations is introduced. The initial radix-2 stage ensures stable convergence, while the subsequent radix-4 stage accelerates convergence without requiring scale-factor compensation. In the final stage, a radix-2 linear vectoring CORDIC (R2-LVC) is used to compute the hyperbolic tangent by dividing hyperbolic sine and cosine values derived from the MR-HRC algorithm.
  The entire architecture is fully pipelined and implemented on an FPGA. The design is realized on an Xilinx Virtex-7 FPGA using a 16-bit fixed-point representation. Experimental results demonstrate a significant reduction in hardware utilization, requiring only 835 logic slices with zero DSP usage. Additionally, the design achieves a mean absolute error of 4.23 10^-4, outperforming several recent sigmoid implementations.

</details>


### [16] [TimingLLM: A Two-Stage Retrieval-Augmented Framework for Pre-Synthesis Timing Prediction from Verilog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23602)
*Armin Abdollahi,Negin Ashrafi,Mehdi Kamal,Massoud Pedram*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了TimingLLM，一个两阶段检索增强的大语言模型流程，能够在无需综合工具的情况下直接从Verilog代码预测时序性能（WNS/TNS），实现了高精度、快速且易于跨工艺库迁移的早期时序预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在RTL迭代过程中，对后综合时序进行早期、无工具的预测是加速设计周期的关键障碍。现有的时序预测方法通常依赖耗时的逻辑综合流程，这阻碍了快速迭代，因此需要一种直接从代码层面快速准确预测时序的方法。

Method: 提出TimingLLM，这是一个两阶段的检索增强LLM流程。第一阶段：使用微调后的LLM作为紧凑的后综合时序预测器，生成路径级的到达/要求时间，并将其汇总为轻量级的结构-时序线索（如门电路计数、关键路径深度、门类型模式）。第二阶段：使用基于LLM的回归器预测WNS/TNS，在最后一个Transformer块应用通过对角转向向量进行引导，该向量从不相交检索库中k个最近的时序标记模块计算得出。

Result: 在VerilogEval基准测试上，TimingLLM达到了WNS相关系数R_WNS = 0.91（MAPE 12%）和TNS相关系数R_TNS = 0.97（MAPE 16%），运行速度比先前方法快1.3-1.6倍。研究构建了一个包含6万个带有综合报告的Verilog模块的新语料库。仅需针对新工艺库或PVT角点用1000个标记模块重新拟合小型回归头即可完成适配，性能持续优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: TimingLLM成功实现了从Verilog代码直接预测后综合时序，具备高精度、高速度和良好的可迁移性，能够显著加速RTL迭代流程，为集成电路设计提供了一个实用的早期时序评估工具。

Abstract: Early, tool-free prediction of post-synthesis timing remains a key obstacle to rapid RTL iteration. We introduce TimingLLM, a two-stage retrieval-augmented LLM pipeline that estimates worst negative slack (WNS) and total negative slack (TNS) directly from Verilog. Stage 1 is a fine-tuned LLM that acts as a compact post-synthesis timing oracle, producing path-level arrivals/required times that are summarized into lightweight structural-timing cues (e.g., bag-of-gates counts, critical-path depth, gate-type patterns). Stage 2 is an LLM-based regressor that predicts WNS/TNS and applies a learned diagonal steering vector at the last transformer block, computed from the k nearest timing-labeled modules in a disjoint retrieval bank. On VerilogEval, TimingLLM attains R_WNS = 0.91 (MAPE 12%) and R_TNS=0.97 (MAPE 16%) while running 1.3-1.6 times faster than prior methods. Training uses a new 60k-module Verilog corpus with synthesis reports, which we will release. After training once, TimingLLM can be adapted to new technology libraries and PVT corners by refitting only a small regression head on 1000 labeled modules per setting, consistently outperforming state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [17] [Hardware-Efficient Softmax and Layer Normalization with Guaranteed Normalization for Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23647)
*Dawon Choi,Hana Kim,Ji-Hoon Kim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文针对Transformer中非GEMM算子（Softmax和LayerNorm）在边缘设备上的硬件开销问题，提出了硬件高效的近似方法，同时保证了归一化特性。在28nm工艺下实现面积分别减少11倍和14倍，精度损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Transformer模型中，Softmax和LayerNorm等非线性非GEMM操作往往主导硬件成本。现有近似研究主要面向排序类任务（如分类），但边缘NLP和生成式AI应用多基于分数导向任务评估，因此必须保证归一化特性的非GEMM操作至关重要。

Method: 提出硬件高效的近似Softmax和LayerNorm设计，采用硬件友好的近似方法，同时严格保持归一化约束（Softmax：概率和为1，LayerNorm：标准差为1）。架构使用Verilog HDL描述，并基于Samsung 28nm CMOS工艺综合。

Result: 精度方面：GLUE +0.07%，SQuAD -0.01%，困惑度 -0.09%，几乎无精度损失。硬件方面：Softmax面积942μm²，LayerNorm面积1199μm²，相比最先进工作分别实现11倍和14倍的面积缩减。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了边缘设备上非GEMM操作的高效硬件近似，在保证归一化特性的前提下，显著降低了面积开销，同时保持了极高的模型精度，适用于边缘NLP和生成式AI应用。

Abstract: In Transformer models, non-GEMM (non-General Matrix Multiplication) operations -- especially Softmax and Layer Normalization (LayerNorm) -- often dominate hardware cost due to their nonlinear nature. To address this, previous approximation studies mainly target rank-oriented tasks, which is acceptable for classification. However, edge Natural Language Processing (NLP) applications and edge generative AI are largely evaluated based on score-oriented tasks, so normalization-guaranteed non-GEMM operations are essential. We propose a hardware-efficient Softmax and LayerNorm with Guaranteed Normalization for Edge devices. Our design employs hardware-efficient approximation methods while preserving the normalization (Softmax: $\sum p = 1$, LayerNorm: $σ= 1$). Our architecture is described in Verilog HDL and synthesized using the Samsung 28nm CMOS process. In accuracy evaluation, we achieve high accuracy with minimal degradation: GLUE +0.07%, SQuAD -0.01%, perplexity -0.09%. Implementation results show that our architecture is small: $942\,μm^2$ for Softmax, $1199\,μm^2$ for LayerNorm. Compared to the state of the art, we achieve up to 11x and 14x reduction in area, respectively.

</details>


### [18] [FlowPlace: Flow Matching for Chip Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23658)
*Peng Xie,Ke Xue,Yunqi Shi,Ruo-Tong Chen,Chengrui Gao,Siyuan Xu,Chenjian Ding,Mingxuan Yuan,Chao Qian*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出FlowPlace方法，通过掩码引导数据生成、基于流的高效训练和硬约束采样，解决了现有芯片布局生成方法在数据质量、采样速度和重叠约束方面的不足，实现更快采样和零重叠布局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的芯片布局方法存在三大局限：使用随机合成数据预训练导致质量受限、采样时间长、依赖梯度求解器导致布局重叠问题。

Method: 提出FlowPlace框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 掩码引导的合成数据生成策略，(2) 基于流模型的高效训练方法并支持灵活先验注入，(3) 硬约束采样机制确保无重叠布局。

Result: 在OpenROAD和ICCAD 2015基准测试上，FlowPlace实现了更优的PPA指标、采样速度提升10-50倍，且生成布局零重叠。

Conclusion: FlowPlace有效克服了现有生成式芯片布局方法的关键缺陷，在性能、效率和约束满足方面取得显著改进，为物理设计中的芯片布局提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Chip placement plays an important role in physical design. While generative models like diffusion models offer promising learning-based solutions, current methods have the following limitations: they use random synthetic data for pre-training, require long sampling times, and often result in overlaps due to their dependence on gradient-based solvers during the sampling process. To overcome these issues, we propose FlowPlace, which features mask-guided synthetic data generation, flow-based efficient training with flexible prior injection, and hard constraint sampling for overlap-free layouts. Experiments on OpenROAD and ICCAD 2015 benchmarks show FlowPlace achieves better PPA metrics, 10-50$\times$ faster sampling efficiency, and zero overlaps.

</details>


### [19] [Architectural Isolation as a Timing Safety Primitive for Edge AI Medical Devices: Controlled Experimental Evidence on a Shared-Silicon Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23831)
*Akul Mallayya Swami*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该研究揭示AI医疗系统验证中存在盲区：即使满足准确性验证且输出稳定（STER=0），系统仍可能因部署负载违反时序约束。通过同硬件对比实验发现，CPU执行路径在联合负载下延迟激增9.8倍，违反10Hz临床周期预算达65%，而GPU路径保持稳定。建议采用STER与延迟联合验证方法以满足FDA稳健性要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前市场前验证协议通常未在推理层实际操作化准确性、输出稳定性和时序约束这三个结构上独立的属性。系统可能满足准确性和稳定性验证，但在部署负载下仍违反时序约束，这种潜在的验证盲区可能对临床安全构成威胁。需要实证证明这些属性的独立性，并提出相应的验证方法。

Method: 在NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano Super上设计对照实验，部署相同的MobileNetV2模型，在相同的对抗负载下对比两条执行路径：专用GPU加速器（TensorRT FP16，半精度浮点）和通用CPU（ONNX Runtime FP32，单精度浮点）。测量并比较两条路径的安全性阈值超标率（STER）、延迟表现以及对10Hz临床周期预算的满足情况。

Result: 两条执行路径均保持STER=0（输出稳定性）。GPU路径（TensorRT FP16）平均延迟低于11ms，满足时序要求。CPU路径（ONNX Runtime FP32）在联合负载下性能退化7.2倍，平均延迟比GPU路径高9.8倍，违反10Hz临床周期预算达65%。这证实了准确性、稳定性和时序约束的结构独立性。

Conclusion: 准确性验证、输出稳定性和时序约束是结构上独立的属性，仅验证前两项无法保证系统的时序安全性。研究提出STER与延迟联合验证作为候选方法，用于在推理层实际操作化美国FDA指南草案FDA-2024-D-4488的稳健性要求，该方法需经监管审查和临床验证后实施。

Abstract: A system can satisfy accuracy-based validation, maintain output stability (Safety-Threshold Exceedance Rate, STER, equal to zero), and still violate timing constraints under deployment load. These are structurally independent properties that current pre-market validation protocols often do not operationalize at the inference layer. This letter demonstrates their independence through a controlled same-hardware experiment: identical MobileNetV2 models are evaluated under identical adversarial load on two execution paths of the same NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano Super, a dedicated GPU accelerator (TensorRT FP16, half-precision floating point) and a general-purpose CPU (ONNX Runtime FP32, single-precision floating point). Both paths maintain STER = 0; the CPU path (ONNX Runtime FP32) degrades 7.2x under combined load (mean latency 9.8x higher than the GPU path (TensorRT FP16), which maintains latency below 11 ms), breaching the 10 Hz clinical cycle budget by 65%. Joint STER and latency verification is proposed as a candidate method for operationalizing U.S. FDA Draft Guidance FDA-2024-D-4488 robustness requirements at the inference layer, subject to regulatory review and clinical validation.

</details>


### [20] [Compilation and Execution of an Embeddable YOLO-NAS on the VTA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24455)
*Anthony Faure-Gignoux,Kevin Delmas,Adrien Gauffriau,Claire Pagetti*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文扩展并全自动化了VTA编译链，解决了片上内存限制问题，成功在FPGA上编译并模拟执行了YOLO-NAS模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前开发的面向航空电子安全关键领域的VTA独立编译器存在局限性，无法编译完整的CNN且不支持参数量超过片上内存容量的大型网络，因此需要扩展编译链以支持复杂CNN的完整部署。

Method: 扩展并全自动化VTA编译链，实现完整的CNN编译支持，并解决参数无法完全装入片上内存的大型CNN的编译问题。

Result: 成功编译并在模拟环境中执行了YOLO-NAS目标检测模型，验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 扩展后的VTA编译链能够支持大型复杂CNN在FPGA加速器上的完整编译与部署，满足了航空电子等安全关键领域的应用需求。

Abstract: Deploying complex Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) on FPGA-based accelerators is a promising way forward for safety-critical domains such as aeronautics. In a previous work, we have explored the Versatile Tensor Accelerator (VTA) and showed its suitability for avionic applications. For that, we developed an initial stand-alone compiler designed with certification in mind. However, this compiler still suffers from some limitations that are overcome in this paper. The contributions consist in extending and fully automating the VTA compilation chain to allow complete CNN compilation and support larger CNNs (which parameters do not fit in the on-chip memory). The effectiveness is demonstrated by the successful compilation and simulated execution of a YOLO-NAS object detection model.

</details>


### [21] [Déjà Vu Packing: Optimizing FPGA Logic Clustering Runtime via Pattern Memoization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24649)
*Milo Liebster,Amin Mohaghegh,Andrew Boutros*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出Déjà Vu打包方法，利用打包签名树识别重复打包模式并缓存合法性检查结果，将FPGA打包过程加速最高29.3倍（平均6.9倍），端到端VPR流程运行时间平均减少5.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着FPGA逻辑块架构日益复杂（包含复杂的逻辑单元和不规则的本地互连），打包阶段的合法性检查需要在每个候选簇上求解多源多宿路由问题，导致打包阶段占据了VPR流程运行时间的58%（7系列）到94%（Stratix 10）不等，成为性能瓶颈。通过分析发现，大量打包簇是少数打包模式的重复，因此许多合法性检查是冗余的。

Method: 提出Déjà Vu打包方法，引入一种新颖的打包签名树（packing signature tree）数据结构。该数据结构能够高效识别重复出现的打包模式，并对这些模式的合法性检查结果进行记忆化（memoization），从而跳过冗余的合法性检查。当遇到已检查过的打包模式时，直接复用之前的检查结果。

Result: 在AMD 7系列架构上，打包加速最高13.4倍，平均3.7倍；在Altera Stratix 10架构上，打包加速最高29.3倍，平均6.9倍。端到端VPR流程运行时间在两种架构上分别平均减少1.6倍和5.3倍，同时保持结果质量不变。

Conclusion: Déjà Vu方法通过识别并缓存重复打包模式的合法性检查结果，显著减少了FPGA CAD流程中打包阶段的冗余计算，在保持结果质量的同时大幅降低了运行时间，证明了利用模式重用优化复杂FPGA架构打包流程的有效性。

Abstract: Implementing a digital circuit on an FPGA fabric requires clustering technology-mapped netlist primitives into coarser-granularity blocks that can be directly mapped to the physical resources available on the FPGA. As the architecture of FPGA logic blocks (LBs) has grown in complexity, with sophisticated logic elements (LEs) and highly irregular local interconnect, this packing problem has become more challenging. To ensure the feasibility of intracluster routing, the computer-aided design (CAD) tools must solve a costly multi-source multi-sink routing problem for each candidate cluster. In this paper, we first show that such packing legality checks consume a significant portion of the CAD flow runtime for LB architectures with complex LEs and local routing structures resembling modern commercial FPGAs. We demonstrate that the packing stage constitutes 58% and 94% of the entire Versatile Place and Route (VPR) flow runtime on average when mapping a wide variety of benchmarks to the AMD 7-series-like and Altera Stratix-10-like VTR architecture captures, respectively. By analyzing the packing algorithm behavior, we observe that a significant fraction of the attempted packed clusters are repetitions of a much smaller number of packing patterns, and therefore many of the packing legality checks are redundant and could be skipped. To this end, we introduce our Déjà Vu packing approach, which leverages a novel packing signature tree data structure that enables efficient identification of recurring packing patterns and memoization of their legality check outcomes. Our approach speeds up the packing by up to 13.4x and 29.3x, with an average of 3.7x and 6.9x, across the evaluated benchmarks on the 7-series and Stratix 10 architectures. These packing runtime gains result in a significant 1.6x and 5.3x average reduction in end-to-end VPR runtime, while maintaining quality of results.

</details>

### [22] [RANalyzer: Automated Continuous RAN Software Evaluation and Regression Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23153)
*Ravis Shirkhani,Reshma Prasad,Leonardo Bonati,Tommaso Melodia,Michele Polese*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了RANalyzer框架，通过结合LLM辅助的语义提取和残差分析，自动量化O-RAN软件更新对性能的影响，并基于两年多的实验数据成功识别出归因于代码变更的性能退化实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在软件驱动的O-RAN架构中，由于无线系统的随机性（如信道条件、干扰和硬件可变性），难以将性能变化归因于特定的软件更新。传统的基于阈值的监控和人工故障排除无法适应现代软件演进的速度和规模。

Method: 提出了RANalyzer自动化测试分析框架。该框架结合了两个核心组件：1）LLM辅助的语义提取，用于按受影响的协议层和功能组件对代码变更进行分类；2）残差分析，通过建模预期性能，将偏差解释为软件诱发的影响，从而量化软件更新在无线信道条件解释范围之外的性能影响。

Result: 基于一个包含超过8,600个自动化测试、跨越OAI栈69个版本的大型数据集（收集于两年多的连续OTA测试），该框架成功识别出可归因于代码变更的性能退化实例，并将其与特定的变更类别进行了关联。

Conclusion: RANalyzer能够有效分离无线环境随机性与软件更新对性能的影响，可集成至CI/CD/CT流水线中，实现大规模软件更新的自动化、持续评估，解决了现有方法无法扩展的问题。

Abstract: Software-driven O-RAN architectures enable rapid innovation through frequent, independent updates to virtualized components. However, attributing performance variations to specific software changes is challenging due to the stochastic nature of wireless systems, where channel conditions, interference, and hardware variability confound analysis. Traditional threshold-based monitoring and manual troubleshooting do not scale with modern software evolution.
  This paper presents RANalyzer, an automated test analysis framework that quantifies the performance impact of software updates beyond what can be explained by wireless channel conditions. RANalyzer combines LLM-assisted semantic extraction with residuals analysis. The first categorizes code changes by affected protocol layers and functional components, while the second provides insights on the effect of load, channel, or code changes on the test performance. We contribute an extensive dataset collected over more than two years of continuous over-the-air testing on an experimental O-RAN testbed, comprising over 8,600 automated tests across 69 releases of the OAI stack. By modeling expected performance and interpreting deviations as software-induced effects, we identify degraded instances attributable to code changes and correlate them with specific change categories. The framework can be integrated into CI/CD/CT pipelines for automated, continuous evaluation of software updates at scale.

</details>


### [23] [Towards Agentic Test-Driven Quality Assurance for 6G Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23285)
*Christos Tranoris,Besiana Agko,Kostis Trantzas,Irene Denazi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于代理和意图驱动的端到端编排框架，将意图共创与测试驱动质量保证范式相结合，利用TM Forum标准知识表示确保主动的SLA合规性，实验表明知识集成对代理系统的可靠性至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现自动化和智能化的端到端服务编排，确保服务等级协议（SLA）的主动合规性，同时解决用户意图从初始构想到可审计规范的精确转化问题，研究提出了将意图共创与测试驱动开发（TDD）范式相结合的框架。

Method: 提出了一种代理式、意图驱动的端到端编排框架，核心方法包括：1) 自主代理迭代细化用户意图为可审计规范；2) 在资源配置前自动从意图推导验证测试（借鉴TDD工作流）；3) 采用符合TM Forum标准的信息模型和目录作为知识表示基础，实现从高层产品服务到底层资源及测试规范的确定性图遍历；4) 通过扩展OpenSlice平台，采用消息驱动的多代理模式并集成MCP（Model Context Protocol）工具访问以实现实时知识检索。

Result: 构建了该架构的原型系统，并以意图共创阶段为评估基线进行了实验。通过集成多个开源大语言模型（LLM）后端与TMF知识库的测试，观察到代理在工具使用可靠性方面存在显著差异，且会产生不同的幻觉模式，这验证了在代理式6G系统中进行稳健知识集成的关键重要性。

Conclusion: 基于标准的知识表示集成对于构建可靠的代理式编排系统至关重要。实验揭示了当前LLM在工具使用和幻觉控制方面的局限性，强调了在6G等关键系统中必须通过稳健的知识集成来提升代理系统的确定性和可靠性。

Abstract: This work proposes an agentic, intent-driven end-to-end (E2E) orchestration framework that integrates intent co-creation with a Test-Driven Quality Assurance paradigm. In this framework, autonomous agents iteratively refine a user's initial intent into a confirmed, auditable specification. Furthermore, the system automatically derives validation tests from these intents before provisioning, directly mirroring the Test-Driven Development workflow in software engineering to ensure proactive Service Level Agreement (SLA) compliance. The architecture is grounded in a standards-aligned knowledge representation using TM Forum (TMF) information models and catalogs. This enables deterministic graph traversal from high-level Product Offerings down to granular Service/Resource and Test specifications. We prototyped this architecture by extending OpenSlice with a message-driven, multi-agent pattern and integrating MCP-enabled (Model Context Protocol) tool access for real-time knowledge retrieval. Currently, our evaluation of the agents targets the intent co-creation phase as a baseline toward full-scale orchestration. Building on experiments with multiple open-source Large Language Model (LLM) backends integrated with the TMF-based knowledge base, we observe substantial variability in tool-use reliability and hallucination patterns, underscoring the critical importance of robust knowledge integration in agentic 6G systems.

</details>


### [24] [An Agentic Framework for Intent Co-Creation in 6G NaaS: Architecture and Open-Source Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23288)
*Kostis Trantzas,Besiana Agko,Christos Tranoris,Irene Denazi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出一种面向6G NaaS的基于智能体的意图驱动端到端编排框架，通过领域专家智能体与知识库协作迭代细化用户请求，实现将模糊意图转化为确定性可执行动作，原型测试显示LLMs指令遵循能力强但在生成有效订单方面存在幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G网络复杂性要求高度自治，但当前基于意图的系统难以处理模糊或不完整的人类请求，缺乏将高层意图可靠转化为机器可执行动作的能力。

Method: 设计基于智能体的意图驱动E2E编排框架：1）利用领域专家智能体池和符合TM Forum标准的知识库(BoK)；2）采用认知与执行解耦的设计原则，将AI推理与标准化执行控制器隔离；3）引入双层记忆系统维持多步协作的一致性；4）基于ETSI OpenSlice和模型上下文协议(MCP)构建原型，并在多个开源大语言模型上进行评估。

Result: 大语言模型展现出较高的指令遵循能力，但在将高层意图转化为有效的、基于目录的订单时存在显著差距，无法完全避免幻觉问题。

Conclusion: 所提框架通过认知与执行解耦确保了安全性和可操作性信任，但当前LLM技术在意图翻译的准确性方面仍需改进，以支持实际NaaS交付场景。

Abstract: 6G network complexity necessitates high levels of autonomy, yet current intent-based systems struggle with ambiguous or incomplete human requests. This paper introduces an agent-based, intent-driven end-to-end (E2E) orchestration framework designed for Network-as-a-Service (NaaS) delivery through collaborative intent co-creation. The proposed system leverages a pool of Domain Expert Agents and a TM Forum-aligned Body-of-Knowledge (BoK) to iteratively refine user requests into deterministic, machine-readable actions. A fundamental design principle is the decoupling of cognition and actuation, where AI-driven reasoning is isolated from standardized execution controllers to ensure safety and operational trust. The framework includes a dual-layer memory system to maintain coherence during multi-step collaborations. The presented prototype, built on ETSI OpenSlice and the Model Context Protocol (MCP), evaluates across several open-source Large Language Models (LLMs). While these models demonstrate high instruction compliance, results reveal a significant gap in translating high-resolution intents into valid, catalog-backed orders without hallucinations.

</details>


### [25] [RadTwin: Generalizable Wireless Digital Twin for Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23310)
*Yuru Zhang,Ming Zhao,Qiang Liu,Ahmed Alkhateeb,Abhishek K. Agrawal,Qi Qu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了RadTwin，一种无需重新训练即可适应动态环境的无线数字孪生框架，通过场景几何条件化实现了比NeRF2高31.6%的SSIM和低91.96%的LPIPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的射线追踪方法依赖于精确的3D环境模型，而现有的神经辐射场方法与特定静态场景绑定，环境变化时需重新训练，无法有效应对动态无线环境的无线电传播建模需求。

Method: RadTwin包含三个核心组件：1）场景表示网络，从点云中提取高层潜在场景特征；2）电磁射线追踪模块，计算物理驱动的稀疏注意力掩码，识别对查询方向有物理信号贡献的体素；3）神经传播解码器，通过掩码交叉注意力聚合相关场景特征，学习给定场景几何下的无线电传播行为。

Result: 在室内场景定制数据集上的实验表明，RadTwin相比NeRF2实现了31.6%的SSIM提升（0.846 vs. 0.643）和91.96%的LPIPS降低（0.023 vs. 0.286），并展现出优越的跨尺度性能、高泛化能力和数据效率。

Conclusion: RadTwin通过显式条件化场景几何实现了对动态环境的适应，无需重新训练即可准确建模无线电传播，为动态无线环境下的实用数字网络孪生提供了重要技术进步。

Abstract: Precisely modeling radio propagation in dynamic wireless environments is fundamental to the realization of wireless digital twins. Traditional ray tracing methods rely on accurate 3D models with detailed environment parameters, while recent neural radiance field approaches learn representations tied to specific static scenes, requiring retraining when environments change. In this paper, we propose RadTwin, a generalizable wireless digital twin framework that explicitly conditions on scene geometry, enabling adaptation to dynamic environments without retraining. RadTwin comprises three key components: 1) a scenario representation network that extracts high-level latent scene features from point clouds, 2) an electromagnetic ray tracing module that computes physics-informed sparse attention masks identifying voxels that physically contribute signals toward each query direction, and 3) a neural propagation decoder that aggregates relevant scene features through masked cross-attention to learn how radio propagation behaves within the given scene geometry. We evaluate RadTwin on a customized dataset of indoor scenes with varying furniture arrangements. Experimental results show that RadTwin achieves 31.6% higher SSIM (0.846 vs. 0.643) and 91.96% lower LPIPS (0.023 vs. 0.286) compared to NeRF2. RadTwin further demonstrates superior cross-scale performance and high generalization and data efficiency, representing a significant advancement toward practical digital network twins for dynamic wireless environments.

</details>


### [26] [Sharing-oriented Resource Allocation for Multi-platoon's Groupcasting and Unicasting Communication based on the Transmission Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23373)
*Chung-Ming Huang,Yen-Hung Wu,Duy-Tuan Dao*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对5G车辆编队的组播和单播通信，提出了基于传输可靠性的共享式资源分配方法（TMPG和RSPU算法），通过三方匹配实现了更高的频谱效率和QoS满意度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆编队中的资源分配面临高移动性和频谱资源有限的挑战，而传统的资源共享方法在组播功率不足时难以保证通信可靠性，亟需一种能同时优化组播和单播通信的资源分配方案。

Method: 针对组播通信，提出TMPG算法，利用三方匹配为PLV/PRV与个体实体（IE）分配共享子信道；针对单播通信，提出RSPU算法，首先考虑簇内干扰将PMV划分成簇，然后采用三方匹配为PMV簇与IE分配共享子信道。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方法在服务质量满意度、已分配子信道数量和频谱效率方面均优于基准方案。

Conclusion: 基于三方匹配的共享式资源分配方法能有效提升车辆编队通信的频谱效率和传输可靠性，为5G环境下车辆编队的资源管理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Resource allocation in vehicular platoons is challenging due to high vehicle mobility and limited spectrum resources. To improve spectral efficiency, resource sharing is commonly adopted. In 5G-based platoons, the Platoon Leader Vehicle (PLV) employs groupcasting to disseminate control messages to Platoon Member Vehicles (PMVs). When the groupcasting power is insufficient, a selected PMV acts as a Platoon Relay Vehicle (PRV) to extend the communication range. In addition, PMVs transmit unicast control messages to the vehicles following them for emergency coordination. This work proposes a sharing-oriented resource allocation method for both groupcasting and unicasting communication based on transmission reliability. For groupcasting, the proposed Tripartite Matching for Platoon Groupcasting (TMPG) algorithm applies tripartite matching to allocate subchannels shared by a PLV/PRV and corresponding individual entities (IEs), which denote cellular users or non-platooning vehicles. For unicasting, the proposed Resource Sharing for Platoons Unicasting (RSPU) algorithm (i) firstly partitions PMVs into clusters by considering intra-cluster interference and then (ii) uses tripartite matching to allocate a subchannel that is shared by a cluster of PMVs and the corresponding IE. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed methods outperform benchmark schemes in terms of Quality of Service (QoS) satisfaction, the number of allocated subchannels, and spectral efficiency.

</details>


### [27] [ARCHES: Adaptive Real-Time Switching of AI Models for the RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23397)
*Neagin Neasamoni Santhi,Davide Villa,Michele Polese,Salvatore D'Oro,Yunseong Lee,Koichiro Furueda,Tommaso Melodia*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出ARCHES框架，在GPU加速的RAN物理层管道中实现AI与传统信号处理专家的实时动态切换，在提升上行吞吐量的同时显著降低GPU功耗与资源占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI模型虽在特定条件下优于传统算法，但难以跨所有无线环境泛化，且计算与能耗成本高，无条件执行不切实际，因此亟需根据实时网络状况动态激活最合适的AI或传统专家。

Method: 构建包含轻量级CUDA切换内核（零间隙输出选择）、基于dApp的控制平面（采集跨层遥测并驱动切换策略）以及基于受控扰动、单调性过滤和层次聚类的策略设计流程的框架。

Result: 在UL信道估计任务上，于良好与恶劣条件下分别实现5.32%与7.23%的中位上行PHY吞吐量增益，控制环路延迟约140μs，决策推理亚微秒级；良好条件下默认使用MMSE相较无条件AI执行节省15.8W（9.6%）GPU功耗与17个百分点的GPU利用率。

Conclusion: ARCHES成功实现了槽边界粒度的无缝专家热切换，验证了自适应专家选择在性能-功耗权衡上的有效性，为RAN智能化部署提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) has become a powerful tool for model-free Radio Access Network (RAN) signal processing and optimization. However, designing a single model that generalizes across all radio environments is challenging. Specialized AI models outperform conventional algorithms only under specific conditions, while their higher compute and energy cost makes unconditional execution impractical at the base station. This creates a need for real-time expert switching: dynamically activating the most appropriate AI or conventional expert based on current network conditions.
  To address this, we propose ARCHES (Adaptive Real-time CUDA Hot-swapping of Experts in the RAN Stack), a framework hosting multiple AI-based and conventional signal processing experts within a GPU-accelerated PHY pipeline, dynamically selecting the most appropriate expert at slot-boundary granularity without dropping or corrupting in-flight data. ARCHES includes a lightweight CUDA switch kernel for zero-gap output selection, a dApp-based control plane that collects cross-layer telemetry and drives the switching policy, and a reusable process for policy design based on controlled perturbation, monotonicity filtering, and hierarchical clustering.
  We validate ARCHES on UL channel estimation, switching between an AI-based and a Minimum Mean Square Error (MMSE) estimator under changing propagation and interference conditions. Implemented on the X5G platform with NVIDIA Aerial and OpenAirInterface (OAI), ARCHES achieves median UL PHY throughput gains of 5.32% and 7.23% under good and poor conditions, with a control-loop latency of ~140 us and sub-microsecond decision inference. Under good conditions, defaulting to MMSE saves 15.8 W of GPU power (9.6%) and 17 percentage points of GPU utilization versus unconditional AI execution, validating the performance-per-watt tradeoff that motivates adaptive expert selection.

</details>


### [28] [Multi-Plane HyperX: A Low-Latency and Cost-Effective Network for Large-Scale AI and HPC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23519)
*Ziyu Wang,Fei Lei,Dezun Dong*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文首次将多平面技术应用于HyperX直连网络，证明了多平面HyperX在直径和成本效益上显著优于现有主流拓扑结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多平面架构在AI数据中心Fat-Tree网络中已广泛应用，但目前尚无研究将其应用于HyperX等直连网络，存在研究空白。

Method: 提出并研究多平面HyperX网络架构，通过单NIC多端口或多NIC方式构建独立网络平面，与多平面Fat-Tree、Dragonfly和Dragonfly+等先进拓扑进行对比分析。

Result: 多平面HyperX网络直径显著更小，成本效益更优，相比现有主流网络拓扑具有明显性能和成本优势。

Conclusion: 多平面HyperX是AI数据中心网络架构的有效创新方案，可作为现有主流拓扑的优越替代选择。

Abstract: Multi-plane architectures have become increasingly prevalent in the Fat-Tree networks of AI data centers. By leveraging multiple ports on a single network interface card (NIC) or multiple NICs within a scale-up domain, each port or NIC is allocated to an independent network plane, thereby provisioning the overall system with multiple network planes. However, no prior literature has explored the application of multi-plane technologies to direct networks such as HyperX. This paper investigates the multi-plane HyperX network and demonstrates that, compared to state-of-the-art network topologies like multi-plane Fat-Tree, Dragonfly, and Dragonfly+, the multi-plane HyperX architecture achieves a significantly smaller network diameter and superior cost-effectiveness.

</details>


### [29] [Adaptive Swin Transformer Partitioning over AI-RAN Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23554)
*Tam Thanh Nguyen,Yong Hao Pua,Tuan Van Ngo,Mao V. Ngo,Jihong Park,Binbin Chen,Tony Q. S. Quek*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文首次实现了基于Swin Transformer的分割推理在动态5G AI-RAN网络上的实时视频目标检测，通过无需重训练的自适应分割和高效激活压缩技术，在真实测试床上验证了系统的可行性并量化了延迟-能耗-隐私的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着视觉模型从CNN向Transformer演进，迫切需要探索Transformer模型在移动边缘计算场景下的分割推理可行性。然而，Transformer固有的巨大中间激活值对上行链路带宽和系统延迟构成严峻挑战，且动态5G网络环境增加了分割点选择的复杂性，此前针对CNN的优化方法难以直接应用。

Method: 1. 将吞吐量感知的自适应分割策略从CNN扩展至Swin Transformer骨干网络，实现无需重训练的分割执行；
2. 提出保精度的激活压缩流水线，显著降低上行链路数据载荷；
3. 集成分布式用户面功能(dUPF)以减少用户面延迟并提升运行稳定性；
4. 在NVIDIA Aerial AI-RAN测试床上构建完整系统，涵盖自适应分割选择、Transformer推理和压缩模块。

Result: 在真实实时检测工作负载上完成了端到端验证。大规模实测表明：(1)基于Transformer的分割推理在动态5G网络中切实可行；(2)激活压缩有效缓解了中间激活值过大的问题；(3)系统能够联合优化推理能耗与5G通信能耗；(4)量化揭示了实际部署中延迟-能耗-隐私三者的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了Transformer模型能够在无需重训练的前提下，通过精心设计的分割策略和压缩技术，在动态5G AI-RAN网络中实现高效的实时视频目标检测，为未来移动AI应用提供了重要的技术参考和系统级解决方案。

Abstract: This paper demonstrates the feasibility of transformer-based split inference for real-time video object detection over dynamic 5G AI-RAN networks. We extend throughput-aware adaptive splitting from CNNs to a Swin Transformer backbone and show that practical split execution is achievable for transformer-based vision models without retraining. To address the large intermediate activations inherent to transformers, we introduce an efficient, accuracy-preserving activation compression pipeline that substantially reduces uplink payload. The complete system -- including adaptive split selection, transformer inference, and compression -- is implemented and validated end-to-end on a real-time detection workload, with distributed UPF (dUPF) integration further reducing user-plane latency and improving runtime stability. Extensive measurements on an NVIDIA Aerial-based AI-RAN testbed jointly account for inference and 5G communication energy, quantifying the latency-energy-privacy trade-offs in realistic deployments.

</details>


### [30] [NODE: Network Wide Top-K Flows in the Data Plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23778)
*Eitan Stein,Lior Zeno,Shir Landau Feibish*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了NODE，一种完全在数据平面运行的网络级Top-k大流检测算法，无需中心化控制器即可实现交换机间的信息聚合，在低于300KB内存开销下达到95%以上的召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的全局Top-k大流检测方案依赖中心化控制器收集和聚合各交换机的测量数据，控制平面的通信过程延迟较高，无法及时检测和缓解持续时间较短的网络事件。因此，需要一种完全在数据平面内运行的低延迟解决方案。

Method: 提出NODE算法，使交换机能够在数据平面内直接聚合网络中所有其他交换机的信息。通过分布式协作机制，确保最终所有交换机都持有相同的全局Top-k流表。

Result: 在合成和真实流量数据上的实验表明，NODE能够有效检测全局Top-k大流，实现了超过95%的召回率，同时每个交换机的内存开销低于300KB。

Conclusion: NODE成功实现了完全在数据平面的网络级Top-k大流检测，消除了对控制平面的依赖和由此带来的延迟，为快速检测和缓解瞬态网络事件提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Monitoring network traffic is crucial for most network tasks, such as, identifying and blocking attacks, pinpointing failures and engineering and rerouting heavy traffic to maintain high throughput. One important metric when monitoring the traffic is finding the top-k heavy flows, that is the k heaviest flows in the traffic. Programmable networks allow performing advanced network analysis right in the data plane. In recent years, various solutions have been proposed for efficiently finding the top-k heavy flows within a single switch. However, at times we may need to find the global top-k flows. Existing solutions for global top-k detection use a centralized controller that collects and aggregates the measurements performed in each of the switches. Yet, the process of sending information to the control plane and then having the controller send back the information to the switches can be very lengthy. In order to be able to detect and mitigate short-lived events, solutions that work completely within the data plane are needed. In this paper we present NODE, a network-wide top-k detection algorithm that operates exclusively in the data plane. NODE allows the switches to aggregate information from all other switches in the network, and ensures that eventually all switches hold an identical global top-k table. We show that NODE manages to detect global top-k flows on both synthetic and real traces, with a recall rate of over 95\% while using less than 300KB per switch.

</details>


### [31] [Optimizing Information Freshness for Wireless Local Area Networks with Multiple APs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23796)
*Ananth Ram Rajagopalan,Jiahui Ni,Vishrant Tripathi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了密集室内WLAN中多AP场景下的全网信息年龄（AoI）最小化问题，提出了具有常数因子最优性保证的随机策略和在线策略，并在仿真中实现了约50%的AoI降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集室内WLAN中多个接入点（AP）在部分重叠频谱上运行以支持延迟敏感应用，但同时传输会产生同频和邻频干扰，使调度决策相互依赖并直接影响信息的新鲜度。现有的软件定义WLAN架构支持集中式协调，这为研究多AP环境下的AoI优化提供了动机。

Method: 首先推导了任意调度策略下可达到的AoI下界；然后设计了相对于该下界具有常数因子最优性保证的平稳随机策略；基于这些见解，开发了一种基于Lyapunov漂移的在线策略，适用于具有动作相关帧长度的系统，并利用新的基于比率的漂移分析建立了常数因子保证；在子模性假设下，证明了每帧调度可通过多项式时间局部搜索近似实现。

Result: 提出的策略在理论上具有常数因子的最优性保证。使用真实WLAN布局的仿真表明，相比分布式单AP基线，所提方法能实现约50%的AoI降低。

Conclusion: 本文针对多AP WLAN中由于干扰导致的调度决策耦合问题，提出了一整套具有理论保证的AoI优化方案，包括随机策略、在线策略和可扩展的近似算法，有效提升了网络的信息新鲜度。

Abstract: Dense indoor WLANs increasingly rely on multiple access points (APs) operating over partially overlapping spectrum to support latency-sensitive applications. In such deployments, simultaneous transmissions across APs create co-channel and adjacent-channel interference, making scheduling decisions interdependent and directly impacting information freshness. Motivated by emerging software-defined WLAN architectures that enable centralized coordination, we study the problem of minimizing network-wide Age of Information (AoI) in multi-AP WLANs. Unlike classical AoI scheduling that runs at a single AP, each scheduling decision is now coupled across APs due to interference. This leads to a new class of combinatorial AoI control problems with action-dependent time evolution. We first derive a lower bound on the achievable AoI under arbitrary scheduling policies. We then design stationary randomized policies that have constant-factor optimality guarantees relative to this bound. Building on these insights, we develop a Lyapunov drift-based online policy for systems with action-dependent frame lengths, and establish constant-factor guarantees using new ratio-based drift analysis. To enable scalable implementation, we further show that per-frame scheduling admits efficient polynomial-time local-search approximations under a submodularity assumption. Simulations using realistic WLAN layouts demonstrate about 50% AoI reduction over distributed single AP baselines.

</details>


### [32] [MatchRDMA: A Segmented and Rate-Matched Long-Haul RDMA Scheme for Geo-distributed LLM Training over OTN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23932)
*Jun Dai,Xiaorun Wang,Xingde Li,Zheng Yang,Kexiong Fang,Zhiqun Gu,Hongxiang Wang,Yuefeng Ji,Jiawei Zhang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出MatchRDMA，一种用于OTN上地理分布式LLM训练的长距离RDMA方案，将跨数据中心吞吐量提升高达20倍，并将目的OTN缓冲区占用减少高达62.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RDMA在跨OTN的地理分布式LLM训练场景中，面临速率不匹配导致的性能瓶颈和缓冲区占用问题。

Method: 提出MatchRDMA方案，采用主动、分段和速率匹配策略，通过协调源端和目的端OTN速率来优化长距离RDMA传输。

Result: 实验结果表明，与传统RDMA相比，该方案将跨数据中心吞吐量提升高达20倍，并将目的OTN缓冲区占用降低高达62.7%。

Conclusion: MatchRDMA有效解决了地理分布式LLM训练中长距离RDMA的性能瓶颈问题，显著提升了传输效率并降低了缓冲区需求。

Abstract: We propose MatchRDMA, a proactive, segmented, and rate-matched long-haul RDMA scheme for geo-distributed LLM training over OTN. By coordinating source and destination OTN rates, it improves inter-DC throughput by up to 20x compared with conventional RDMA, and reduces destination-OTN buffer occupancy by up to 62.7%.

</details>


### [33] [Optimizing power by selective IP card shutdown using transport slicing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24181)
*Alfonso Sánchez-Macián,Óscar González de Dios,José Alberto Hernández,Liesbeth Roelens,Pablo Armingol Robles,Juan Pedro Fernadez-Palacios,Ramón Casellas,Filippo Cugini*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对6G网络的高能耗问题，提出了一种基于IP的双切片策略（日切片/夜切片），利用多目标进化算法优化，在低流量时段可关闭超过40%的线路卡，在保障服务质量的同时显著降低能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 即将到来的第六代（6G）移动网络及支持AI应用的网络面临着日益增长的能源需求，这给网络运营商带来了运营成本和环境影响方面的巨大挑战。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的基于IP的网络切片策略，采用双切片方法：日切片用于满足高峰流量时段的高性能需求，夜切片通过在低流量需求期间停用基于卡的路由器中多余的线路卡来优化节能。应用基于帕累托的进化算法（NSGA-II、CTAEA和AGE-MOEA）联合优化能耗和延迟。

Result: 在SNDlib india35拓扑上进行的实验表明，多目标优化可以在低流量时段停用超过40%的线路卡，提供显著的节能效果，同时保持可接受的性能。此外，使用AGE-MOEA的多服务扩展引入了差异化的QoS约束，将高级流量的延迟保持在7ms以下，同时保留了可观的节能效果。

Conclusion: 所提出的双切片网络策略能够有效平衡能耗与网络性能，通过在低流量时段智能休眠网络设备硬件，实现了显著的成本节约与环保效益，证明了多目标优化在网络能效管理中的有效性。

Abstract: The increasing energy demands of upcoming sixth-generation (6G) mobile networks and networks supporting AI applications pose significant challenges for network operators in terms of operational costs and environmental impact. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a novel IP-based network slicing strategy that optimizes energy efficiency through a dual-slice approach. The proposed solution consists of a Day Slice, designed to meet high-performance requirements during peak traffic hours, and a Night Slice, optimized for energy savings by deactivating excess line-cards in card-based routers during periods of low traffic demand. The traffic is switched between the Day and Night Slices at predefined times, assuming appropriate traffic engineering mechanisms are in place to minimize disruption and support session continuity. We apply Pareto-based evolutionary algorithms (NSGA-II, CTAEA, and AGE-MOEA) to jointly optimize energy consumption and latency. Experiments conducted on the SNDlib india35 topology demonstrate that multi-objective optimization can deactivate over 40% of line cards during low-traffic periods, providing significant energy savings while maintaining acceptable performance. Additionally, a multi-service extension using AGE-MOEA introduces differentiated QoS constraints, maintaining latency below 7 ms for premium traffic while preserving substantial energy savings.

</details>

*Carlos S. Alvarez-Merino,Alejandro Ramirez-Arroyo,Rasmus Suhr Mogensen,Morten V. Pedersen,Miguel Villanueva-Fernández,Emil J. Khatib,Sergio Fortes,Raquel Barco,Preben E. Mogensen*
