TL;DR: 本文通过实测评估了商用5G非独立组网中的多连接策略，提出PAAF框架，发现在覆盖受限的农村场景下，部分复制方案能显著降低开销并提升上行业务可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务关键型与移动自主系统对低时延可靠通信有严格要求，但商用5G网络在农村等覆盖受限场景下，上行链路性能常因无线资源受限和功率控制机制而成为瓶颈，现有覆盖指标无法准确推断实际性能。

Method: 在城市、郊区和农村环境中对两家移动网络运营商开展测量实验，分析上/下行单包时延、丢包率及无线层KPI；评估链路聚合、切换策略、条件包复制等多种多连接策略，并提出基于无线条件、时延和服务成本的PAAF框架以选择性激活冗余。

Result: 测量结果表明，仅依据RSRP等覆盖指标无法推断时延与可靠性；在覆盖受限场景，上行功率受限操作对性能影响显著，且不同运营商间的损伤呈部分相关性；部分复制方案可在农村场景下接近全多连接可靠性，同时显著降低复制开销。

Conclusion: 针对上行功率受限的农村5G场景，采用考虑无线、时延与成本的PAAF框架及部分复制策略，可高效提升关键业务的上行可靠性与时延表现，优于仅依赖覆盖指示的传统方法。

Abstract: Reliable low-latency communication is a key requirement for mission-critical and mobile autonomous systems, including teleoperation, autonomous navigation, and real-time uplink-dominant telemetry applications. While commercial 5G networks often provide adequate downlink performance, uplink performance in rural deployments may be constrained by radio-resource limitations and uplink power-control mechanisms. This paper presents a comprehensive experimental evaluation of multi-connectivity strategies over commercial 5G Non-Standalone networks, based on measurement campaigns conducted in urban, suburban, and rural environments. The study analyzes per-packet uplink and downlink latency, packet loss, and radio-layer KPIs across two mobile network operators. The measurements indicate that latency and reliability cannot be inferred solely from coverage indicators such as RSRP. In coverage-constrained scenarios, performance appears to be strongly influenced by uplink power-limited operation and partially correlated impairments across operators. Several multi-connectivity strategies are evaluated, including link aggregation, switching-based policies, and conditional packet duplication. A Primary-Anchored Adaptive Failover (PAAF) framework is introduced to selectively activate redundancy based on radio, latency and service cost considerations. The results suggest that Partial Duplication (PD) approaches can approach the reliability of multi-connectivity while substantially reducing duplication overhead in the evaluated rural scenario.

</details>


### [35] [Large-scale wireless network management via Open-RAN Tandem Apps: Cell on/off switching use case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24465)
*Paweł Kryszkiewicz,Łukasz Kułacz,Marcin Pakuła,Marcin Dryjanski,Marcin Hoffmann,Piotr Skrzypczak,Heiko Lehmann,Martin Stahn*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出Tandem Apps概念，通过在O-RAN架构的两个RIC层级部署紧密耦合的优化机制，实现既具备全局视野又保持敏捷低复杂度的网络管理方案，并以小区开关切换为例验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着移动网络复杂度增加，网络管理与优化日益困难。集中式算法面临高控制数据开销和计算负载，而分布式方法性能往往远未达到最优，亟需一种平衡全局视野与执行效率的新型管理架构。

Method: 利用O-RAN架构中两层RAN智能控制器（RIC），设计运行于两个控制器上的一对紧密耦合的优化机制——Tandem Apps，通过架构和功能拆分实现敏捷、低复杂度的解决方案。文中以小区开关切换为具体用例进行实现，并在大规模异构网络中使用真实网络数据进行评估，同时使用商用网络软件验证其符合O-RAN标准。

Result: 在大规模异构网络中使用真实网络数据的实验表明，Tandem Apps能够有效实现小区开关切换优化。验证结果显示该方案完全符合O-RAN标准，与商用网络软件兼容。

Conclusion: Tandem Apps概念成功将集中式全局视野与分布式执行效率相结合，为O-RAN架构下的分层网络管理提供了一种新的设计范式，在保持标准合规性的同时实现了性能与复杂度的良好平衡。

Abstract: With growing mobile-network complexity, management and optimization have become increasingly difficult. Centralized algorithms face high control-data overhead and computational load, while distributed approaches often perform far from optimally. The O-RAN architecture introduces two tiers of RAN Intelligent Controllers (RICs), enabling hierarchical network-management schemes. This work proposes Tandem Apps: a pair of tightly coupled optimization mechanisms running on both controllers. We show how to design Tandem Apps through architectural and functional splitting to achieve an agile, low-complexity solution that still preserves a global network view. As an example, we implement Tandem Apps for cell on/off switching and evaluate them in a large heterogeneous network using real network data. Although the Tandem Apps concept is new, it remains fully compliant with the O-RAN standard, as validated using commercial network software.

</details>


### [36] [TARMM: Scaling Delay-Critical Edge AI Offloading in 5G O-RAN via Temporal Graph Mobility Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24501)
*Peihao Yan,Yun Chen,Jie Lu,Qijun Wang,Huacheng Zeng*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了TARMM系统，一个基于O-RAN架构的5G移动性管理方案，利用时序图模型和多智能体强化学习（MARL）优化边缘AI卸载的切换决策，显著降低了尾延迟和丢包率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴的时延敏感型边缘AI应用（如VR感知和实时视频分析）对5G网络的时延和可靠性提出了严格要求，但现有的移动性管理机制主要是被动响应式的，无法适应动态网络条件，导致切换决策不佳和性能下降。

Method: 提出TARMM系统，其核心是构建一个时序图模型来捕捉无线接入网（RAN）在用户和小区之间的时空动态特性，从而实现近实时的切换决策。在此基础上，设计了结合基于规则的动作掩码和主动资源准备的多智能体强化学习（MARL）框架，以确保切换的安全性、稳定性和高效性。系统在多小区室内5G O-RAN测试平台上实现。

Result: 在多小区室内5G O-RAN测试平台上使用多样化的VR工作负载进行了广泛实验。结果表明，与最先进的方法相比，TARMM将尾延迟降低了高达44%，丢包率降低了高达56%。

Conclusion: TARMM通过利用时空图模型和MARL框架，成功解决了5G网络中时延敏感型边缘AI应用面临的移动性管理挑战，证明了主动式、智能化的切换决策机制在提升网络性能方面的有效性。

Abstract: Emerging delay-critical edge AI applications, such as VR perception and real-time video analytics, impose stringent latency and reliability requirements on 5G networks. However, existing mobility management mechanisms are largely reactive and fail to adapt to dynamic network conditions, resulting in suboptimal handover decisions and degraded performance. In this paper, we present TARMM, a 5G Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) system that optimizes user mobility management for delay-critical edge AI offloading. The core of TARMM is a temporal graph model that captures the spatiotemporal dynamics of the RAN across users and cells, enabling near real-time handover decisions. Building on this representation, we design a multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) framework with rule-based action masking and proactive resource preparation to ensure safe, stable, and efficient handovers. We implement TARMM on a multi-cell indoor 5G O-RAN testbed and evaluate it using diverse VR workloads. Extensive experiments show that TARMM reduces tail latency by up to 44% and packet loss by up to 56% compared to state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [37] [DECOFFEE: Decentralized Reinforcement Learning for Time-critical Workload Offloading and Energy Efficiency across the Computing Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24507)
*Anastasios Giannopoulos,Sotirios Spantideas,Panagiotis Trakadas*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DECOFFEE，一个用于边缘-云计算连续体的去中心化强化学习框架，通过双对抗DQN架构和LSTM预测，解决了大规模分布式基础设施中时间关键型工作负载的卸载和能效优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着延迟敏感且电池受限的物联网应用快速增长，在边缘-云计算连续体中亟需智能的工作负载放置机制。然而，由于随机到达、异构计算能力和时变网络条件，大规模分布式基础设施中的工作负载放置是一个高度动态且非凸的优化问题，现有方法难以在满足严格超时约束的同时优化执行延迟和能耗。

Method: 提出DECOFFEE框架，采用多智能体强化学习方案。每个边缘代理作为自主学习实体，基于本地系统观测和预测的网络条件推导最优策略。工作负载放置过程被建模为并行马尔可夫决策过程，使用增强型双对抗深度Q网络（DQN）架构求解，并结合长短期记忆网络（LSTM）预测未来负载条件，联合优化系统延迟、能耗和工作负载丢弃率。

Result: 大量仿真实验表明，DECOFFEE及其变体在不同流量和网络条件下，始终优于传统的基于规则和启发式放置策略，在延迟、能耗和工作负载丢弃率方面均实现了显著降低。

Conclusion: DECOFFEE框架通过去中心化多智能体学习架构，成功解决了边缘-云计算连续体中工作负载放置的动态优化问题，证明了强化学习结合序列预测在处理时间关键型、能效敏感型任务卸载方面的有效性。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of latency-sensitive and battery-constrained Internet-of-Things (IoT) applications has intensified the need for intelligent workload placement mechanisms across the Edge-Cloud computing continuum. In such environments, far-edge nodes must dynamically decide whether to execute workloads locally or offload them to neighboring nodes or the cloud, while accounting for execution delay, energy consumption, and strict timeout constraints. However, workload placement in large-scale distributed infrastructures is a highly dynamic and non-convex optimization problem due to stochastic arrivals, heterogeneous computing capacities, and time-varying network conditions. This paper proposes DECOFFEE, a decentralized reinforcement learning framework for time-critical workload offloading and energy-efficient operation across the computing continuum. The proposed multi-agent learning scheme jointly optimizes system delay, energy consumption, and workload drop rate through adaptive placement decisions. Each edge agent operates as an autonomous learning entity that derives an optimal policy from local system observations and predicted network conditions. The workload placement process is formulated as parallel Markov Decision Processes and solved using a Double Dueling Deep Q-Network (DQN) architecture enhanced with Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) forecasting to anticipate future load conditions. Extensive simulations demonstrate that DECOFFEE and its variants consistently outperform conventional rule-based and heuristic placement strategies, achieving significant reductions in delay, energy consumption, and workload drop rate under varying traffic and network conditions.

</details>

### [38] [Complete Cyclic Subtask Graphs for Tool-Using LLM Agents: Flexibility, Cost, and Bottlenecks in Multi-Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22820)
*Luay Gharzeddine,Samer Saab*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了完全循环子任务图（Complete Cyclic Subtask Graphs），作为一种最大灵活性的多智能体架构，用于研究显式重访机制在长视野工具使用任务中的效果。实验发现在ALFWorld中重访有助于恢复和探索，在TextCraft中简单的前向执行更高效，而在Finance-Agent中外部瓶颈（检索、接地）比工作流灵活性更关键。总体而言，增加的灵活性会带来显著的推理成本开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野工具使用任务有时需要重访早期子任务以进行恢复和探索，但目前缺乏对多智能体工作流灵活性带来的协调开销和推理成本的系统性分析。作者希望建立一个最大灵活性的实验框架，明确地研究子任务级别的重访机制何时有益、何时增加成本、何时受限于外部瓶颈。

Method: 提出了完全循环子任务图架构，其中可执行子任务节点全连接，由统一的状态分析与路由智能体使用自然语言标准选择转移。评估了任务特定图（Spec-Cyc）和基准通用图（Gen-Cyc），在TextCraft、ALFWorld和Finance-Agent三个基准上进行测试，并进行了消融实验，包括规划器/执行器/路由器强度、工具暴露（通用vs专用）、n-shot成功轨迹摘要和故障注入随机子任务扰动。

Result: 三个基准展现出三种不同模式：ALFWorld中显式重访支持恢复和探索；TextCraft作为先决条件链领域，更倾向于简单前向执行的效率；Finance-Agent主要受检索、接地和证据合成瓶颈限制。Token消耗对比显示增加的灵活性比单个ReAct智能体显著更昂贵。

Conclusion: 完全循环子任务图作为一个最大灵活性的实验透镜，能够有效测量多智能体重访机制在何时有帮助、何时主要增加协调成本、何时被外部任务瓶颈主导。研究揭示了工作流灵活性需要与任务特性匹配，否则会带来不必要的开销。

Abstract: Long-horizon tool-using tasks sometimes benefit from revisiting earlier subtasks for recovery and exploration, but added multi-agent workflow flexibility can also introduce coordination overhead and substantial inference cost. We study complete cyclic subtask graphs, a deliberately maximally flexible multi-agent architecture in which executable subtask nodes are fully connected and a unified state-analysis-and-routing agent selects transitions using natural-language criteria. This makes unrestricted revisitation explicit and directly analyzable at the subtask level. We evaluate task-specific (Spec-Cyc) and benchmark-generic (Gen-Cyc) graphs on TextCraft, ALFWorld, and Finance-Agent, with ablations over planner/executor/router strength, tool exposure (generalist vs specialized), $n$-shot successful trajectory summaries, and fault-injected random subtask perturbations. The benchmarks expose three distinct regimes. ALFWorld highlights a setting where explicit revisitation supports recovery and exploration; TextCraft, a largely prerequisite-chain domain, often favors the efficiency of simpler forward execution; and Finance-Agent remains bottlenecked by retrieval, grounding, and evidence synthesis more than by workflow flexibility alone. Shared-win token comparisons further show that the added flexibility can be substantially more expensive than a single ReAct agent. Overall, we use complete cyclic subtask graphs as a maximally flexible experimental lens for measuring when multi-agent revisitation helps, when it mainly adds coordination cost, and when external task bottlenecks dominate.

</details>


### [39] [Beyond Single-Agent Alignment: Preventing Context-Fragmented Violations in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22879)
*Jie Wu,Ming Gong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文识别了一类新型安全风险——上下文碎片化违规（CFVs），即由于关键策略事实分散在不同部门的私有上下文中，导致单个Agent行为看似安全合规，但整体违反组织策略。提出了分布式零信任执行架构Distributed Sentinel，通过语义污点标记协议实现跨域策略验证，在基准测试中F1达到0.95，证明了外部集中执行层的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于提示词的对齐机制和单体拦截器难以应对跨上下文孤岛的违规行为，而前沿LLM在多智能体工作流中表现出显著的违规率（14-98%），跨域数据流的违规率系统性高于同域流，说明自我规避机制不可靠，需要外部执行层。

Method: 提出Distributed Sentinel分布式零信任执行架构，引入语义污点标记（STT）协议；通过轻量级Sidecar代理跨组织边界传播安全状态，不暴露原始跨域数据；实现反事实图模拟进行跨域策略验证。构建PhantomEcosystem基准，包含9类真实跨智能体违规场景及对抗性平衡的安全控制。

Result: 在PhantomEcosystem基准上，Distributed Sentinel达到F1=0.95，端到端延迟106ms（A100上16ms验证+90ms实体提取）；对比方法中，基于提示词的过滤F1=0.85，基于规则的DLP F1=0.65；8个前沿LLM在执行导向的多智能体工作流中违规率为14-98%，跨域数据流违规率显著高于同域流。

Conclusion: 多智能体系统安全受益于在个体Agent之上运行的集中执行层，自我规避机制不可靠；Distributed Sentinel为跨上下文策略违规提供了有效的检测方案。

Abstract: We identify and formalize a novel security risk: Context-Fragmented Violations (CFVs) - a class of policy breaches where individual agent actions appear locally safe and reasonable, yet collectively violate organizational policies because critical policy facts are siloed in different departments private contexts. Existing prompt-based alignment mechanisms and monolithic interceptors are poorly matched to violations that span contextual islands. We propose Distributed Sentinel, a distributed zero-trust enforcement architecture that introduces the Semantic Taint Token (STT) Protocol. Through lightweight sidecar proxies, our system propagates security state across organizational boundaries without exposing raw cross-domain data, enabling Counterfactual Graph Simulation for cross-domain policy verification. We construct PhantomEcosystem, a comprehensive benchmark comprising 9 categories of realistic cross-agent violation scenarios with adversarially balanced safe controls. On this benchmark, Distributed Sentinel achieves F1 = 0.95 with 106ms end-to-end latency (16ms verification + 90ms entity extraction on A100), compared to 0.85 F1 for prompt-based filtering and 0.65 for rule-based DLP. To empirically validate the need for external enforcement, we evaluate eight frontier LLMs in execution-oriented multi-agent workflows with per-agent domain world models. All models exhibit substantial violation rates (14-98%), with cross-domain data flows showing systematically higher violation rates than same-domain flows. These results indicate that self-avoidance is unreliable and that multi-agent security benefits from a centralized enforcement layer operating above individual agents.

</details>


### [40] [Usable Agent Discovery for Decentralized AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23080)
*Patrizio Dazzi,Emanuele Carlini,Matteo Mordacchini,Saul Urso*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究了双层Churn（节点级和代理级）下的去中心化代理发现问题，发现结构化覆盖网络在稳定和节点Churn情况下表现更佳，而基于Gossip的覆盖网络在服务就绪性主导的场景下更具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模代理系统运行在分布式基础设施上，面临节点级Churn（故障和主机离开）与代理级Churn（按需激活、停用和状态变化）的双重挑战。这两种Churn的交互重新定义了结构化与非结构化覆盖网络之间的经典权衡，需要深入研究以指导系统设计。

Method: 假设节点托管多个代理，代理在温态和冷态之间切换。使用Kademlia作为结构化覆盖网络的代表，Cyclon+Vicinity作为基于Gossip的基线，对比了四种不同机制：稳定、仅节点Churn、仅代理冷却以及混合机制，以评估路由效率、弹性和服务就绪性的表现差异。

Result: 在稳定和仅节点Churn机制下，结构化覆盖网络表现出更强的鲁棒性和路由效率；而基于Gossip的覆盖网络保持竞争力，并且在服务就绪性主导的场景中可以提供更快的响应速度。

Conclusion: 结构化覆盖网络和基于Gossip的覆盖网络各有优势：前者适合稳定或以节点故障为主的环境，后者更适合服务就绪性要求高的动态环境。系统设计应根据具体的Churn模式和性能需求来选择合适的覆盖网络类型。

Abstract: Large-scale agentic systems run on distributed infrastructures where many software agents share physical hosts and are discovered via peer-to-peer mechanisms. Discovery must handle node-level churn from failures and host departures and agent-level churn from demand-driven activation, deactivation, and state changes. Their interaction reshapes classic trade-offs between structured and unstructured overlays. We study decentralized agent discovery under this two-level churn, assuming nodes host multiple agents, overlays are structured or gossip-based, and agents switch between warm and cold states. Using Kademlia as a structured and Cyclon+Vicinity as a gossip baseline, we compare stable, node-churn-only, agent-cooling-only, and combined regimes to see when routing efficiency, resilience, and service readiness align or favor different designs. Structured overlays are more robust and efficient in stable and node-churn regimes, while gossip-based overlays remain competitive and can be faster when readiness dominates.

</details>


### [41] [Architecture Matters for Multi-Agent Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23459)
*Ben Hagag,William L. Anderson,Christian Schroeder de Witt,Sarah Scheffler*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究通过实证分析发现，多智能体系统（MAS）在多数架构配置下比单智能体更易受攻击（攻击成功率最高相差3.8倍），且不存在普遍安全的设计方案，揭示了系统架构决策在任务性能与攻击防御之间存在复杂权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统（MAS）在生产部署中日益普及，但引入了单智能体场景中不存在的安全风险。即使单个智能体具有强健的安全性，协调它们的架构决策也可能产生尚未被系统表征的攻击面，因此需要研究MAS设计决策如何影响任务性能与攻击防御之间的权衡。

Method: 在三个智能体环境（浏览器、桌面和代码）中测试13种架构配置，采用分阶段评估方法区分规划拒绝、执行阶段拦截、部分有害执行和成功攻击完成，研究三个关键设计选择：（i）智能体角色（决定权限和责任分配）；（ii）通信拓扑（塑造智能体交互方式和时机）；（iii）记忆（决定每个智能体可访问的上下文和状态可见性）。

Result: 多智能体架构在大多数配置下比独立智能体更脆弱，在相当或更高良性准确率的情况下，攻击成功率变化高达3.8倍，且没有任何单一设计具有普遍安全性。

Conclusion: 研究结果推动了超越单智能体安全属性的评估方法的发展，表明需要更深入地研究多智能体系统的架构安全问题。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS), composed of networks of two or more autonomous AI agents, have become increasingly popular in production deployments, yet introduce security risks that do not arise in single-agent settings. Even if individual agents exhibit robust security, architectural decisions governing their coordination can create attack surfaces that have not been systematically characterized. In this work, we present an empirical study of how MAS design decisions shape the tradeoff between task performance and attack resistance. Across three agentic environments (browser, desktop, and code) and 13 architectural configurations, we use stagewise evaluations that distinguish planning refusal, execution-stage interception, partial harmful execution, and successful attack completion to study three key design choices: (i) agent roles, which determine how authority and responsibility are allocated; (ii) communication topology, which shapes how and when agents interact; and (iii) memory, which determines the context and state visibility accessible to each agent. We find that multi-agent architectures are more vulnerable than standalone agents in the majority of configurations, with attack success rates varying by up to 3.8x at comparable or higher benign accuracy, and that no single design is universally safer. These results motivate the development of further evaluations that move beyond the security properties of a single agent.

</details>


### [42] [DLM: Unified Decision Language Models for Offline Multi-Agent Sequential Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23557)
*Zhuohui Zhang,Bin Cheng,Bin He*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了决策语言模型（DLM），将多智能体决策制定为对话式序列预测问题，在离线多智能体强化学习中实现了优于基线的性能和强大的零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离线多智能体强化学习方法依赖固定的观测格式和动作空间，限制了泛化能力。而大语言模型提供了灵活的建模接口，能够自然地适应异构的观测和动作。

Method: 提出决策语言模型（DLM），将多智能体决策制定为在“集中训练、分散执行”范式下的对话式序列预测问题。训练分两阶段：监督微调阶段，利用对话式数据集进行带有智能体间上下文的集中训练，并从离线轨迹生成可执行动作；随后是组相对策略优化阶段，通过轻量级奖励函数增强对分布外动作的鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，统一的DLM优于强离线MARL基线和基于LLM的对话式决策方法，并展示了对跨任务未见场景的强大零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: DLM成功利用LLM的灵活建模能力解决了离线MARL中的泛化难题，为构建可扩展、可复用的多智能体决策策略提供了新途径。

Abstract: Building scalable and reusable multi-agent decision policies from offline datasets remains a challenge in offline multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), as existing methods often rely on fixed observation formats and action spaces that limit generalization. In contrast, large language models (LLMs) offer a flexible modeling interface that can naturally accommodate heterogeneous observations and actions. Motivated by this, we propose the Decision Language Model (DLM), which formulates multi-agent decision making as a dialogue-style sequence prediction problem under the centralized training with decentralized execution paradigm. DLM is trained in two stages: a supervised fine-tuning phase, which leverages dialogue-style datasets for centralized training with inter-agent context and generates executable actions from offline trajectories, followed by a group relative policy optimization phase to enhance robustness to out-of-distribution actions through lightweight reward functions. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that a unified DLM outperforms strong offline MARL baselines and LLM-based conversational decision-making methods, while demonstrating strong zero-shot generalization to unseen scenarios across tasks.

</details>


### [43] [EndoGov: A knowledge-governed multi-agent expert system for endometrial cancer risk stratification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23802)
*Weiye Dai,Liyun Shi,Zanxiang He,Yuling Ma,Mengyuan Lin,Dianxiang Sun,Liming Nie*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出EndoGov，一个双层多智能体专家系统，通过显式临床规则治理实现子宫内膜癌风险分层，在保证指南合规性的同时保持竞争性预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态AI模型在子宫内膜癌风险分层中虽优化了整体预测性能，但缺乏执行强制性指南覆盖的机制，例如将POLE突变肿瘤分配至低风险组等临床规则。

Method: 提出双层多智能体系统EndoGov，将决策过程分解为D(x) = G(P(x), R)。第一层包含病理、分子和临床智能体，从冻结的基础模型特征或结构化记录生成模式约束报告；第二层查询证据级加权指南知识图谱，对高优先级覆盖使用确定性硬路径规则，对模糊案例使用约束软路径推理。

Result: 在TCGA-UCEC数据集(n=541)上，EndoGov达到0.943准确率、0.973宏AUC，条件逻辑违规率仅0.93%；在CPTAC-UCEC数据集(n=95)上，准确率达0.842，而锁定迁移神经基线低于0.31。后端交换实验表明硬路径合规性对LLM后端具有不变性。端到端安全分解定位残留故障主要源于上游分子检测而非下游治理。

Conclusion: 显式临床规则治理可提供指南合规、可审计的子宫内膜癌风险分配，同时保持竞争性判别能力，为临床AI系统的安全部署提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multimodal artificial intelligence models for endometrial cancer (EC) risk stratification typically optimize aggregate predictive performance but provide limited mechanisms for enforcing mandatory guideline overrides, such as assigning POLE-mutated tumors to the low-risk group despite high-grade morphology. We present EndoGov, a two-tier multi-agent expert system that factorizes the decision process as D(x) = G(P(x), R), where specialist agents P extract structured evidence and a governance agent G applies an executable rule set R. Tier 1 comprises pathology, molecular, and clinical agents that independently generate schema-constrained reports from frozen foundation-model features or structured records. Tier 2 queries an evidence-level-weighted Guideline Knowledge Graph, using deterministic hard-path rules for high-priority overrides and constrained soft-path reasoning for ambiguous cases. In TCGA-UCEC (n=541), EndoGov achieved 0.943 accuracy, 0.973 macro AUC, and a conditional logic-violation rate (C-LVR) of 0.93% among trigger-exposed cases. In CPTAC-UCEC (n=95), where reference labels are guideline-derived, EndoGov reached 0.842 accuracy compared with < 0.31 for locked-transfer neural baselines, supporting governance-pathway transfer under distribution shift rather than validation against independent clinical truth. End-to-end safety decomposition localized residual failures primarily to upstream molecular detection rather than downstream governance. Backend-swap experiments further showed that hard-path compliance is invariant to the LLM backend. These findings indicate that explicit clinical-rule governance can provide guideline-compliant, auditable EC risk assignment while preserving competitive discrimination.

</details>

### [44] [The Spectral Lifecycle of Transformer Training: Transient Compression Waves, Persistent Spectral Gradients, and the Q/K--V Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22778)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了Transformer预训练过程中权重矩阵奇异值谱的动态变化，发现了瞬态压缩波、持续谱梯度和Q/K-V功能不对称性三种现象，并证明了谱结构在预测层重要性和剪枝方面的实际价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Transformer模型广泛应用，但其预训练过程中权重矩阵奇异值谱的动态演化规律尚不清楚。理解这些动态特性对于揭示模型训练机制、指导模型压缩和优化具有重要意义。

Method: 在三个模型规模（30M-285M参数）上，每隔25步跟踪每个权重矩阵的完整SVD分解，在九个模型（自定义、GPT-2、Pythia；30M-1B参数；8-36层）上进行验证，建立双时间尺度动力学模型，并设计谱引导的剪枝实验。

Result: 发现三种现象：（1）瞬态压缩波：稳定秩压缩以行波形式从早期层传播到晚期层；（2）持续谱梯度：幂律指数α形成永久深度梯度，呈非单调倒U形；（3）Q/K-V功能不对称性：值/输出投影均匀压缩，查询/键投影承载深度依赖动态。推导出缩放定律（Δα∝L^0.26，R²=0.99），α可预测层重要性（ρ=0.69-0.84），谱引导剪枝比Last-N启发式方法优1.1×-3.6×。

Conclusion: 秩和谱形状编码了关于训练的根本不同信息，谱结构对模型行为具有因果影响。这为理解Transformer训练动态提供了新视角，并为模型压缩和层重要性评估提供了理论指导和实用工具。

Abstract: We present the first systematic study of weight matrix singular value spectra \emph{during} transformer pretraining, tracking full SVD decompositions of every weight matrix at 25-step intervals across three model scales (30M--285M parameters). We discover three phenomena: \textbf{(1)~Transient Compression Waves:} stable rank compression propagates as a traveling wave from early to late layers, creating a dramatic gradient that peaks early then \emph{reverses} -- late layers eventually over-compress past early layers. \textbf{(2)~Persistent Spectral Gradients:} the power-law exponent~$α$ develops a permanent depth gradient forming a non-monotonic inverted-U in deeper models, with peaks shifting toward earlier layers as depth increases. \textbf{(3)~Q/K--V Functional Asymmetry:} value/output projections compress uniformly while query/key projections carry the full depth-dependent dynamics. The dissociation between transient compression and persistent spectral shape reveals that \emph{rank and spectral shape encode fundamentally different information about training}. We formalize this as a two-timescale dynamical model and derive scaling laws ($Δα\propto L^{0.26}$, $R^2{=}0.99$). We validate on nine models across three families (custom, GPT-2, Pythia; 30M--1B parameters; 8--36 layers), demonstrate that $α$ predicts layer importance ($ρ{=}0.69$--$0.84$, $p{<}0.02$), and show that spectral-guided pruning outperforms Last-N heuristics by $1.1{\times}$--$3.6{\times}$ across seven models in two families (GPT-2 124M--774M, Pythia 160M--1B), with worst-vs-best gaps up to $23.7{\times}$ confirming the causal role of spectral structure.

</details>


### [45] [KARL: Mitigating Hallucinations in LLMs via Knowledge-Boundary-Aware Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22779)
*Cheng Gao,Cheng Huang,Kangyang Luo,Ziqing Qiao,Shuzheng Si,Huimin Chen,Chaojun Xiao,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出KARL框架，通过知识边界感知奖励和两阶段强化学习策略，使大模型能够根据自身知识边界动态决定是否回答问题，在抑制幻觉的同时保持高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法虽然能让大模型自主 abstention（弃答），但由于其静态奖励机制不考虑模型的知识边界，往往导致模型过度谨慎，从而损害回答准确率。

Method: 提出KARL框架，包含两个核心创新：1) 知识边界感知奖励，利用组内响应统计进行在线知识边界估计，动态奖励正确回答或引导性弃答；2) 两阶段强化学习训练策略，第一阶段探索知识边界并避免“弃答陷阱”，第二阶段将知识边界外的错误回答转化为弃答，同时不牺牲准确率。

Result: 在多个基准测试上的实验表明，KARL在分布内和分布外场景下均实现了优越的准确率-幻觉权衡，有效抑制幻觉的同时保持了高准确率。

Conclusion: KARL成功地将大模型的弃答行为与其动态变化的知识边界持续对齐，解决了现有方法在准确率与幻觉之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Enabling large language models (LLMs) to appropriately abstain from answering questions beyond their knowledge is crucial for mitigating hallucinations. While existing reinforcement learning methods foster autonomous abstention, they often compromise answer accuracy because their static reward mechanisms, agnostic to models' knowledge boundaries, drive models toward excessive caution. In this work, we propose KARL, a novel framework that continuously aligns an LLM's abstention behavior with its evolving knowledge boundary. KARL introduces two core innovations: a Knowledge-Boundary-Aware Reward that performs online knowledge boundary estimation using within-group response statistics, dynamically rewarding correct answers or guided abstention; and a Two-Stage RL Training Strategy that first explores the knowledge boundary and bypasses the "abstention trap", and subsequently converts incorrect answers beyond the knowledge boundary into abstentions without sacrificing accuracy. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that KARL achieves a superior accuracy-hallucination trade-off, effectively suppressing hallucinations while maintaining high accuracy across both in-distribution and out-of-distribution scenarios.

</details>


### [46] [BiTA: Bidirectional Gated Recurrent Unit-Transformer Aggregator in a Temporal Graph Network Framework for Alert Prediction in Computer Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22781)
*Zahra Makki Nayeri,Mohsen Rezvani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了BiTA框架，通过在时序图神经网络中引入双向GRU-Transformer聚合器，解决了现有方法难以捕获递归多尺度时间模式的问题，在网络告警预测任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时序图神经网络方法主要依赖单向或单一机制的时序聚合，限制了其对现实网络攻击行为中常见的递归、多尺度时间模式的捕获能力，无法充分建模复杂的攻击时序特征。

Method: 提出BiTA（双向GRU-Transformer聚合器），通过重新设计TGN框架内的时序聚合函数，联合编码双向序列依赖和节点时序邻域上的长程上下文关系，在保持原有TGN记忆和消息传递结构的同时，实现不同尺度的互补时序推理。

Result: 在真实网络告警数据集上，BiTA在AUC、平均精度、平均倒数排名和各类别预测准确率等关键指标上均显著优于现有的时序图模型，在直推和归纳设置下均表现出鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: BiTA是一个可扩展且可解释的框架，能够有效支持实时网络威胁预测，为构建更智能和自适应的入侵检测系统铺平了道路。

Abstract: Proactive alert prediction in computer networks is critical for mitigating evolving cyber threats and enabling timely defensive actions. Temporal Graph Neural Networks (TGNs) provide a principled framework for modeling time-evolving interactions; however, existing TGN-based methods predominantly rely on unidirectional or single-mechanism temporal aggregation, which limits their ability to capture recursive, multi-scale temporal patterns commonly observed in real-world attack behaviors. In this paper, we propose BiTA, a Bidirectional Gated Recurrent Unit-Transformer Aggregator for temporal graph learning. Rather than introducing a deeper or higher-capacity model, BiTA redesigns the temporal aggregation function within the TGN framework by jointly encoding bidirectional sequential dependencies and long-range contextual relations over each node's temporal neighborhood. This aggregation strategy enables complementary temporal reasoning at different scales while preserving the original TGN memory and message-passing structure. We evaluate BiTA on real-world alert datasets, demonstrating significant improvements in key performance metrics such as area under the curve, average precision, mean reciprocal rank, and per-category prediction accuracy when compared to state-of-the-art temporal graph models. BiTA outperforms baseline methods under both transductive and inductive settings, highlighting its robustness and generalization capabilities in dynamic network environments. BiTA is a scalable and interpretable framework for real-time cyber threat anticipation, paving the way toward more intelligent and adaptive intrusion detection systems.

</details>


### [47] [Stochastic KV Routing: Enabling Adaptive Depth-Wise Cache Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22782)
*Anastasiia Filippova,David Grangier,Marco Cuturi,João Monteiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出随机跨层注意力训练方法，使模型能共享或丢弃各层KV缓存，从而显著降低推理时的内存占用且保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer推理中KV缓存的内存占用巨大，影响服务成本。现有压缩与驱逐方法主要沿时间轴，而深度维度虽被认为有冗余，但跨层共享因会降低吞吐或增加首token延迟而难以实用。

Method: 提出一种简单训练策略：随机跨层注意力。训练时，每层随机选择使用自身的KV状态或前一层的KV状态，使模型对多种深度维度的缓存共享策略具有鲁棒性，部署时可根据硬件约束灵活选择策略，如直接丢弃某些层的缓存。

Result: 在预训练或微调中应用该方法，可在多种模型家族实现深度维度的缓存共享。在数据受限的大模型上，该方法展现出正则化效果，常能保持或提升性能，同时显著降低缓存内存占用。

Conclusion: 深度维度的缓存优化是一条可行路径，随机跨层注意力训练方法简单高效，为KV缓存内存缩减提供了新范式。

Abstract: Serving transformer language models with high throughput requires caching Key-Values (KVs) to avoid redundant computation during autoregressive generation. The memory footprint of KV caching is significant and heavily impacts serving costs. This work proposes to lessen these memory requirements. While recent work has largely addressed KV cache reduction via compression and eviction along the temporal axis, we argue that the \emph{depth} dimension offers an orthogonal and robust avenue for optimization. Although prior research suggests that a full cache for every layer is redundant, implementing cross-layer cache sharing remains a practical challenge; existing methods typically suffer from reduced throughput or increased time-to-first-token. In this paper, we demonstrate that dropping a layer's cache offers efficient optimization without information loss. We propose a simple training approach: random cross-layer attention. During training, layers randomly choose to attend either to their own KV states or those of a preceding layer. This stochastic process adapts the model to be robust to various depth-wise cache sharing strategies, ensuring flexibility for unknown hardware constraints at deployment time. Our evaluations show that applying this scheme during pre-training or fine-tuning enables depth-wise cache sharing for various model families. Furthermore, for larger models in data-constrained settings, this approach is suggestive of a regularization-like effect, frequently preserving or improving performance while significantly reducing the cache's memory footprint.

</details>


### [48] [Parameter Efficiency Is Not Memory Efficiency: Rethinking Fine-Tuning for On-Device LLM Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22783)
*Irene Tenison,Stella Ahn,Miriam Kim,Ebtisam Alshehri,Lalana Kagal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LARS框架，通过约束激活子空间而非模型参数，解耦内存消耗与序列长度的关系，在GPU和CPU上分别平均降低33.54%和51.95%的内存占用，实现了资源受限设备上的大语言模型个性化适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战了"参数高效等同于内存高效"的广泛假设。现有的PEFT方法（如LoRA和IA3）虽然显著减少了可训练参数，但仍受限于随序列长度线性增长的中间张量，常在设备上触发内存溢出错误，无法真正实现端侧适配。

Method: 提出LARS（Low-memory Activation-Rank Subspace）框架，与传统PEFT方法对模型参数施加低秩约束不同，LARS对训练过程中使用的激活子空间施加约束，直接针对内存消耗的主要来源，从根本上平抑内存增长率，实现内存消耗与序列长度的解耦。

Result: 在GPU和CPU上相比LoRA分别平均降低33.54%和51.95%的内存占用，涵盖推理、理解和长上下文数据集及多种模型，同时保持竞争力的准确率和吞吐量。在树莓派和消费级CPU上的部署验证了其在资源受限硬件和边缘设备上的可扩展性。

Conclusion: LARS为资源受限硬件和边缘设备上复杂的大语言模型个性化提供了可扩展的路径，证明了激活子空间约束是解决序列长度相关内存瓶颈的有效方法，实现了真正的设备端适配能力。

Abstract: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) has become the standard for adapting large language models (LLMs). In this work we challenge the wide-spread assumption that parameter efficiency equates memory efficiency and on-device adaptability. We show that this is not true - while methods like LoRA and IA3 significantly reduce trainable parameters, they remain bound by intermediate tensors that scale linearly with sequence length, often triggering out-of-memory errors on-device. In this work, we introduce LARS (Low-memory Activation-Rank Subspace), a novel adaptation framework that decouples memory consumption from sequence length. While prior PEFT methods apply low-rank constraints to model parameters, LARS instead constrains the activation subspace used during training, directly targeting the dominant source of memory consumption and fundamentally flattening the memory growth rate. LARS reduces the memory footprint by an average of 33.54% on GPUs and 51.95% on CPUs in comparison to LoRA across reasoning, understanding and long-context datasets using different models while maintaining competitive accuracy and throughput. Besides GPUs, we deploy on Raspberry Pi and consumer-grade CPUs to demonstrate that LARS provides a scalable path for sophisticated LLM personalization on resource-constrained hardware and edge devices.

</details>


### [49] [Learning Without Adversarial Training: A Physics-Informed Neural Network for Secure Power System State Estimation under False Data Injection Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22784)
*Solon Falas,Markos Asprou,Charalambos Konstantinou,Maria K. Michael*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理信息神经网络(PINN)的电力系统状态估计方法，通过动态损失加权策略提高对隐蔽性虚假数据注入攻击的鲁棒性，在IEEE 118节点系统上表现出优于传统固定权重PINN变体的准确性和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力系统状态估计是控制中心运行的核心，随着电网数字化程度提高，其鲁棒性成为信息物理安全的关键问题。神经网络方法作为传统模型驱动方法的替代方案受到关注，而PINN通过将潮流一致性嵌入学习目标已显示出更好的精度，但对隐蔽性FDIA攻击的鲁棒性仍需提升。

Method: 提出一种基于PINN的电力系统状态估计模型，不采用对抗训练，而是基于同方差不确定性的动态损失加权方法，自动学习数据拟合项和物理残差项的相对权重比例，降低对人工权重调参的敏感性。针对隐蔽约束的AC虚假数据注入攻击进行防御设计。

Result: 在IEEE 118节点系统上进行测试，采用代表性的隐蔽性FDIA攻击族（包括状态扭曲、负荷重分配、线路过载和残差约束隐蔽破坏）。以电压幅值和相位角的平均绝对误差(MAE)为性能指标，结果表明该方法比现有固定权重PINN变体具有更高的准确性和稳定性。

Conclusion: 所提出的动态损失加权PINN模型能有效保护电力系统状态估计免受隐蔽性FDIA攻击，无需对抗训练即可实现良好防御效果，为智能电网安全状态估计提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: State estimation is a cornerstone of power system control-center operations, and its robust operation is increasingly a cyber-physical security concern as modern grids become more digitalized and communication-intensive. Neural network-based approaches have gained attention as alternatives to conventional model-based state estimation methods. Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs), which embed power-flow consistency into the learning objective, have shown improved accuracy over existing approaches. This work proposes a PINN-based model for Power System State Estimation (PSSE) that protects the estimation process against the stealth-constrained AC False Data Injection Attacks (FDIAs) considered in this study. The model is developed without adversarial training. Instead, a dynamic loss-weighting formulation based on homoscedastic uncertainty learns the relative scaling of supervised data-fit and physics-residual terms during training, reducing sensitivity to manual weight tuning. Robustness is evaluated on the IEEE 118-bus system using representative stealthy-FDIA families including state distortion, load redistribution, line overloading, and residual-constrained stealth corruption. Performance is measured using Mean Absolute Error (MAE) on voltage magnitudes and phase angles. Results demonstrate higher accuracy and stability than existing fixed-weight PINN variants.

</details>


### [50] [CoFi-PGMA: Counterfactual Policy Gradients under Filtered Feedback for Multi-Agent LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22785)
*Stela Tong,Elai Ben-Gal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CoFi-PGMA框架，通过基于边际贡献的反事实训练目标，解决多智能体LLM系统中因路由选择或协作奖励导致的反馈信号过滤问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统中，路由机制仅对被选中的响应提供反馈（选择门控反馈），协作机制产生共享奖励导致个体贡献难以区分，这使得传统的单策略RLHF目标函数在多智能体场景下出现错配。

Method: 提出CoFi-PGMA框架，推导基于边际贡献的反事实单智能体训练目标。针对路由系统，该目标对应选择门控反馈的离策略修正；针对协作系统，该目标简化为用于信用分配的留一差分奖励。方法还分析了softmax路由如何诱导风险敏感激励，并整合了反事实估计器、多轮感知奖励和策略优化方法。

Result: 在实际推理数据集上验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: CoFi-PGMA为多智能体LLM系统中的过滤反馈学习提供了统一的解决方案，能够正确修正路由和协作机制下的学习信号。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) deployments increasingly rely on multi-agent architectures in which multiple models either compete through routing mechanisms or collaborate to produce a final answer. In both settings, the learning signal received by each agent is filtered by the system mechanism. Routing produces selection-gated feedback where only the chosen response is evaluated, while collaboration produces shared rewards that obscure the individual contribution of each agent. As a result, standard RLHF objectives designed for a single deployed policy become misspecified. We introduce CoFi-PGMA (Counterfactual Policy Gradients under Filtered Feedback for Multi-Agent LLMs), a unified framework for learning under filtered feedback in multi-agent LLM systems. Our approach derives a counterfactual per-agent training objective based on marginal contribution, which corrects the learning signal under both routing and collaborative mechanisms. For routing systems, the objective corresponds to off-policy corrections for selection-gated feedback, while for collaborative systems it reduces to leave-one-out difference rewards for credit assignment. We further analyze how softmax routing induces risk-sensitive incentives and provide practical training algorithms that integrate counterfactual estimators, multiturn-aware rewards, and policy optimization methods, and demonstrate the approach on a real-world reasoning dataset.

</details>


### [51] [Scaling Multi-Node Mixture-of-Experts Inference Using Expert Activation Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23150)
*Abhimanyu Bambhaniya,Geonhwa Jeong,Jason Park,Jiecao Yu,Jaewon Lee,Pengchao Wang,Changkyu Kim,Chunqiang Tang,Tushar Krishna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过分析主流MoE模型的专家激活规律，提出工作负载感知的微批分组和专家放置策略，将节点间通信数据减少高达20倍，从而降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最先进的MoE大模型在推理时面临专家负载不平衡和低效令牌路由的瓶颈，特别是在多节点部署中，令牌无法保证路由到本地专家，导致巨大的节点间all-to-all通信开销。

Method: 研究者对Llama 4 Maverick、DeepSeek V3-671B和Qwen3-230B-A22B等开源MoE模型进行了性能分析，收集了超过10万条真实专家激活轨迹。基于发现的专家激活规律（可变负载不平衡、领域特定激活、预填充与解码激活强相关），提出了工作负载感知的微批分组和专家放置策略，以最大化令牌到目标专家的地方性。

Result: 所提出的优化方法在所有模型和数据集上将all-to-all通信数据减少高达20倍，降低了MoE解码延迟并提高了加速器利用率。

Conclusion: 通过系统性地表征和优化MoE推理中的负载不平衡和路由问题，可以显著提升大规模部署的效率和性能，为未来MoE模型的推理优化提供了新的见解和方法。

Abstract: Most recent state-of-the-art (SOTA) large language models (LLMs) use Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures to scale model capacity without proportional per-token compute, enabling higher-quality outputs at manageable serving costs. However, MoE inference at scale is fundamentally bottlenecked by expert load imbalance and inefficient token routing, especially in multi-node deployments where tokens are not guaranteed to be routed to local experts, resulting in significant inter-node all-to-all communication overhead.
  To systematically characterize these challenges, we profile SOTA open-source MoE models, including Llama 4 Maverick, DeepSeek V3-671B, and Qwen3-230B-A22B, on various datasets and collected over 100k real expert activation traces. Upon studying the expert activation patterns, we uncover various persistent properties across all the frontier MoE models: variable expert load imbalance, domain-specific expert activation where expert popularity shifts across task families (code, math, chat, general), and a strong correlation between prefill and decode expert activations. Motivated by these findings, we propose workload-aware micro-batch grouping and an expert placement strategy to maximize token locality to the destination expert, thereby reducing inter-node communication. Across models and datasets, these optimizations help reduce all2all communication data up to 20, resulting in lower MoE decode latency and better accelerator utilization.

</details>


### [52] [AutoCompress: Critical Layer Isolation for Efficient Transformer Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22786)
*Archit Thorat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了AutoCompress方法，基于Layer 0承载关键信息的发现，通过保护第0层的架构设计实现了2.47倍的压缩比，显著优于均匀压缩基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者发现小型Transformer中第0层承载了不成比例的任务关键信息，其基于NTK的重要性分数为3.6，而其他层最大仅为0.054，差距超过60倍。这一经验性发现促使作者专门设计架构来保护这一关键层。

Method: 提出Critical Layer Isolation (CLI)架构：保持第0层全维度不变，通过学习的瓶颈压缩所有中间层，并在最后一层恢复全维度。该方法应用于GPT-2 Medium模型。

Result: CLI-GPT2在WikiText-103上达到204.5的困惑度，参数量仅143.8M（原始354.8M），实现了2.47倍压缩比和59.5%的参数减少。消融实验显示，同等大小的均匀瓶颈基线在相同训练条件下困惑度仅为571.8，证实保护第0层的架构设计是性能提升的主要驱动因素。

Conclusion: 保护关键层（第0层）的架构决策比单纯减少模型规模更重要。该研究为Transformer压缩提供了新的见解，证明了层特异性处理的有效性。代码和检查点已公开。

Abstract: We present AutoCompress, a transformer compression method motivated by an empirical finding: in small transformers, Layer 0 carries disproportionately high task-critical information, with an NTK-based importance score of 3.6 compared to a maximum of 0.054 for all other layers -- a gap of over 60x. Based on this finding, we propose Critical Layer Isolation (CLI), an architecture that protects Layer 0 at full dimensionality, compresses all intermediate layers through a learned bottleneck, and restores the full dimension at the final layer. Applied to GPT-2 Medium (354.8M parameters), CLI-GPT2 achieves 204.5 perplexity on WikiText-103 with only 143.8M parameters -- a 2.47x compression ratio and 59.5% parameter reduction. Crucially, an ablation study demonstrates that a uniform bottleneck baseline of comparable size achieves only 571.8 perplexity under identical training conditions, confirming that the architectural decision to protect Layer 0 -- rather than simply reducing model size -- is the primary driver of performance. Code and checkpoints are publicly available.

</details>


### [53] [Efficient VQ-QAT and Mixed Vector/Linear quantized Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23172)
*Terry Gou,Puneet Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了三种基于向量量化的模型权重压缩技术，引入余弦相似度分配和可微分NAS搜索配置，虽然性能未全面超越现有方法，但为VQ压缩的设计权衡提供了有价值的见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决向量量化（VQ）模型压缩中存在的码本崩溃问题，并实现端到端训练，研究者希望改进权重压缩方法，同时探索自适应选择量化配置的可能性。

Method: 1. 采用基于余弦相似度的分配策略以缓解码本崩溃并支持端到端训练；
2. 借鉴可微分K均值（DKM）的注意力机制思想，结合余弦相似度分配、top-1采样和直通估计器，避免了加权平均重建；
3. 引入可微分神经架构搜索（NAS），自适应选择各层的量化配置以优化压缩过程。

Result: 所提方法在部分量化级别上未能持续超越现有方法，但通过实验揭示了VQ模型压缩方法的设计权衡与行为特性。

Conclusion: 尽管性能未全面领先，本研究为理解和设计向量量化模型压缩方法提供了重要见解，指出了设计权衡的关键因素，为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: In this work, we developed and tested 3 techniques for vector quantization (VQ) based model weight compression. To mitigate codebook collapse and enable end-to-end training, we adopted cosine similarity-based assignment. Building on ideas from attention-based formulations in Differentiable K-Means (DKM), we further improved this approach by using cosine similarity for assignment combined with top-1 sampling and a straight-through estimator, thereby eliminating the need for weighted-average reconstruction. Finally, we investigated the use of differentiable neural architecture search (NAS) to adaptively select layer-wise quantization configurations, further optimizing the compression process. Although our method does not consistently outperform existing approaches across all quantization levels, it provides useful insights into the design trade-offs and behaviors of VQ-based model compression methods.

</details>


### [54] [Conformal PM2.5 Mapping Under Spatial Covariate Shift: Satellite-Reanalysis Fusion for Africa's Green Industrial Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22787)
*Yaw Osei Adjei,Davis Opoku,Ephraim Abotsi,Kwadwo Owusu Amanqua,Oliver Kornyo,Elisha Soglo-Ahianyo,Cephas Anertey Abbey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究基于非洲29个国家404个监测点的数据，开发了卫星-再分析PM2.5融合系统，揭示了模型在地理泛化上的真实困难，并提出了区域可靠性分级和监测优先级评分，以指导非洲绿色工业化进程中的空气质量基础设施扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲绿色工业化迫切需要可靠的空气质量监测基础设施，但现有监测覆盖不足且模型在地理泛化方面存在挑战，需要准确评估预测的可靠性及其适用范围。

Method: 利用OpenAQ数据集（2017-2022年，29个非洲国家，404个监测点，2,068,901条记录），结合LightGBM模型、抗泄漏空间交叉验证和分裂共形预测方法，量化预测结果及其地理适用边界。

Result: 在5折位置分组空间交叉验证下，LightGBM的RMSE为30.83±5.07 μg/m³，MAE为14.54±1.66 μg/m³，R²为0.134±0.023，宏观F1为0.336±0.018。R²虽远低于随机划分基准（>0.90），但反映的是真实的地理泛化困难而非模型失败。共形预测显示东非覆盖率严重下降（实际PICP=65.3%，名义为90%），与中强度的协变量偏移一致（湿度KS=0.2237，卫星边界层高度KS=0.2558）。

Conclusion: 研究将发现转化为区域可靠性标志（高/中/低/不可靠）和监测优先级评分，指导基础设施扩展向负担最重且未监测的人群倾斜，直接支持非洲绿色工业化转型及相关可持续发展目标（SDGs 3.9、7.1.2、9、11.6.2和13）。

Abstract: Africa's green industrialization imperative demands reliable infrastructure for monitoring air quality. We present a satellite-reanalysis PM2.5 fusion system trained on 2,068,901 records from 404 monitoring locations in 29 African countries (OpenAQ, 2017-2022), combining LightGBM with leakage-resistant spatial cross-validation and conformal prediction to quantify predictions and their geographic applicability limits. Under 5-fold location-grouped spatial cross-validation, LightGBM achieves RMSE = 30.83 +/- 5.07 ug/m3, MAE = 14.54 +/- 1.66 ug/m3, R2 = 0.134 +/- 0.023, and macro F1 = 0.336 +/- 0.018. This R2 is substantially below random-split benchmarks (>0.90) but reflects true geographic generalisation difficulty rather than model failure. Split conformal prediction targeting 90% marginal coverage reveals severe East Africa degradation (actual PICP = 65.3% vs. nominal 90%), consistent with medium-strength covariate shift (humidity KS = 0.2237, sat_pblh KS = 0.2558). We operationalise these findings through regional reliability flags (High/Medium/Low/Unreliable) and a monitor prioritisation score directing infrastructure expansion toward highest-burden unmonitored populations, directly supporting Africa's green industrial transition and SDGs 3.9, 7.1.2, 9, 11.6.2, and 13.

</details>


### [55] [Evaluating CUDA Tile for AI Workloads on Hopper and Blackwell GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23466)
*Divakar Kumar Yadav,Tian Zhao,Deepak Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文首次独立跨架构评估NVIDIA CuTile：在B200上融合注意力性能达FlashAttention-2的2.5倍（仅60行Python），但GEMM仅为cuBLAS的52-79%；在RTX PRO 6000上仅达53%，跨架构优化差距明显。Triton跨平台可移植性更强（62-101% cuBLAS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NVIDIA的CuTile提出基于Python、以Tile为中心的GPU核函数开发抽象，旨在简化编程同时保持Tensor Core与TMA的高效性。现有研究缺乏对CuTile在跨架构（Hopper与Blackwell）下的独立性能与可移植性评估，与cuBLAS、Triton、WMMA等主流方案的系统性对比也尚未展开。

Method: 在H100 NVL、B200及RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell Server Edition三款GPU上，首次对CuTile进行独立、跨架构的评估。基准测试涵盖GEMM、融合多头注意力及端到端LLM推理（BF16/FP16精度），对比cuBLAS、Triton、WMMA与原始SIMT。统一评估性能与可移植性。

Result: CuTile效果高度依赖负载与架构。在B200上，融合注意力达1007 TFLOP/s，超FlashAttention-2约2.5倍（60行Python）；GEMM达cuBLAS的52-79%（22行，WMMA需123行）。但在RTX PRO 6000（sm_120）上，相同核函数仅达FlashAttention-2的53%，跨架构优化缺口显著。Triton在所有平台无需架构特定调优即可达cuBLAS的62-101%，可移植性更强。

Conclusion: CuTile可用简洁Python代码在特定架构（如B200）上实现高性能，适合作为手写CUDA核函数的实用替代，但尚不足以替代厂商优化库。跨架构可移植性有待提升；若需稳健跨平台性能，Triton仍是更优选择。

Abstract: NVIDIA's CUDA Tile (CuTile) introduces a Python-based, tile-centric abstraction for GPU kernel development that aims to simplify programming while retaining Tensor Core and Tensor Memory Accelerator (TMA) efficiency on modern GPUs. We present the first independent, cross-architecture evaluation of CuTile against established approaches such as cuBLAS, Triton, WMMA, and raw SIMT on three NVIDIA GPUs spanning Hopper and Blackwell: H100 NVL, B200, and RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell Server Edition. We benchmark representative AI workloads, including GEMM, fused multi-head attention, and end-to-end LLM inference in BF16/FP16 precision, to assess both performance and portability.
  Our results show that CuTile effectiveness is strongly workload- and architecture-dependent. On datacenter-class Blackwell (B200), CuTile achieves up to 1007 TFLOP/s for fused attention, outperforming FlashAttention-2 by 2.5x while requiring only 60 lines of Python kernel code. For GEMM, CuTile reaches 52-79% of cuBLAS performance in 22 lines of code (versus 123 for WMMA), making it a practical replacement for hand-written CUDA kernels but not yet for vendor-optimized libraries. However, the same CuTile attention kernel achieves only 53% of FlashAttention-2 throughput on RTX PRO 6000 (sm_120), exposing significant cross-architecture optimization gaps. In contrast, Triton sustains 62-101% of cuBLAS performance across all tested platforms without architecture-specific tuning, demonstrating substantially stronger portability.

</details>


### [56] [Avionic Main Fuel Pump Simulation and Fault-Diagnosis Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22869)
*Felix Leonhard Janzen,Lukas Moddemann,Alexander Diedrich,Oliver Niggemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对飞机关键系统异常检测数据稀缺问题，构建了一个高保真的飞机主燃油泵物理仿真基准数据集，并验证了RNN-VAE和SOM-VAE在该数据上进行异常检测和运行模式离散化的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在信息物理系统（特别是飞机等关键应用）中，由于数据保护问题和部分可观察性，用于训练异常检测和诊断算法的数据非常匮乏。为了解决这一固有的数据缺乏问题，作者需要构建高质量的仿真数据集。

Method: 作者使用MATLAB/Simulink Simscape Fluids构建了一个常见的飞机主燃油泵系统的高保真、物理信息协同仿真模型，生成了带有健康和故障模式注释的时间序列数据。为验证基准的可行性，应用了无监督循环变分自编码器（RNN-VAE）进行异常检测，以及SOM-VAE进行运行模式离散化，旨在区分健康和故障状态。

Result: 成功构建并公开了一个高保真的飞机主燃油泵系统仿真基准数据集，包含详细的健康与故障模式标注。实验证明，RNN-VAE和SOM-VAE模型能够有效利用该数据进行异常检测和运行模式离散化，成功区分了健康与故障条件。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了解决关键系统异常检测数据稀缺问题的有效途径，即通过高保真物理仿真生成高质量标注数据。该基准数据集为后续相关算法的研究与评估提供了重要支持。

Abstract: In many cyber-physical systems, especially in critical applications such as aeroplanes, data to train anomaly detection and diagnosis algorithms is lacking due to data protection issues and partial observability. To combat this inherent lack of data, we introduce a high-fidelity, physics-informed co-simulation of a common aircraft main-fuel-pump system modelled in \textsc{MATLAB/Simulink Simscape Fluids}. We also describe its generated time-series data with health and fault mode annotations. To show feasibility of our benchmark, we apply an unsupervised Recurrent Variational Autoencoder (RNN-VAE) for anomaly detection and a SOM-VAE for operating mode discretization, trained to separate healthy and faulty conditions.

</details>


### [57] [Hybrid JIT-CUDA Graph Optimization for Low-Latency Large Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23467)
*Divakar Kumar Yadav,Tian Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合运行时框架，结合JIT编译与CUDA Graph执行，有效降低了LLM在短序列、低延迟场景下的推理延迟和方差，TTFT最高降低66.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）在自然语言和多模态任务上表现优异，但在交互式、短序列场景中，推理延迟和kernel启动开销仍是制约其实际部署的主要瓶颈。

Method: 提出一种混合运行时框架，将Transformer推理划分为静态与动态组件：静态部分通过CUDA Graph重放执行，动态部分通过JIT编译内核处理，并支持跨解码步骤的异步图捕获与重用，从而在减少启动开销的同时保持运行时灵活性。

Result: 在LLaMA-2 7B模型上，单GPU、批大小为1的条件下（prompt长度10-500 tokens），该方法将Time-to-First-Token（TTFT）最高降低66.0%，且P99延迟低于TensorRT-LLM。

Conclusion: 混合JIT-CUDA Graph执行能有效减少短序列LLM工作负载的推理延迟和方差，是面向延迟敏感型AI应用的实用优化策略。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved strong performance across natural language and multimodal tasks, yet their practical deployment remains constrained by inference latency and kernel launch overhead, particularly in interactive, short-sequence settings. This paper presents a hybrid runtime framework that combines Just-In-Time (JIT) compilation with CUDA Graph execution to reduce launch overhead while preserving runtime flexibility during autoregressive decoding. The framework partitions transformer inference into static components executed via CUDA Graph replay and dynamic components handled through JIT-compiled kernels, enabling asynchronous graph capture and reuse across decoding steps.
  We evaluate the proposed approach on LLaMA-2 7B using single-GPU, batch-size-one inference across prompt lengths from 10 to 500 tokens. Experimental results show that the hybrid runtime reduces Time-to-First-Token (TTFT) by up to 66.0% and achieves lower P99 latency compared with TensorRT-LLM in this regime. These results indicate that hybrid JIT-CUDA Graph execution can effectively reduce inference latency and variance for short-sequence LLM workloads, making it a practical optimization strategy for latency-sensitive AI applications.

</details>


### [58] [Towards Understanding the Expressive Power of GNNs with Global Readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22870)
*Maurice Funk,Daumantas Kojelis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了ACR-GNNs的一阶性质表达能力，证明仅用求和聚合和读出即可超越C2逻辑，并发现限制局部聚合或限定图度数可使GNNs表达能力精确对应于带全局计数的分级模态逻辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对ACR-GNNs一阶性质表达能力的逻辑刻画难题，旨在明确其与C2逻辑的关系边界，厘清聚合与读出函数如何推动GNNs表达能力超越C2。

Method: 通过理论证明分析ACR-GNNs的表达能力：首先证明求和聚合与读出足以超越C2逻辑；其次分别研究限制局部聚合、以及在有界度图上运行两种情形，考察其逻辑刻画。

Result: 仅用求和聚合和读出的GNNs可表达C2逻辑无法定义的FO性质；限制局部聚合或在有界度图上运行时，GNNs恰好等价于带全局计数的分级模态逻辑。

Conclusion: ACR-GNNs的表达能力下界超越C2逻辑，但在特定约束下可精确刻画；正是聚合与读出的无界交互使GNNs表达能力突破C2。

Abstract: We study the expressive power of message-passing aggregate-combine-readout graph neural networks (ACR-GNNs). Particularly, we focus on the first-order (FO) properties expressible by this formalism. While a tight logical characterisation remains a difficult open question, we make two contributions towards answering it. First, we show that sum aggregation and readout suffice for GNNs to capture FO properties that cannot be expressed in the logic C2 on both directed and undirected graphs. This strengthens known results by Hauke and Wał{\k e}ga (2026) where aggregation and readout functions are specially crafted for the task. Second, we identify two natural ways of restoring characterisability (with regard to C2) for ACR-GNNs. One option is to limit local aggregation (without imposing restrictions on global readout), whilst the second is to run ACR-GNNs over graphs of bounded degree (but unbounded size). In both cases, the FO properties captured by GNNs are exactly those definable by a formula in graded modal logic with global counting modalities. Our results thus establish an innate lower- and upper-bound in terms of how far (fragments of) C2 can be taken to characterise GNNs, and imply that is indeed the unbounded interaction of aggregation and readout that pushes the logical expressive power of GNNs above C2.

</details>


### [59] [When Policies Cannot Be Retrained: A Unified Closed-Form View of Post-Training Steering in Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22873)
*Elias Hossain,Mohammad Jahid Ibna Basher,Ivan Garibay,Ozlem Garibay,Niloofar Yousefi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究冻结离线演员的部署时适应问题。主要发现是PoE和KL正则化适应表现为优雅降级而非普适性能提升：在退化先验下能锚定冻结演员避免崩溃，但在高难度任务中存在性能上限。这两种方法应被视为部署时导向的安全机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习从固定数据集学习策略，但部署目标可能在训练后发生变化。由于数据、成本或治理限制，训练好的演员往往无法重新训练。因此需要研究在冻结演员情况下的部署时适应方法。

Method: 研究使用专家乘积(PoE)组合与目标条件先验进行部署时适应。在冻结演员设置下，推导出闭合形式等式：对角高斯演员和先验下，系数为α的PoE产生与β=α/(1-α)的KL正则化适应相同的确定性策略。提出KL预算选择器恢复近oracle操作点。

Result: 在四个D4RL环境(3,900 MuJoCo回合)中观察到4/5/3的HELP/FROZEN/HURT分裂。扩展到六个更难单元和两个AntMaze诊断时发现演员能力天花板：medium-expert在所有9个单元的所有测试α值下都是HURT，AntMaze使用行为克隆冻结演员时所有组合规则成功率为零。精度加权组合在退化或随机先验下仍锚定冻结演员，而加性和仅先验适应则崩溃。

Conclusion: PoE和KL正则化适应最好被视为部署时导向的单演员锚定安全机制，提供优雅降级而非普适性能增益，在性能上有明确上限约束。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) can learn effective policies from fixed datasets, but deployment objectives may change after training, and in many applications the trained actor cannot be retrained because of data, cost, or governance constraints. We study deployment-time adaptation for frozen offline actors using Product-of-Experts (PoE) composition with a goal-conditioned prior. Our main practical finding is graceful degradation rather than universal performance gain: under degraded or random priors, precision-weighted composition remains anchored to the frozen actor, while additive and prior-only adaptation collapse, and a KL-budget selector often recovers a near-oracle operating point. We also make explicit a closed-form identity in the frozen-actor setting: for diagonal-Gaussian actors and priors, PoE with coefficient alpha yields the same deterministic policy as KL-regularized adaptation with beta = alpha / (1 - alpha), with posterior covariances differing only by a global scalar factor. Empirically, across four D4RL environments (3,900 MuJoCo episodes), we observe a 4/5/3 HELP/FROZEN/HURT split. Extending the analysis to six harder cells and two AntMaze diagnostics reveals an actor-competence ceiling: medium-expert remains HURT in all 9 cells at every tested alpha, while AntMaze with a behavior-cloned frozen actor yields zero success for all composition rules. Overall, PoE and KL-regularized adaptation are best viewed as a single actor-anchored safety mechanism for deployment-time steering.

</details>


### [60] [MTServe: Efficient Serving for Generative Recommendation Models with Hierarchical Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22881)
*Xin Wang,Chi Ma,Shaobin Chen,Pu Wang,Menglei Zhou,Junyi Qiu,Qiaorui Chen,Jiayu Sun,Shijie Liu,Zehuan Wang,Lei Yu,Chuan Liu,Fei Jiang,Wei Lin,Hao Wang,Jiawei Jiang,Xiao Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MTServe系统，通过分层缓存管理解决生成式推荐中KV缓存的存储爆炸问题，实现3.1倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐模型推理成本高昂，跨请求KV缓存复用虽能优化但面临用户状态存储规模远超GPU物理内存限制的挑战。

Method: 设计分层缓存管理系统，利用主机RAM作为可扩展备份存储实现GPU内存虚拟化；引入混合存储布局、异步数据传输流水线以及局部性驱动的替换策略来弥补层级间的I/O差距。

Result: 在公共和生产数据集上实现高达3.1倍加速，同时保持接近完美的命中率（>98.5%）。

Conclusion: MTServe有效解决了生成式推荐中KV缓存的存储瓶颈问题，通过系统级优化实现了显著的推理加速和优异的缓存命中率。

Abstract: Generative recommendation (GR) offers superior modeling capabilities but suffers from prohibitive inference costs due to the repeated encoding of long user histories. While cross-request Key-Value (KV) cache reuse presents a significant optimization opportunity, the massive scale of individual user states creates a storage explosion that far exceeds physical GPU limits. We propose MTServe, a hierarchical cache management system that virtualizes GPU memory by leveraging host RAM as a scalable backup store. To bridge the I/O gap between tiers, MTServe introduces a suite of system-level optimizations, including a hybrid storage layout, an asynchronous data transfer pipeline, and a locality-driven replacement policy. On both public and production datasets, MTServe delivers up to 3.1* speedup while maintaining near-perfect hit ratios (>98.5%).

</details>


### [61] [Predicting Wind Loads on Container Ships in Harbor Environments through Multi-Fidelity Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22882)
*Matilde Fiore,Andrea Bresciani,Miguel Alfonso Mendez,Jeroen van Beeck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种多保真度代理模型框架，融合经验公式与CFD模型预测现代大型集装箱船的风载荷系数，相比传统方法显著提高了预测精度并降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代集装箱船由于受风面积增大面临更大的风载荷，现有经验模型主要针对较小受风面积的船只开发，缺乏准确性且未考虑附近结构物的影响，无法满足现代大型船舶系泊设计的需求。

Method: 提出一种多保真度代理建模框架，结合经验公式、简化CFD模型和详细CFD模型，利用递推协同克里金方法融合不同保真度信息；通过敏感性分析识别关键几何参数，采用序列采样高效构建训练数据库。

Result: 在多种装载配置和两个不同的港口环境中验证了代理模型。多保真度方法相比单保真度模型显著提高了预测精度，同时大幅减少了对高保真仿真的依赖；该框架能捕捉风载荷对关键几何参数的依赖性，性能始终优于传统经验公式。

Conclusion: 该多保真度框架为船舶工程应用提供了一种稳健且高效的工具，能够在降低计算成本的同时实现高精度的风载荷预测，有效解决了现有方法在现代大型集装箱船上的局限性。

Abstract: Modern container ships face higher wind loads due to increased windage areas, making accurate predictions of wind loads essential for mooring design. Existing empirical models, largely developed for container ships with smaller windage areas and simpler geometrical configurations than those of modern large-scale vessels, often lack accuracy and do not account for the influence of nearby structures. This study proposes a multi-fidelity surrogate modelling framework for the prediction of wind-load coefficients, combining empirical correlations with simplified and detailed CFD models for ships in open-sea and harbor environments. The approach relies on recursive co-kriging to consistently fuse information across fidelity levels, enabling accurate predictions at a reduced computational cost. A sensitivity analysis is used to identify the most influential geometric parameters, and the resulting reduced parameter space is explored through sequential sampling to efficiently construct the training database.
  The surrogate models are validated over a wide range of loading configurations and for two distinct harbor environments. The results demonstrate that the multi-fidelity approach significantly improves prediction accuracy compared to single-fidelity models, while substantially reducing the reliance on high-fidelity simulations. In particular, the proposed framework captures the dependence of wind loads on key geometric parameters and consistently outperforms traditional empirical correlations, providing a robust and efficient tool for engineering applications.

</details>


### [62] [FlashOverlap: Minimizing Tail Latency in Communication Overlap for Distributed LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24013)
*Rezaul Karim,Austin Wen,Wang Zongzuo,Weiwei Zhang,Yang Liu,Walid Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Flash-Overlap方法，通过分解P2P通信替代传统集合通信，消除分布式大模型训练中的通信尾延迟，有效提升计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型分布式训练中的并行化策略（如张量并行、数据并行）会产生巨大的数据通信开销，严重阻碍计算效率；现有的基于数据切片的通信-计算重叠技术存在尾延迟问题。

Method: 提出Flash-Overlap技术，用分解的点对点（P2P）通信替代传统的reduce-scatter和all-gather集合操作，并调度分区的计算以实现细粒度的通信-计算重叠，从而消除尾延迟。

Result: 实验评估表明，该方法能够持续实现更低的延迟、更高的模型FLOPS利用率（MFU）和高吞吐量。

Conclusion: Flash-Overlap是一种通用的解决方案，兼容数据并行训练和多种张量级并行策略（包括TPSP和UP），能有效缓解分布式训练和推理中的通信瓶颈。

Abstract: The rapid growth in the size of large language models has necessitated the partitioning of computational workloads across accelerators such as GPUs, TPUs, and NPUs. However, these parallelization strategies incur substantial data communication overhead significantly hindering computational efficiency. While communication-computation overlap presents a promising direction, existing data slicing based solutions suffer from tail latency. To overcome this limitation, this research introduces a novel communication-computation overlap technique to eliminate this tail latency in state of the art overlap methods for distributed LLM training. The aim of this technique is to effectively mitigate communication bottleneck of tensor parallelism and data parallelism for distributed training and inference. In particular, we propose a novel method termed Flash-Overlap that replaces conventional collective operations of reduce-scatter and all-gather with decomposed peer-to-peer (P2P) communication and schedules partitioned computations to enable fine-grained overlap. Our method provides an exact algorithm for reducing communication overhead that eliminates tail latency. Moreover, it presents a versatile solution compatible with data-parallel training and various tensor-level parallelism strategies, including TPSP and UP. Experimental evaluations demonstrate that our technique consistently achieves lower latency, superior Model FLOPS Utilization (MFU), and high throughput.

</details>


### [63] [Quantifying and Mitigating Self-Preference Bias of LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22891)
*Jinming Yang,Chuxian Qiu,Zhenyu Deng,Xinshan Jiao,Tao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个自动化框架来量化和缓解LLM评估中的自偏好偏差(SPB)，发现模型能力与低SPB无相关甚至负相关，并通过结构化多维评估策略平均减少31.5%的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge方法在自动评估系统中发挥关键作用，但自偏好偏差(SPB)会扭曲其可扩展性和可信度。现有测量方法依赖昂贵的人工标注，且混淆了生成能力与评估立场，不适合大规模实际部署。

Method: 提出了一个全自动化框架来量化和缓解SPB：(1)构建质量差异可忽略的等质量响应对，实现无需人工金标准的统计分解；(2)提出基于认知负荷分解的结构化多维评估策略来缓解偏差。

Result: 在20个主流LLM上的实证分析表明：(1)先进能力往往与低SPB无关甚至负相关；(2)所提出的结构化多维评估策略平均减少31.5%的SPB。

Conclusion: 该框架解决了LLM评估中自偏好偏差的量化与缓解问题，证明了模型能力与评估公正性的独立性，为构建更可信的LLM评估系统提供了有效方法。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge has become a dominant approach in automated evaluation systems, playing critical roles in model alignment, leaderboard construction, quality control, and so on. However, the scalability and trustworthiness of this approach can be substantially distorted by Self-Preference Bias (SPB), which is a directional evaluative deviation in which LLMs systematically favor or disfavor their own generated outputs during evaluation. Existing measurements rely on costly human annotations and conflate generative capability with evaluative stance, and thus are impractical for large-scale deployment in real-world systems. To address this issue, we introduce a fully automated framework to quantifying and mitigating SPB, which constructs equal-quality pairs of responses with negligible quality differences, enabling statistical disentanglement of discriminability from bias propensity without human gold standards. Empirical analysis across 20 mainstream LLMs reveals that advanced capabilities are often uncorrelated, or even negatively correlated, with low SPB. To mitigate this bias, we propose a structured multi-dimensional evaluation strategy grounded in cognitive load decomposition, which reduces SPB by 31.5\% on average.

</details>


### [64] [FreeScale: Distributed Training for Sequence Recommendation Models with Minimal Scaling Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24073)
*Chenhao Feng,Haoli Zhang,Shakhzod Ali-Zade,Yanli Zhao,Liang Luo,Jennifer Cao,Lisen Deng,Siqiao Chen,Chenyu Zhao,Tristan Rice,Daniel Johnson,Min Si,Tiantu Xu,Yi Zhang,Siqi Yan,Chuanhao Zhuge,Min Ni,Bi Xue,Qunshu Zhang,Shen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FreeScale系统，通过负载均衡、通信计算重叠和SM-Free技术，解决了大规模推荐模型训练中的计算气泡问题，在256卡H100集群上实现了90.3%的计算气泡减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代工业级深度学习推荐模型在处理序列交互历史时，数据特征的异构性导致大规模训练中出现严重的掉队者(stragglers)和阻塞通信，从而产生大量计算气泡，造成计算资源的严重浪费。

Method: 提出FreeScale解决方案，包含三个核心技术：(1)通过精细的负载均衡输入样本缓解掉队者问题；(2)通过将优先级嵌入通信与计算重叠来最小化阻塞通信；(3)采用SM-Free技术在通信过程中解决计算与通信重叠时的GPU资源竞争。

Result: 在256个H100 GPU的真实工作负载上进行的实证评估表明，FreeScale能够实现高达90.3%的计算气泡减少。

Conclusion: FreeScale有效解决了大规模推荐模型训练中的资源利用率问题，显著提升了训练效率，为工业级深度学习推荐系统的大规模部署提供了可行的优化方案。

Abstract: Modern industrial Deep Learning Recommendation Models typically extract user preferences through the analysis of sequential interaction histories, subsequently generating predictions based on these derived interests. The inherent heterogeneity in data characteristics frequently result in substantial under-utilization of computational resources during large-scale training, primarily due to computational bubbles caused by severe stragglers and slow blocking communications. This paper introduces FreeScale, a solution designed to (1) mitigate the straggler problem through meticulously load balanced input samples (2) minimize the blocking communication by overlapping prioritized embedding communications with computations (3) resolve the GPU resource competition during computation and communication overlapping by communicating through SM-Free techniques. Empirical evaluation demonstrates that FreeScale achieves up to 90.3% reduction in computational bubbles when applied to real-world workloads running on 256 H100 GPUs.

</details>


### [65] [StackFeat RL: Reinforcement Learning over Iterative Dual Criterion Feature Selection for Stable Biomarker Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22892)
*A. Yermekov,D. A. Herrera-Martí*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了StackFeat-RL，一种基于强化学习的元学习特征选择框架，在COVID-19和阿尔茨海默病基因组数据上以更少特征实现了最高预测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维基因组数据（$d \gg n$）的特征选择需要兼顾准确性、稀疏性和稳定性，现有方法存在需人工设定阈值、数据扰动下不稳定或忽略生物结构等问题。

Method: 提出StackFeat-RL元学习框架，通过REINFORCE策略梯度优化迭代双准则特征选择算法的超参数；双准则要求系数一致性和选择频率，迭代累积通过大数定律提供收敛保证。

Result: 在COVID-19 miRNA数据（GSE240888，332特征）和三个阿尔茨海默病分类任务（GSE84422，13237基因）上，StackFeat-RL在所有评估方法中取得最高预测准确率，且所需特征数减少3-4倍。

Conclusion: StackFeat-RL有效解决了高维基因组数据特征选择的准确性-稀疏性-稳定性权衡问题，在生物标志物发现任务中显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Feature selection in high-dimensional genomic data ($d \gg n$) demands methods that are simultaneously accurate, sparse, and stable. Existing approaches either require manual threshold specification (mRMR, stability selection), produce unstable selections under data perturbation (Lasso, Boruta), or ignore biological structure entirely. We introduce StackFeat-RL, a meta-learning framework that optimises the hyperparameters of an iterative dual-criterion feature selection algorithm via REINFORCE policy gradients. The dual criterion, requiring both coefficient consistency and selection frequency, guards against two failure modes missed by single-criterion methods, while iterative accumulation provides convergence guarantees via the law of large numbers.
  On COVID-19 miRNA data (GSE240888, 332 features) and three Alzheimer's disease classification tasks (GSE84422, 13237 genes; Normal vs.\ Possible, Probable, and Definite AD), StackFeat-RL achieves the highest predictive accuracy among all evaluated methods, including ElasticNet, Boruta, mRMR, and stability selection, while requiring 3--4$\times$ fewer features.
  Keywords: feature selection, reinforcement learning, REINFORCE, elastic net, biomarker discovery, Alzheimer's disease, dual-criterion selection, protein interaction networks

</details>


### [66] [An Analysis of Active Learning Algorithms using Real-World Crowd-sourced Text Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23290)
*Varun Totakura,Ankita Singh,Yushun Dong,Shayok Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过众包平台收集真实人工标注数据，对8种常见的深度主动学习算法在噪声标注者场景下进行了首次大规模实证研究，填补了现有研究使用机器学习模型模拟噪声标注者与现实场景脱节的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动学习研究通常假设标注者是无误的，或使用机器学习模型模拟噪声标注者。然而现实世界中，标注者不仅可能提供错误标签，还可能拒绝提供标签。机器学习模型难以准确捕捉真实人类标注的复杂性和噪声模式，因此亟需在真实人工标注数据上评估主动学习算法的性能。

Method: 1. 从众包平台收集真实人工标注数据，涵盖3个基准文本分类数据集；
2. 整理8种常用主动学习技术（结合深度神经网络）；
3. 在获得的真实标注数据上进行大规模实证实验，考虑标注者提供错误标签和拒绝标注两种真实场景。

Result: 研究揭示了主动学习算法在真实噪声标注场景下的性能表现，分析了算法在面对标注错误和标注拒绝时的鲁棒性和局限性，相关实验数据已开源。

Conclusion: 本研究为深度主动学习系统在现实应用中的部署提供了宝贵的实证见解，强调了在真实人工标注条件下评估主动学习算法的重要性，所开源的真实标注数据集可支持后续研究。

Abstract: Active learning algorithms automatically identify the most informative samples from large amounts of unlabeled data and tremendously reduce human annotation effort in inducing a machine learning model. In a conventional active learning setup, the labeling oracles are assumed to be infallible, that is, they always provide correct answers (in terms of class labels) to the queried unlabeled instances, which cannot be guaranteed in real-world applications. To this end, a body of research has focused on the development of active learning algorithms in the presence of imperfect / noisy oracles. Existing research on active learning with noisy oracles typically simulate the oracles using machine learning models; however, real-world situations are much more challenging, and using ML models to simulate the annotation patterns may not appropriately capture the nuances of real-world annotation challenges. In this research, we first collect annotations of text samples (from 3 benchmark text classification datasets) from crowd-sourced workers through a crowd-sourcing platform. We then conduct extensive empirical studies of 8 commonly used active learning techniques (in conjunction with deep neural networks) using the obtained annotations. Our analyses sheds light on the performance of these techniques under real-world challenges, where annotators can provide incorrect labels, and can also refuse to provide labels. We hope this research will provide valuable insights that will be useful for the deployment of deep active learning systems in real-world applications. The obtained annotations can be accessed at https://github.com/varuntotakura/al_rcta/.

</details>


### [67] [Utility-Aware Data Pricing: Token-Level Quality and Empirical Training Gain for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22893)
*Minghui Xu,Qi Luo,Kun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种动态数据估值框架，通过三层架构（信息密度、训练增益、密码学验证）实现基于效用的定价，准确衡量数据对大语言模型的贡献，实验证明其性能显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的“行数×质量系数”数据估值方法过于简单线性，无法捕捉数据对大语言模型（LLM）能力的细微、非线性贡献，导致数据价值评估不准确，阻碍了公平的数据市场发展。

Method: 提出动态数据估值框架，包含三层：（1）使用香农熵和数据质量分数的token级信息密度度量；（2）通过影响函数、代理模型策略和数据Shapley值测量经验训练增益；（3）利用哈希承诺、Merkle树和防篡改训练账本实现密码学可验证性。

Result: 在指令跟随、数学推理和代码摘要三个真实领域进行实验验证，结果表明基于代理模型的经验增益方法在效用排名对齐上接近完美，显著优于行数和token计数基线方法。

Conclusion: 该框架能够建立公平的数据即服务经济，使高推理数据根据其对模型智能的实际贡献进行定价，同时提供可信数据市场所需的透明度和可审计性。

Abstract: Traditional data valuation methods based on ``row-count $\times$ quality coefficient'' paradigms fail to capture the nuanced, nonlinear contributions that data makes to Large Language Model (LLM) capabilities. This paper presents a dynamic data valuation framework that transitions from static accounting to utility-based pricing. Our approach operates on three layers: (1) token-level information density metrics using Shannon entropy and Data Quality Scores; (2) empirical training gain measurement through influence functions, proxy model strategies, and Data Shapley values; and (3) cryptographic verifiability through hash-based commitments, Merkle trees, and a tamper-evident training ledger. We provide comprehensive experimental validation on three real domains (instruction following, mathematical reasoning, and code summarization), demonstrating that proxy-based empirical gain achieves near-perfect ranking alignment with realized utility, substantially outperforming row-count and token-count baselines. This framework enables a fair Data-as-a-Service economy where high-reasoning data is priced according to its actual contribution to model intelligence, while providing the transparency and auditability necessary for trustworthy data markets.

</details>


### [68] [Accelerating Frequency Domain Diffusion Models with Error-Feedback Event-Driven Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22901)
*Dong Liu,Haisheng Wang,Yanxuan Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出E²-CRF方法，利用频谱局部性和镜像对称特性，通过闭环误差反馈系统自适应缓存Transformer KV特征，实现频域扩散模型约2.2倍加速且保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在时间序列生成中表现优异，但其推理速度缓慢，限制了实际部署。现有频域扩散模型仍有加速空间。

Method: 利用信号能量集中在低频的频谱局部性和镜像对称性，设计闭环误差反馈系统，在扩散步骤间自适应缓存Transformer KV特征。采用事件驱动的残差动态触发重计算，而非固定调度，选择性重计算高能量或快速变化的token，同时复用稳定高频分量的缓存特征。

Result: 在5个数据集上验证，实现约2.2倍加速，同时保持样本质量。提供了标准正则假设下的充分条件误差和复杂度界。

Conclusion: E²-CRF缓存策略与扩散过程的结构到细节演进自然对齐，有效加速频域扩散模型。代码已开源并集成到FastCache-xDiT中。

Abstract: Diffusion models achieve remarkable success in time series generation. However, slow inference limits their practical deployment. We propose E$^2$-CRF (Error-Feedback Event-Driven Cumulative Residual Feature caching) to accelerate frequency domain diffusion models. Our method exploits two structural properties: (1) spectral localization, where signal energy concentrates in low frequencies, and (2) mirror symmetry, which halves the effective frequency dimension. E$^2$-CRF uses a closed-loop error-feedback system that adaptively caches transformer KV features across diffusion steps. We trigger recomputation using event-driven residual dynamics instead of fixed schedules. Our method selectively recomputes high-energy or rapidly-changing tokens while reusing cached features for stable high-frequency components. E$^2$-CRF achieves ~2.2 speedup while maintaining sample quality. We demonstrate effectiveness on 5 datasets. Our caching strategy naturally aligns with the diffusion process's structure-to-detail progression. We include sufficient-condition error and complexity bounds under standard regularity assumptions (Appendix), alongside empirical validation. Our code is available at https://github.com/NoakLiu/FastFourierDiffusion and is also integrated in https://github.com/NoakLiu/FastCache-xDiT.

</details>


### [69] [Deep Clustering for Climate: Analyzing Teleconnections through Learned Categorical States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22909)
*Lívia Meinhardt,Dário Oliveira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用掩码孪生网络（Masked Siamese Networks）将气候时间序列离散化为语义丰富的聚类，成功识别出有意义的气候状态，并发现其与厄尔尼诺现象存在统计关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解和表征复杂的气候变率对于科学分析和预测建模至关重要，但从原始变量中识别有意义的气候状态面临高噪声和非线性依赖等挑战。

Method: 采用掩码孪生网络（Masked Siamese Networks）对气候时间序列进行自监督离散化，将其划分为语义丰富的聚类，重点针对日最低和最高温度数据进行分析。

Result: 生成的聚类反映了有意义的气候状态，为下游任务提供了简化的表示；能够对特定气候情景进行采样和分析；聚类表现出与厄尔尼诺事件的统计关联。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明自监督离散化是气候数据分析的有力工具，为未来整合更丰富的气候指标开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Understanding and representing complex climate variability is essential for both scientific analysis and predictive modeling. However, identifying meaningful climate regimes from raw variables is challenging, as they exhibit high noise and nonlinear dependencies. In this work, we explore the use of Masked Siamese Networks to discretize climate time series into semantically rich clusters. Focusing on daily minimum and maximum temperature, we show that the resulting representations: (i) yield clusters that reflect meaningful climate states under our modeling assumptions, offering a simplified representation for downstream use; (ii) enable sampling and analysis of specific climate scenarios; and (iii) exhibit statistical associations with El Niño events, underscoring their scientific relevance. Our findings highlight the potential of self-supervised discretization as a tool for climate data analysis and open avenues for incorporating richer climate indicators in future work.

</details>


### [70] [Score-Repellent Monte Carlo: Toward Efficient Non-Markovian Sampler with Constant Memory in General State Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22948)
*Jie Hu,Lingyun Chen,Geeho Kim,Jinyoung Choi,Bohyung Han,Do Young Eun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Score-Repellent Monte Carlo (SRMC)框架，通过指数得分倾斜将历史轨迹编码为斥力，以常数内存O(d)实现高维空间中的历史依赖采样，将渐近协方差降低至O(1/α)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有历史依赖采样方案通过有限状态空间上的经验测度编码历史，这在高维离散配置空间中不可行，或在连续域中不适定；需要一种适用于一般状态空间的通用方法。

Method: 提出SRMC框架：利用得分的滑动平均在R^d中总结轨迹历史，通过指数得分倾斜将其转化为代理目标，引入参数α控制斥力强度。该代理族在标准MCMC意义下无需归一化，可包装任意基础核：每次迭代在当前代理π_{θ_n}上运行基础核，同时在线更新历史。

Result: 利用受控马尔可夫噪声的随机近似分析历史递归与MC估计器的耦合演化，证明了几乎必然收敛和联合中心极限定理；识别出渐近协方差随α增加以O(1/α)比例降低的机制。连续目标和离散能量模型实验显示估计方差降低、模覆盖改善，同时保持O(d)内存和较低迭代开销。

Conclusion: SRMC将有限状态历史依赖采样器的近零方差效应扩展至一般状态空间，以常数内存实现历史依赖采样，为高维采样问题提供了通用且理论完备的解决方案。

Abstract: History-dependent sampling can reduce long-run Monte Carlo variance by discouraging redundant revisits, but existing schemes typically encode history through empirical measure on finite state spaces, which is infeasible in high-dimensional discrete configuration spaces or ill-posed in continuous domains. We propose Score-Repellent Monte Carlo (SRMC) framework that summarizes trajectory history by a running average of score evaluations in $R^d$, where $d$ is the dimension of the score and state representation. This history is converted into a surrogate target through an exponential score tilt, indexed with $α$ that represents the strength of repellence in controlling the magnitude of the history-based repulsion. The surrogate family is normalization-free in the standard MCMC sense, yielding a generic wrapper: at each iteration, any base kernel targeting $π$ can instead be run on the current surrogate $π_{θ_n}$ while the history is updated online. We analyze the coupled evolution of the history recursion and Monte Carlo estimators using stochastic approximation with controlled Markovian noise, establishing almost sure convergence and a joint central limit theorem. We further identify regimes in which the asymptotic covariance decreases as $α$ increases, with scaling $O(1/α)$, extending the near-zero-variance effect of finite-state history-dependent samplers to general state spaces with constant memory. Experiments on continuous targets and discrete energy-based models demonstrate improved estimator variance and mode coverage, while retaining $O(d)$ memory usage and modest per-iteration overhead.

</details>
