The study compares supervised, contrastive, and self-distillation methods over two standard benchmark datasets, MNIST and FashionMNIST. The analysis of the experimental results shows that self-supervised methods create substantially fewer regions to achieve comparable accuracy to supervised models. Contrastive methods rapidly expand regions over time, whereas self-distillation methods tend to consolidate by merging neighbouring regions. Lastly, we can detect representation collapse early within the geometric space of linear regions. Our analysis suggests that polytopal metrics can serve as reliable indicators of representation quality and model performance.

</details>


### [182] [An Automatic Ground Collision Avoidance System with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24403)
*Seyyid Osman Sevgili,Atahan Cilan,Mahir Demir,Özgün Can Yürütken,Ümit Can Bekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了一种面向高级喷气教练机的AI自动地面防撞系统（AGCAS），通过有限观测空间内的视线查询方法，旨在提升飞行安全与作战效能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着航空航天工程领域持续演进，AI技术的集成对于在严格时间约束下提升作战效率至关重要。然而，在高级喷气教练机这一特定场景中，现有AGCAS系统面临观测空间有限的挑战，亟需一种能在有限信息下实现精确防撞的解决方案。

Method: 提出了一种专门针对高级喷气教练机设计的AI驱动AGCAS系统。该方法核心在于解决有限观测空间下的防撞问题，通过对地形服务器进行视线（line-of-sight）查询，以实现对地形的精确感知与碰撞规避。

Result: 该系统成功设计并验证了在有限观测空间内的运行能力，利用视线查询机制确保了防撞过程的精确性与高效性。研究结果表明该方法能有效应对高级喷气教练机的特殊需求。

Conclusion: 基于AI的AGCAS系统能够显著提升高级喷气教练机的安全性和作战能力，验证了将AI技术集成至航空防撞系统的可行性与有效性，为未来航空工程发展提供了重要参考。

Abstract: This article evaluates an artificial intelligence (AI)-based Automatic Ground Collision Avoidance System (AGCAS) designed for advanced jet trainers to enhance operational effectiveness. In the continuously evolving field of aerospace engineering, the integration of AI is crucial for advancing operations with improved timing constraints and efficiency. Our study explores the design process of an AI-driven AGCAS, specifically tailored for advanced jet trainers, focusing on addressing the AGCAS problem within a limited observation space. The system utilizes line-of-sight queries on a terrain server to ensure precise and efficient collision avoidance. This approach aims to significantly improve the safety and operational capabilities of advanced jet trainers.

</details>


### [183] [Advancing Ligand-based Virtual Screening and Molecular Generation with Pretrained Molecular Embedding Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24474)
*Shiyun Wa,Yifei Wang,Simone Sciabola,Ye Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了预训练嵌入距离（PED）作为分子相似性度量，该方法无需特定任务训练，在虚拟筛选和分子生成任务中表现有效，具有可扩展性和通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分子相似性度量方法（如基于指纹的Tanimoto系数或3D形状叠加）在大规模计算时成本高昂或依赖手工特征；现有深度学习方法依赖特定监督或昂贵数据标注，限制了跨靶点的通用性。

Method: 提出预训练嵌入距离（PED），直接利用预训练分子模型的嵌入向量计算分子间距离，作为相似性度量指标，无需针对特定任务进行额外训练。

Result: 实验结果表明PED与传统相似性度量具有显著相关性，在虚拟筛选的分子排序任务和基于奖励设计的分子生成任务中均表现有效，证明预训练嵌入能够捕获丰富的结构信息。

Conclusion: 预训练分子嵌入捕获了丰富的结构信息，可作为现代AI药物发现中一种有前景且可扩展的相似性度量方法。

Abstract: Molecular similarity plays a central role in ligand-based drug discovery, such as virtual screening, analog searching, and goal-directed molecular generation. However, traditional similarity measures, ranging from fingerprint-based Tanimoto coefficients to 3D shape overlays, are often computationally expensive at scale or rely on hand-crafted molecular descriptors. Meanwhile, many deep learning approaches to similarity-aware design still depend on similarity-specific supervision or costly data curation, limiting their generality across targets. In this work, we propose pretrained embedding distance (PED) as an effective alternative, computed directly from pretrained molecular models without task-specific training. Experimental results show that PED exhibits distinct correlations with traditional similarity metrics, and performs effectively in both ranking molecules for virtual screening and guiding molecular generation via reward design. These findings suggest that pretrained molecular embeddings capture rich structural information and can serve as a promising and scalable similarity measurement for modern AI-aided drug discovery.

</details>


### [184] [SceneSelect: Selective Learning for Trajectory Scene Classification and Expert Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24514)
*Xinrun Wang,Deshun Xia,Ke Xu,Weijie Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SceneSelect框架，通过场景感知的选择性学习范式，动态将输入路由至最合适的专家模型，在三个公开数据集上平均提升10.5%的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹预测方法采用单一统一模型处理所有场景，但现实环境中存在极高的场景异质性（运动速度、空间密度和交互模式的严重差异），导致严重的泛化差距、精度下降和计算浪费。这种以模型为中心的范式在应对极端异质性时存在根本性缺陷。

Method: 提出以场景为中心的选择性学习范式和SceneSelect实现：(1)利用可解释的几何和运动特征进行无监督聚类以发现潜在场景分类体系；(2)训练高度解耦的分类模块将实时输入分配到场景类别；(3)通过可扩展的即插即用调度策略将轨迹序列分发到最优专家预测器。解耦设计确保无需昂贵的联合重训练即可实现良好泛化和模型集成。

Result: 在三个公开基准数据集（ETH-UCY、SDD和NBA）上的大量实验表明，该方法始终优于强单模型和集成基线，平均提升10.5%，验证了场景感知选择性学习的有效性。

Conclusion: 场景感知的选择性学习范式有效克服了传统模型中心方法的局限性，通过显式分析场景特征并动态路由至专家模型，显著提升了轨迹预测的准确性和泛化能力，为处理高场景异质性提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate trajectory prediction is fundamentally challenging due to high scene heterogeneity - the severe variance in motion velocity, spatial density, and interaction patterns across different real-world environments. However, most existing approaches typically train a single unified model, expecting a fixed-capacity architecture to generalize universally across all possible scenarios. This conventional model-centric paradigm is fundamentally flawed when confronting such extreme heterogeneity, inevitably leading to a severe generalization gap, degraded accuracy, and massive computational waste. To overcome this bottleneck, rather than refining restricted model-centric architectures, we propose selective learning, a novel scene-centric paradigm. It explicitly analyzes the characteristics of the underlying scene to dynamically route inputs to the most appropriate expert models. As a concrete implementation of this paradigm, we introduce SceneSelect. Specifically, SceneSelect utilizes unsupervised clustering on interpretable geometric and kinematic features to discover a latent scene taxonomy. A highly decoupled classification module is then trained to assign real-time inputs to these scene categories, and a highly extensible, plug-and-play scheduling policy automatically dispatches the trajectory sequence to the optimal expert predictor. Crucially, this decoupled design ensures excellent generalization capabilities, allowing seamless integration with different off-the-shelf models and robust adaptation across new datasets without requiring computationally expensive joint retraining. Extensive experiments on three public benchmarks (ETH-UCY, SDD, and NBA) demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms strong single-model and ensemble baselines, achieving an average improvement of 10.5%, showcasing the effectiveness of scene-aware selective learning.

</details>


### [185] [Prior-Agnostic Robust Forecast Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24517)
*Zhi Chen,Cheng Peng,Wei Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个简洁的闭式对数几率聚合器，通过在对数几率空间中线性组合预测，在未知状态空间的鲁棒预测聚合中取得了（近）紧的最小最大化遗憾保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注已知二元状态空间（状态为0或1）的设置。本文研究先验不可知的鲁棒预测聚合，聚合器仅观察专家的报告，但对潜在的联合信息结构、完整先验以及潜在状态空间均未知。与固定二元状态空间{0, 1}的标准模型不同，本文允许（二元）未知状态值为[0, 1]中的任意数字。

Method: 提出了一个简单、显式、闭式的对数几率聚合器（log-odds aggregator），该方法在对数几率空间中线性组合预测。研究涵盖了三种知识机制：条件独立信号、Blackwell有序结构以及一般信息结构。同时，扩展至聚合器额外知晓每位专家边际预测分布的模型。

Result: 在条件独立信号下，未知状态空间使鲁棒聚合比已知状态环境更难（更大的下界），聚合器实现0.0255的最坏情况遗憾。在已知状态空间{0,1}的经典设置中，遗憾严格低于0.0226，这是首个实现该遗憾上界的显式闭式聚合器。在聚合器知晓边际预测分布的扩展模型中，广义对数几率规则实现0.0228的遗憾，下界为0.0225。

Conclusion: 本文首次提供了一个闭式对数几率聚合器，在多种信息结构下达到了（近）紧的最小最大化遗憾保证，在已知与未知状态空间设置下均改进或匹配了现有结果。

Abstract: Robust forecast aggregation combines the predictions of multiple information sources to perform well in the worst case across all possible information structures. Previous work largely focuses on settings with a known binary state space, where the state is either 0 or 1. We study prior-agnostic robust forecast aggregation in which the aggregator observes only experts' reports, yet is ignorant of both the underlying joint information structure and the full prior, including the underlying state space. Unlike the standard model that fixes the binary state space {0, 1}, we allow the (binary) unknown state values to be arbitrary numbers in [0, 1], so the same reported probability may correspond to very different realized outcome frequencies across environments.
  Our main contribution is a simple, explicit, closed-form log-odds aggregator that linearly pools forecasts in logit space, together with (nearly-)tight minimax-regret guarantees across three knowledge regimes. We first show that under conditionally independent (CI) signals, robust aggregation with an unknown state space is strictly harder than in the known-state setting by establishing a larger lower bound, and our aggregation rule can achieve a worst-case regret of 0.0255. Along the way, we also characterize tight regret bounds for Blackwell-ordered structures and for general information structures. In the classical setting with known state space {0,1}, our aggregator achieves regret strictly below 0.0226 for CI structures. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first explicit closed-form aggregator that achieves a regret upper bound strictly less than 0.0226. Finally, we extend the model where the aggregator additionally knows each expert's marginal forecast distribution; in this setting, with the CI structures, we show that a generalized log-odds rule achieves regret of 0.0228, complementing with a lower bound of 0.0225.

</details>


### [186] [A Reward-Free Viewpoint on Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24532)
*Ying-Tu Chen,Wei Hung,Bing-Shu Wu,Zhang-Wei Hong,Ping-Chun Hsieh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将无奖励强化学习（RFRL）系统性地适配到多目标强化学习（MORL）中，通过将RFRL训练目标作为辅助任务并提出偏好引导的探索策略，在MO-Gymnasium任务上显著优于现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多目标强化学习通常训练一个以偏好加权奖励为条件的单一策略网络，但缺乏对未知用户偏好的有效适应能力。无奖励强化学习（RFRL）能够为任意可能的奖励函数学习最优策略，这与MORL处理未知偏好的挑战天然契合，但目前两者独立发展，尚未被结合研究。

Method: 将最先进的RFRL算法适配到MORL设置中，把RFRL的训练目标作为辅助任务引入MORL框架，使得知识共享能力超越训练时给定的多目标奖励函数范围；同时引入偏好引导的探索策略，使学习过程聚焦于环境中与用户偏好相关的部分。

Result: 在多个MO-Gymnasium任务上进行了广泛的实验和消融研究，所提方法在性能和数据效率方面显著优于现有最先进的MORL方法。

Conclusion: 这是首个系统性地将RFRL适配到MORL的工作，证明了RFRL作为多目标策略学习的可扩展且实证有效的解决方案的潜力。

Abstract: Many sequential decision-making tasks involve optimizing multiple conflicting objectives, requiring policies that adapt to different user preferences. In multi-objective reinforcement learning (MORL), one widely studied approach} addresses this by training a single policy network conditioned on preference-weighted rewards. In this paper, we explore a novel algorithmic perspective: leveraging reward-free reinforcement learning (RFRL) for MORL. While RFRL has historically been studied independently of MORL, it learns optimal policies for any possible reward function, making it a natural fit for MORL's challenge of handling unknown user preferences. We propose using the RFRL's training objective as an auxiliary task to enhance MORL, enabling more effective knowledge sharing beyond the multi-objective reward function given at training time. To this end, we adapt a state-of-the-art RFRL algorithm to the MORL setting and introduce a preference-guided exploration strategy that focuses learning on relevant parts of the environment. Through extensive experiments and ablation studies, we demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms the state-of-the-art MORL methods across diverse MO-Gymnasium tasks, achieving superior performance and data efficiency. This work provides the first systematic adaptation of RFRL to MORL, demonstrating its potential as a scalable and empirically effective solution to multi-objective policy learning.

</details>


### [187] [Stochastic simultaneous optimistic optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24537)
*Michal Valko,Alexandra Carpentier,Rémi Munos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了StoSOO算法，用于在未知局部平滑性的半度量下解决噪声观测函数的全局最大化问题，性能媲美最优特定调参算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对噪声环境下的函数全局最大化问题，现有基于一般空间bandit的方法通常假设已知函数的局部平滑性（以半度量形式描述）。本文的动机是放宽这一强假设，研究在未知局部平滑性信息的情况下，如何设计有效的优化算法。

Method: 提出了StoSOO（Stochastic Simultaneous Optimistic Optimization）算法。该算法采用乐观策略（optimistic strategy），通过迭代构建函数域分层划分（hierarchical partitions）上的置信上界（upper confidence bounds），来决定下一个采样点。其核心在于不需要预先知道描述局部平滑性的半度量信息。

Result: 对StoSOO算法进行了有限时间分析（finite-time analysis）。结果显示，即使在函数局部平滑性未知的情况下，该算法的性能表现几乎与那些经过特定调参、拥有平滑性先验知识的最优算法一样好。

Conclusion: StoSOO算法成功解决了未知局部平滑性条件下的随机函数全局优化问题，证明了在缺乏先验度量知识时，通过乐观优化策略仍可实现高效能，具有重要的理论和实际意义。

Abstract: We study the problem of global maximization of a function f given a finite number of evaluations perturbed by noise. We consider a very weak assumption on the function, namely that it is locally smooth (in some precise sense) with respect to some semi-metric, around one of its global maxima. Compared to previous works on bandits in general spaces (Kleinberg et al., 2008; Bubeck et al., 2011a) our algorithm does not require the knowledge of this semi-metric. Our algorithm, StoSOO, follows an optimistic strategy to iteratively construct upper confidence bounds over the hierarchical partitions of the function domain to decide which point to sample next. A finite-time analysis of StoSOO shows that it performs almost as well as the best specifically-tuned algorithms even though the local smoothness of the function is not known.

</details>


### [188] [Dialysis Risk Prediction and Treatment Effect Estimation for AKI patients using Longitudinal Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24547)
*Kalyani P. Pande,Evan Yang,Bryan Zhu,Sandeep K. Mallipattu,Alisa Yurovsky,Tengfei Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种基于Transformer的因果多头模型，利用电子健康记录（EHR）数据评估药物暴露对透析或终末期肾病（ESRD）风险的影响。模型在预测任务上取得了AUC 0.694的性能，因果分析显示ACE/ARB类药物具有部分保护作用，而袢利尿剂则显示出恶化风险的信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进展为透析或终末期肾病（ESRD）虽然罕见，但具有重要的临床意义。临床医生亟需证据来了解药物暴露如何影响患者的远期风险，以指导临床用药决策。

Method: 构建了一个固定时间窗口的EHR队列（90天观察期，730天预测期，N=81401），使用Transformer架构的因果多头模型，结合诊断、手术、用药序列及肾脏实验室检查趋势（肌酐、BUN、eGFR）。模型通过反事实暴露移除和插入，在完整用药历史设置下估计药物和成分层面的平均处理效应（ATEs）。事后因果分析采用了IPTW、AIPW、朴素法和协变量调整OLS方法。

Result: 在测试集上，模型预测性能达到AUC 0.694，PR-AUC 0.094。在选定决策阈值（0.883）下，F1分数为0.201，Brier分数为0.018。事后因果分析显示：ACE/ARB药物暴露具有部分保护方向的证据，而袢利尿剂则显示出恶化方向的信号。

Conclusion: 该研究成功验证了利用Transformer模型和因果推断方法从EHR数据中量化药物对肾脏预后风险的可行性，为临床理解特定药物类别（如ACE/ARB和袢利尿剂）对肾功能进展的影响提供了实证支持。

Abstract: Progression to dialysis or end-stage renal disease is a rare but clinically important outcome. Clinicians need evidence on how medication exposures influence downstream risk. We constructed a fixed-window EHR cohort (90-day observation, 730-day prediction; N=81401; dialysis/ESRD prevalence: 1.1%) and modeled sequences of diagnoses, procedures, and medications with kidney laboratory trends (creatinine, BUN, eGFR). A transformer-based causal multi-head model was trained to estimate drug- and ingredient-level average treatment effects (ATEs) using counterfactual exposure removal and insertion under a full medication history setup. On test set, predictive performance reached an AUC of 0.694 and PR-AUC of 0.094. At the selected decision threshold (0.883), the model achieved an F1 score of 0.201 with a Brier score of 0.018. Post-hoc causal analyses of lab changes (eGFR, creatinine, BUN) using IPTW, AIPW, naive, and covariate-adjusted OLS methods assessed clinical directionality. Results showed partial protective-direction support for ACE/ARB exposures and worsening-direction signals for loop diuretics.

</details>


### [189] [GradMAP: Gradient-Based Multi-Agent Proximal Learning for Grid-Edge Flexibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24549)
*Yihong Zhou,Hongtai Zeng,Thomas Morstyn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GradMAP算法，通过在训练中嵌入三相交流潮流模型并利用隐式微分传播约束违规，实现了大规模电网边缘设备的快速、完全去中心化控制策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协调大规模电网边缘设备需要一种在部署时完全去中心化且能遵守三相交流配电网物理约束的学习方法。现有方法难以在保持去中心化决策的同时有效处理复杂的网络约束。

Method: GradMAP采用基于梯度的多智能体近端学习框架：1）为每个智能体训练独立的神经网络策略，无参数共享；2）在线决策仅依赖局部观测，无需通信；3）离线训练时，将可微分三相交流潮流模型嵌入原始-对偶学习循环，利用隐式微分传播精确的网络约束违规信息；4）在动作空间定义信任区域，通过近端代理重用昂贵的环境梯度以加速训练。

Result: 在IEEE 123节点馈线上测试1000个智能体（电池、热泵、可控发电机）：1）在单张RTX PRO 5000 GPU上15分钟内完成训练，比基于梯度的自监督学习基准快3-5倍，训练效率显著优于多智能体强化学习基准；2）样本外测试中实现了最低的运行成本和约束违规水平之一。

Conclusion: GradMAP成功解决了大规模电网边缘设备协调中的去中心化部署与网络物理约束遵从的双重挑战，为配电网分布式资源管理提供了一种高效、实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Coordinating large populations of grid-edge devices requires learning methods that remain fully decentralised in deployment while still respecting three-phase AC distribution-network physics. This paper proposes gradient-based multi-agent proximal learning (GradMAP) to address this challenge. GradMAP trains independent neural-network policies for each agent without any parameter sharing, and each agent uses only its own local observation for online decision-making without communication. During offline training, GradMAP embeds a differentiable three-phase AC power-flow model in a primal-dual learning loop and uses implicit differentiation to propagate exact network-constraint violations to update the policy parameters. To speed up training, GradMAP reuses expensive environment gradients through a proximal surrogate within a trust region defined in the more direct policy-output (action) space, instead of the probability distribution space used in other works, such as PPO. In case studies with 1,000 agents managing batteries, heat pumps, and controllable generators on the IEEE 123-bus feeder, GradMAP learns decentralised policies that minimise three-phase AC load-flow constraint violations within 15 minutes of training on a single workstation-class NVIDIA RTX PRO 5000 Blackwell 48GB GPU. This is a 3--5x training speed-up over gradient-based self-supervised learning benchmarks and substantially better training efficiency than multi-agent reinforcement-learning benchmarks. In out-of-sample tests, GradMAP also delivers among the lowest operating cost and constraint violations.

</details>


### [190] [Efficient learning by implicit exploration in bandit problems with side observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24555)
*Tomas Kocak,Gergely Neu,Michal Valko,Remi Munos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对介于全信息和强盗反馈之间的部分可观测在线学习问题，提出了基于“隐式探索”策略的首个不需要预先知道观测系统即可实现近似最优遗憾界的算法，并扩展至在线组合优化场景，兼顾了计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线学习研究多集中于全信息反馈或强盗反馈两种极端情况，而现实中常存在介于两者之间的部分可观测场景（如学习者可观测到自身及其他部分动作的损失）。针对这一信息反馈缺口，本文旨在设计无需预先知晓观测系统即可达到最优性能的学习算法。

Method: 作者提出了“隐式探索” 这一新型探索策略。针对第一种部分可观测设置，设计了无需预知观测系统即可达到近似最优遗憾界的算法；针对在线组合优化问题中介于半强盗和全反馈之间的设置，提出了另一种始终具有计算效率的算法，其调参机制略复杂但性质相似。

Result: 所提出的两种算法均能提供近似最优的遗憾保证，且不需要在选择动作前知晓环境的观测系统。理论分析表明，隐式探索策略在计算效率和信息论效率上均优于该问题先前研究的探索策略。

Conclusion: 本文成功解决了部分可观测在线学习领域中无需预知观测系统的算法设计难题，所提出的隐式探索策略不仅理论性质优越，也具有实际计算可行性，为介于全信息和强盗反馈之间的中间反馈场景提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: We consider online learning problems under a partial observability model capturing situations where the information conveyed to the learner is between full information and bandit feedback. In the simplest variant, we assume that in addition to its own loss, the learner also gets to observe losses of some other actions. The revealed losses depend on the learner's action and a directed observation system chosen by the environment. For this setting, we propose the first algorithm that enjoys near-optimal regret guarantees without having to know the observation system before selecting its actions. Along similar lines, we also define a new partial information setting that models online combinatorial optimization problems where the feedback received by the learner is between semi-bandit and full feedback. As the predictions of our first algorithm cannot be always computed efficiently in this setting, we propose another algorithm with similar properties and with the benefit of always being computationally efficient, at the price of a slightly more complicated tuning mechanism. Both algorithms rely on a novel exploration strategy called implicit exploration, which is shown to be more efficient both computationally and information-theoretically than previously studied exploration strategies for the problem.

</details>


### [191] [Fraud Detection in Cryptocurrency Markets with Spatio-Temporal Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24590)
*Lidia Losavio,Luca Persia,Madan Sathe,Dimosthenis Pasadakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于图神经网络（GNN）的加密货币市场操纵检测方法，通过构建代币关系图并结合时空GNN架构，在真实数据集上显著提升了“拉高出货”欺诈检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的欺诈检测机制通常基于机器学习方法，将每个金融资产（代币）及其交易视为独立样本进行处理。然而，市场操纵策略很少是孤立事件，而是表现为相关资产之间的协调、重复和频繁转移。这种关系结构是信号的重要组成部分，可以通过图形方式有效表示，现有方法忽视了这一关键特征。

Method: 提出了三种基于聚合小时市场数据的图构建方法，利用统一的时空图神经网络（GNN）架构进行处理。该架构结合了基于注意力的空间聚合机制和时序Transformer编码，以同时捕获资产间的关系结构和时间动态特征。

Result: 在跨越三年多的加密货币“拉高出货”（pump-and-dump）真实数据集上进行了评估。对比结果显示，基于图的模型在检测异常事件方面显著优于标准机器学习基线模型，证明了学习市场连接性对检测协调市场操纵方案具有重要作用。

Conclusion: 研究表明，学习市场连接性（关系结构）能为检测协调性市场操纵方案带来显著收益，基于图的方法在金融欺诈检测领域具有优越性和应用潜力。

Abstract: Technological advancements in cryptocurrency markets have increased accessibility for investors, but concurrently exposed them to the risks of market manipulations. Existing fraud detection mechanisms typically rely on machine learning methods that treat each financial asset (i.e., token) and its related transactions independently. However, market manipulation strategies are rarely isolated events, but are rather characterized by coordination, repetition, and frequent transfers among related assets. This suggests that relational structure constitutes an integral component of the signal and can be effectively represented through graphical means. In this paper, we propose three graph construction methods that rely on aggregated hourly market data. The proposed graphs are processed by a unified spatio-temporal Graph Neural Network (GNN) architecture that combines attention-based spatial aggregation with temporal Transformer encoding. We evaluate our methodology on a real-world dataset comprised of pump-and-dump schemes in cryptocurrency markets, spanning a period of over three years. Our comparative results showcase that our graph-based models achieve significant improvements over standard machine learning baselines in detecting anomalous events. Our work highlights that learned market connectivity provides substantial gains for detecting coordinated market manipulation schemes.

</details>


### [192] [Uncovering Latent Patterns in Social Media Usage and Mental Health: A Clustering-Based Approach Using Unsupervised Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24611)
*Md All Shahria,Sanjeda Dewan Mithila,Touhid Alam,Mohammad Sakib Mahmood,Mahfuza Khatun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用无监督机器学习（K-Means聚类）对551名参与者的社交媒体使用与心理健康数据进行分群，识别出6种具有不同风险特征的用户画像，揭示了使用时长与焦虑等症状的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体的普及引发了对心理健康影响的广泛关注。尽管现有研究多关注相关性分析，但缺乏利用无监督机器学习基于行为和心理模式对用户进行细分的尝试，导致难以识别不同群体间的差异化风险特征。本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 收集551名参与者的在线调查数据，采用KNN插补处理缺失值，对分类变量（如性别）进行独热编码，并通过IQR和Z-score方法进行异常值检测。使用K-Means算法进行聚类，通过肘部法则和轮廓系数（0.32）确定最优聚类数为6，并结合PCA进行降维可视化。

Result: 成功构建了6个用户聚类群体，揭示了隐藏的行为与心理模式。相关性热力图显示社交媒体使用时长与焦虑之间存在0.28的相关性。

Conclusion: 研究证明了利用无监督机器学习对社交媒体用户进行细分的有效性，能够揭示不同群体的心理健康风险特征，为后续针对性干预提供了依据。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of social media has heightened interest in its psychological effects, particularly on mental health indicators such as anxiety, depression, loneliness, and sleep quality, as these platforms increasingly influence social interactions and well-being. Although previous research has examined correlations between social media use and mental health, few studies have utilized unsupervised machine learning to segment users based on behavioral and psychological patterns, leaving a gap in identifying distinct risk profiles across diverse groups. This study seeks to address this by segmenting individuals according to their social media usage and psychological well-being, employing clustering to reveal hidden patterns and evaluate their mental health implications. Data from 551 participants, collected via an online survey, were preprocessed using KNN imputation for missing values, one-hot encoding for categorical variables like Gender with 5 unique values, and outlier detection via IQR and Z-score methods. K-Means clustering, optimized at 6 clusters using the Elbow Method and a Silhouette Score of 0.32, was applied, with PCA reducing 22 dimensions for visualization and a correlation heatmap highlighting relationships, such as a 0.28 correlation between social media hours and anxiety.

</details>


### [193] [Cortex-Inspired Continual Learning: Unsupervised Instantiation and Recovery of Functional Task Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24637)
*Kevin McKee,Thomas Hazy,Yicong Zheng,Zacharie Bugaud,Thomas Miconi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出功能任务网络（FTN），一种受哺乳动物新皮层启发的参数隔离方法，通过三维掩码生成程序（梯度下降→空间平滑→K-胜者通吃二值化）实现块序列持续学习中的零遗忘和无监督任务推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块序列持续学习要求单一模型同时解决两个核心矛盾：防止灾难性遗忘，以及在无任务标签的推理阶段识别当前输入对应的先验解决方案。现有方法难以兼顾结构化遗忘防护与高效任务推断。

Method: 提出功能任务网络（FTN）：1）构建高维自组织二值掩码，管理大规模小深度网络群体（模拟锥体神经元树突）；2）三阶段掩码生成：梯度下降识别任务相关神经元→平滑核促进空间连续性→K-胜者通吃二值化（固定容量预算）；3）空间组织机制将组合优化问题O(C(H,K))降维为近线性扫描O(H)，实现并行化梯度更新。

Result: 在三个持续学习基准上验证：合成多任务分类/回归生成器、标签打乱MNIST（概念漂移）、置换MNIST（域漂移）。细粒度平滑版本（FTN-Slow）实现接近零遗忘；大核快速版本（FTN-Fast）以少量遗忘换取速度提升。空间机制使掩码搜索复杂度从组合优化降至近线性扫描。

Conclusion: FTN通过仿生学结构设计实现了理论保证与实践效能的统一：不相交掩码确保梯度更新隔离，提供抗灾难性遗忘的结构化保障；单步梯度恢复历史任务子网络，实现无监督任务分割。该方法为持续学习提供了可并行化、高效率的参数隔离新范式。

Abstract: Block-sequential continual learning demands that a single model both protect prior solutions from catastrophic forgetting and efficiently infer at inference time which prior solution matches the current input without task labels. We present Functional Task Networks (FTN), a parameter-isolation method inspired by structural and dynamical motifs found in the mammalian neocortex. Similar to mixture-of-experts, this method uses a high dimensional, self-organizing binary mask over a large population of small but deep networks, inspired by dendritic models of pyramidal neurons. The mask is produced by a three-stage procedure: (1) gradient descent on a continuous mask identifies task-relevant neurons, (2) a smoothing kernel biases the result toward spatial contiguity, (3) and k-winner-take-all binarizes the resulting group at a fixed capacity budget. Like mixture-of-experts, each neuron is an independent deep network, so disjoint masks give exactly disjoint gradient updates, providing structural guarantees against catastrophic forgetting. This three-stage procedure recovers the sub-network of a previously-trained task in a single gradient step, providing unsupervised task segmentation at inference time. We test it on three continual-learning benchmarks: (1) a synthetic multi-task classification/regression generator, (2) MNIST with shuffled class labels (pure concept shift), and (3) Permuted MNIST (domain shift). On all three, FTN with fine grained smoothing (FTN-Slow) results in nearly zero forgetting. FTN with a large kernel and only 2 iterations of smoothing (FTN-Fast) trades off some retention for increased speed. We show that the spatial organization mechanism reduces the effective mask search from the combinatorial top-k subset problem in O(C(H,K)) to the complexity of a near-linear scan in O(H) over compact cortical neighborhoods, which is parallelized by the gradient-based update.

</details>


### [194] [The Last Human-Written Paper: Agent-Native Research Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24658)
*Jiachen Liu,Jiaxin Pei,Jintao Huang,Chenglei Si,Ao Qu,Xiangru Tang,Runyu Lu,Lichang Chen,Xiaoyan Bai,Haizhong Zheng,Carl Chen,Zhiyang Chen,Haojie Ye,Yujuan Fu,Zexue He,Zijian Jin,Zhenyu Zhang,Shangquan Sun,Maestro Harmon,John Dianzhuo Wang,Jianqiao Zeng,Jiachen Sun,Mingyuan Wu,Baoyu Zhou,Yuchen You,Shijian Lu,Yiming Qiu,Fan Lai,Yuan Yuan,Yao Li,Junyuan Hong,Ruihao Zhu,Beidi Chen,Alex Pentland,Ang Chen,Mosharaf Chowdhury,Zechen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent-Native Research Artifact (Ara)协议，用机器可执行的研究包替代传统线性叙事论文，显著提升AI代理理解、复现和扩展研究工作的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统科学出版将分支迭代的研究过程压缩为线性叙事，丢弃了大量研究发现，产生了两种结构性成本：故事税（丢弃失败实验和分支探索）和工程税（论文描述不足以支持智能体完整实现）。这对人类读者尚可接受，但对需要理解和复现工作的AI代理而言成为关键障碍。

Method: 提出Ara协议，包含四层结构：科学逻辑、完整规范的可执行代码、保留失败路径的探索图、以及原始输出证据。构建三个支持机制：Live Research Manager（捕获开发过程中的决策和死路）、Ara Compiler（将传统PDF和代码库转换为Ara格式）、Ara原生评审系统（自动化客观检查）。

Result: 在PaperBench和RE-Bench测试中，Ara将问答准确率从72.4%提升至93.7%，复现成功率从57.4%提升至64.4%。在五个开放式扩展任务中，保留的失败轨迹加速了进展，但也可能限制高能力智能体突破既有框架。

Conclusion: Ara协议通过保存完整的研究过程和实现细节，有效解决了传统论文对AI代理的结构性障碍，大幅提升了研究工作的可理解性和可复现性，为AI驱动的科学研究提供了新的出版范式。

Abstract: Scientific publication compresses a branching, iterative research process into a linear narrative, discarding the majority of what was discovered along the way. This compilation imposes two structural costs: a Storytelling Tax, where failed experiments, rejected hypotheses, and the branching exploration process are discarded to fit a linear narrative; and an Engineering Tax, where the gap between reviewer-sufficient prose and agent-sufficient specification leaves critical implementation details unwritten. Tolerable for human readers, these costs become critical when AI agents must understand, reproduce, and extend published work. We introduce the Agent-Native Research Artifact (Ara), a protocol that replaces the narrative paper with a machine-executable research package structured around four layers: scientific logic, executable code with full specifications, an exploration graph that preserves the failures compilation discards, and evidence grounding every claim in raw outputs. Three mechanisms support the ecosystem: a Live Research Manager that captures decisions and dead ends during ordinary development; an Ara Compiler that translates legacy PDFs and repos into Aras; and an Ara-native review system that automates objective checks so human reviewers can focus on significance, novelty, and taste. On PaperBench and RE-Bench, Ara raises question-answering accuracy from 72.4% to 93.7% and reproduction success from 57.4% to 64.4%. On RE-Bench's five open-ended extension tasks, preserved failure traces in Ara accelerate progress, but can also constrain a capable agent from stepping outside the prior-run box depending on the agent's capabilities.

</details>


### [195] [A Functorial Formulation of Neighborhood Aggregating Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24672)
*Sun Woo Park,Yun Young Choi,U Jin Choi,Youngho Woo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用连续函数集的预层和共预层结构，对卷积/消息传递神经网络进行数学解释，并通过层论中的阻碍条件揭示其经验局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为卷积神经网络（或消息传递神经网络）提供严格的数学理论基础，解释其在实践中表现出的局限性。

Method: 利用拓扑空间上连续函数集的预层（presheaves）和共预层（copresheaves）结构对网络进行数学建模；分析这些结构无法成为层（sheaves）或共层（cosheaves）的阻碍条件。

Result: 提出了一种理论启发式方法，通过层论中的阻碍条件成功阐释了卷积神经网络和消息传递神经网络在实际应用中表现出的若干局限性。

Conclusion: 通过代数拓扑和层论视角，为理解神经网络的结构约束和性能瓶颈提供了新的理论框架和解释工具。

Abstract: We provide a mathematical interpretation of convolutional (or message passing) neural networks by using presheaves and copresheaves of the set of continuous functions over a topological space. Based on this interpretation, we formulate a theoretical heuristic which elaborates a number of empirical limitations of these neural networks by using obstructions on such sets of continuous functions over a topological space to be sheaves or copresheaves.

</details>


### [196] [Diffusion-Guided Feature Selection via Nishimori Temperature: Noise-Based Spectral Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24692)
*Vasiliy S. Usatyuk,Denis A. Sapozhnikov,Sergey I. Egorov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于噪声的谱嵌入(NBSE)框架，用于高维数据的无贪婪搜索特征选择，在保留30%特征时精度损失<1%，优于ANOVA F检验和随机选择最多6.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对高维数据特征选择中贪婪搜索效率低和对噪声敏感的问题，需要一种物理启发式、无需贪婪搜索且对噪声鲁棒的特征选择方法。

Method: 构建样本稀疏相似图，识别Bethe Hessian奇异的Nishimori温度β_N，利用最小特征向量捕获度校正扩散过程主导模式；通过转置数据矩阵在特征空间应用NBSE，获得一维谱嵌入揭示冗余或语义相关维度组，通过平衡分箱选择每组的代表性特征。

Result: 理论证明有色高斯扰动对β_N的偏移至多为O(σ̄²)，保证对测量噪声的鲁棒性；在MobileNetV2和EfficientNet-B4的ImageNet嵌入实验中，在仅保留30%特征时精度下降低于1%，相比ANOVA F检验和随机选择精度提升高达6.8%。

Conclusion: NBSE提供了一种有效的物理启发式特征选择方法，无需贪婪搜索即可识别信息特征，对噪声具有理论保证的鲁棒性，在实际应用中实现了高效的特征压缩且性能优异。

Abstract: We propose Noise-Based Spectral Embedding (NBSE), a physics-informed framework for selecting informative features from high-dimensional data without greedy search. NBSE constructs a sparse similarity graph on the samples and identifies the Nishimori temperature $β_N$ the critical inverse temperature at which the Bethe Hessian becomes singular. The corresponding smallest eigenvector captures the dominant mode of an intrinsically degree-corrected diffusion process, naturally reweighting nodes to prevent hub dominance. By transposing the data matrix and applying NBSE in feature space, we obtain a one-dimensional spectral embedding that reveals groups of redundant or semantically related dimensions; balanced binning then selects one representative per group. We prove that coloured Gaussian perturbations shift $β_N$ by at most $O(\barσ^2)$, guaranteeing robustness to measurement noise. Experiments on ImageNet embeddings from MobileNetV2 and EfficientNet-B4 show that NBSE preserves classification accuracy even under aggressive compression: on EfficientNet-B4 the accuracy drop is below $1\%$ when retaining only $30\%$ of features, outperforming ANOVA $F$-test and random selection by up to $6.8\%$.

</details>


### [197] [Scalable Hyperparameter-Divergent Ensemble Training with Automatic Learning Rate Exploration for Large Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24708)
*Hailing Cheng,Tao Huang,Chen Zhu,Antonio Alonso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HDET方法，将数据并行训练中的GPU副本重新用于同时探索学习率等超参数，在不增加通信开销和训练预算的情况下自动优化超参数配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据并行随机梯度下降训练中，N个GPU副本计算几乎相同的更新，导致训练过程中学习率配置空间完全未被探索，存在资源利用效率低和超参数调优成本高的问题。

Method: 提出超参数发散集成训练（HDET），采用交替的两阶段操作：fan-out阶段各副本在结构化、对称的学习率分布下独立训练；converge阶段每隔T步通过AllReduce对参数进行平均。在此基础上设计自动学习率控制器，将副本间的相对训练损失作为性能信号，通过基于动量的无梯度元更新来调整共享基础调度。该框架还可推广到dropout率、注意力缩放温度、权重衰减系数等标量超参数。

Result: HDET作为一个即插即用的组件（可替代PyTorch的OneCycleLR调度器），无需修改模型架构、优化器或数据管道，即可生成自适应的学习率调度，在优化质量和泛化能力上均有提升。

Conclusion: HDET成功将数据并行训练中的冗余计算转化为超参数探索优势，实现了零阶超梯度的在线超参数优化，为大规模神经网络训练提供了一种高效、通用的自动化超参数调优方案。

Abstract: Training large neural networks with data-parallel stochastic gradient descent allocates N GPU replicas to compute effectively identical updates -- a practice that leaves the rich space of learning rate configurations entirely unexplored during training. We propose Hyperparameter-Divergent Ensemble Training (HDET), a method that repurposes these replicas for simultaneous learning rate exploration at negligible communication overhead. HDET operates in alternating phases: a fan-out stage in which replicas train independently under a structured, symmetric spread of learning rates, and a converge stage in which parameters are averaged across all replicas via AllReduce every T steps. Building on this ensemble substrate, we further propose an automatic learning rate (auto-LR) controller that treats the relative training loss across replicas as a performance signal, updating the shared base schedule toward higher-performing configurations via a momentum-based gradient-free meta-update. The combined method produces a self-adapting learning rate schedule that improves both optimization quality and generalization without additional hyperparameter sweeps or training budget.
  Crucially, the framework generalizes beyond learning rate: any scalar hyperparameter that does not alter model architecture -- such as dropout rate, attention scale temperature, or weight-decay coefficient -- can be explored across replicas using the same fan-out/converge protocol, with inter-replica loss differences serving as zero-order hypergradients that guide the search direction. HDET is implemented as a drop-in replacement for PyTorch's OneCycleLR scheduler, requiring no changes to model architecture, optimizer, or data pipeline.

</details>


### [198] [SpecRLBench: A Benchmark for Generalization in Specification-Guided Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24729)
*Zijian Guo,İlker Işık,H. M. Sabbir Ahmad,Wenchao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了SpecRLBench，一个用于评估基于线性时序逻辑(LTL)的规范引导强化学习方法泛化能力的基准测试。该基准涵盖导航和操作领域，包含多难度级别、动静环境、多种机器人动力学和观测模态。通过大规模实验，揭示了现有方法的优势与局限，为开发更具泛化能力的算法提供了平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管规范引导强化学习在处理复杂时序扩展任务方面展现出潜力，但现有方法在未见过的规范和多样化环境中的泛化能力尚未得到充分理解。缺乏系统性的评估基准来衡量这些方法的泛化性能，阻碍了该领域的进一步发展。

Method: 提出SpecRLBench基准测试框架，涵盖导航和操作两个领域。基准设计包括：多个难度级别、静态和动态环境、多样化的机器人动力学模型、不同的观测模态。通过结构化的实验设计，系统性评估LTL规范引导RL方法在不同复杂度条件下的表现。

Result: 通过大规模实证评估，刻画了现有规范引导RL方法的优势与局限性。揭示了随着规范复杂度和环境复杂度增加时所出现的挑战。为不同方法提供了系统性的比较分析，识别了当前方法在泛化能力方面的关键瓶颈。

Conclusion: SpecRLBench为规范引导强化学习方法的系统比较提供了结构化平台，有助于推动更具泛化能力的规范引导RL方法的发展。该基准填补了领域内评估工具的空白，为未来研究提供了统一的标准测试环境。

Abstract: Specification-guided reinforcement learning (RL) provides a principled framework for encoding complex, temporally extended tasks using formal specifications such as linear temporal logic (LTL). While recent methods have shown promising results, their ability to generalize across unseen specifications and diverse environments remains insufficiently understood. In this work, we introduce SpecRLBench, a benchmark designed to evaluate the generalization capabilities of LTL-based specification-guided RL methods. The benchmark spans multiple difficulty levels across navigation and manipulation domains, incorporating both static and dynamic environments, diverse robot dynamics, and varied observation modalities. Through extensive empirical evaluation, we characterize the strengths and limitations of existing approaches and reveal the challenges that emerge as specification and environment complexity increase. SpecRLBench provides a structured platform for systematic comparison and supports the development of more generalizable specification-guided RL methods. Code is available at https://github.com/BU-DEPEND-Lab/SpecRLBench.

</details>


### [199] [Learning to Think from Multiple Thinkers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24737)
*Nirmit Joshi,Roey Magen,Nathan Srebro,Nikolaos Tsilivis,Gal Vardi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在被动学习设置下，来自多个思考者的CoT监督学习可能变得困难；但在主动学习设置下，存在高效算法能以少量CoT数据和适度数量的思考者实现学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究当CoT（思维链）监督数据来自多个不同思考者时的学习问题。这些思考者提供的解法虽然正确但可能系统性地不同（例如不同人解数学题的步骤，或不同程序解决同一问题的执行轨迹）。这类场景在现实中很常见，但现有理论主要关注单一思考者的情况。

Method: 一方面，作者基于密码学假设，证明在被动数据收集设置下，即使使用两个或少数几个不同思考者提供的CoT监督，学习也可能变得困难。另一方面，作者提出了一种通用的计算高效主动学习算法，该算法所需CoT数据量与目标精度ε无关，思考者数量随log(1/ε)·loglog(1/ε)增长，被动端结果数据量按(1/ε)·polylog(1/ε)增长。

Result: （1）负向结果：在密码学假设下，即使只有两个或少数思考者，被动CoT监督学习也可能变得困难；（2）正向结果：主动学习算法能高效学习，且CoT数据需求与目标精度解耦，只需少量CoT数据和适度数量的思考者即可实现。

Conclusion: 多思考者CoT监督学习在被动和主动学习设置下存在显著差异。被动学习面临本质性困难，而主动学习能够有效利用多思考者信息，为实际中多源CoT数据的有效利用提供了理论依据和算法支持。

Abstract: We study learning with Chain-of-Thought (CoT) supervision from multiple thinkers, all of whom provide correct but possibly systematically different solutions, e.g., step-by-step solutions to math problems written by different thinkers, or step-by-step execution traces of different programs solving the same problem.
  We consider classes that are computationally easy to learn using CoT supervision from a single thinker, but hard to learn with only end-result supervision, i.e., without CoT (Joshi et al. 2025). We establish that, under cryptographic assumptions, learning can be hard from CoT supervision provided by two or a few different thinkers, in passive data-collection settings.
  On the other hand, we provide a generic computationally efficient active learning algorithm that learns with a small amount of CoT data per thinker that is completely independent of the target accuracy $\varepsilon$, a moderate number of thinkers that scales as $\log \frac{1}{\varepsilon}\log \log \frac{1}{\varepsilon}$, and sufficient passive end-result data that scales as $\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\cdot poly\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon}$.

</details>


### [200] [Conflict-Aware Harmonized Rotational Gradient for Multiscale Kinetic Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24745)
*Zhangyong Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了HRGrad方法，通过参数隐藏表示串行化和梯度对齐机制，解决了多尺度时间依赖动力学问题中多任务学习的梯度冲突问题，有效克服了渐近保持神经网络(APNNs)的失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多尺度时间依赖动力学问题中存在从微观到宏观物理的渐近转变，不同渐近区域的任务求解经常出现梯度冲突，导致多任务学习失败，这是需要解决的核心挑战。

Method: 提出HRGrad方法：1) 显式编码参数的隐藏表示，将求解任务串行化以同时训练；2) 分割预测结果构建任务损失；3) 引入新型梯度对齐度量，确保最终更新与各任务特定梯度之间具有正的点积，动态调整梯度幅度。

Result: 在BK方程和线性输运方程的全Knudsen数范围内进行广泛实验，结果表明HRGrad有效克服了APNNs在此类问题中的失效模式，并在数学上证明了方法的收敛性。

Conclusion: HRGrad方法成功解决了多尺度动力学问题中多任务学习的梯度冲突挑战，为渐近保持神经网络提供了一种可靠的训练方法，具有重要的理论和实践价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a harmonized rotational gradient method, termed HRGrad, for simultaneously tackling multiscale time-dependent kinetic problems with varying small parameters.
  These parameters exhibit asymptotic transitions from microscopic to macroscopic physics, making it a challenging multi-task problem to solve over all ranges simultaneously.
  Solving tasks in different asymptotic regions often encounter gradient conflicts, which can lead to the failure of multi-task learning.
  To address this challenge, we explicitly encode a hidden representation of these parameters, ensuring that the corresponding solving tasks are serialized for simultaneous training.
  Furthermore, to mitigate gradient conflicts, we segment the prediction results to construct task losses and introduce a novel gradient alignment metric to ensure a positive dot product between the final update and each loss-specific gradient.
  This metric maintains consistent optimization rates for all task losses and dynamically adjusts gradient magnitudes based on conflict levels.
  Moreover, we provide a mathematical proof demonstrating the convergence of the HRGrad method, which is evaluated across a range of challenging asymptotic-preserving neural networks (APNNs) scenarios.
  We conduct an extensive set of experiments encompassing the Bhatnagar-Gross-Krook (BGK) equation and the linear transport equation in all ranges of Knudsen number.
  Our results indicate that HRGrad effectively overcomes the `failure modes' of APNNs in these problems.

</details>


### [201] [The Optimal Sample Complexity of Multiclass and List Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24749)
*Chirag Pabbaraju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了多类分类假设类的最大超图密度以其DS维度为上界，从而确定了多类分类和列表学习中样本复杂度对DS维度的最优依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二分类的最优样本复杂度已由VC维度很好地刻画，但多类分类的最优样本复杂度问题长期悬而未决。尽管DS维度被认为是多类分类的合适量化参数，但在样本复杂度的上下界之间长期存在√DS的差距，该领域亟需填补这一理论空白。

Method: 基于Hanneke等人（2026）提出的关于DS维度对多类假设类的新型代数刻画，作者证明了任意多类假设类的最大超图密度受其DS维度上界约束，从而验证了Daniely和Shalev-Shwartz（2014）的长期猜想。

Result: 证明了多类假设类的最大超图密度上界为DS维度，成功验证了2014年的猜想；基于此，确定了多类分类及列表学习中样本复杂度对DS维度的最优依赖关系。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了多类学习理论中的重要空白，证明了DS维度能够准确刻画多类分类的样本复杂度，为多类分类和列表学习提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: While the optimal sample complexity of binary classification in terms of the VC dimension is well-established, determining the optimal sample complexity of multiclass classification has remained open. The appropriate complexity parameter for multiclass classification is the DS dimension, and despite significant efforts, a gap of $\sqrt{\text{DS}}$ has persisted between the upper and lower bounds on sample complexity.
  Recent work by Hanneke et al. (2026) shows a novel algebraic characterization of multiclass hypothesis classes in terms of their DS dimension. Building up on this, we show that the maximum hypergraph density of any multiclass hypothesis class is upper-bounded by its DS dimension. This proves a longstanding conjecture of Daniely and Shalev-Shwartz (2014). As a consequence, we determine the optimal dependence of the sample complexity on the DS dimension for multiclass as well as list learning.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [202] [An Intelligent Fault Diagnosis Method for General Aviation Aircraft Based on Multi-Fidelity Digital Twin and FMEA Knowledge Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22777)
*Zhihuan Wei,Yang Hu,Xinhang Chen,Yiming Zhang,Jie Liu,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多保真度数字孪生的智能故障诊断框架，通过高低保真度双路径残差计算，在20类故障任务上达到96.2%的Macro-F1，同时实现4.3倍推理加速，并发现残差特征质量对诊断性能的影响约为分类器架构的5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用航空飞机故障诊断面临真实故障数据稀缺、故障类型多样、故障特征微弱三大挑战，现有方法难以兼顾诊断精度、实时性与结果的可解释性。

Method: 框架包含四个模块：基于JSBSim 6-DoF的高保真飞行动力学仿真；基于FMEA的三层故障注入引擎（涵盖19种发动机故障）；多保真度残差计算框架（高保真路径使用配对镜像残差，低保真路径使用GRU代理模型实现实时预测）；以及融合FMEA知识的LLM可解释诊断报告生成。最后使用1D-CNN进行端到端分类。

Result: 配对镜像残差方案在20类故障任务上达到96.2%的Macro-F1；GRU代理方案以仅0.6%的性能代价实现4.3倍推理加速。对24种方案的对比研究表明，残差特征质量对诊断性能的贡献约为分类器架构的5倍，确立了“残差质量优先”的设计原则。

Conclusion: 所提多保真度数字孪生框架有效解决了数据稀缺与实时性矛盾，验证了残差特征质量在故障诊断中的核心地位，为通用航空飞机故障诊断系统的设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Fault diagnosis of general aviation aircraft faces challenges including scarce real fault data, diverse fault types, and weak fault signatures. This paper proposes an intelligent fault diagnosis framework based on multi-fidelity digital twin, integrating four modules: high-fidelity flight dynamics simulation, FMEA-driven fault injection, multi-fidelity residual feature extraction, and large language model (LLM)-enhanced interpretable report generation. A digital twin is constructed using the JSBSim six-degree-of-freedom (6-DoF) flight dynamics engine, generating 23-channel engine health monitoring data via semi-empirical sensor synthesis equations. A three-layer fault injection engine based on failure mode and effects analysis (FMEA) models the physical causal propagation of 19 engine fault types. A multi-fidelity residual computation framework comprising paired-mirror residuals and GRU surrogate prediction residuals is proposed: the high-fidelity path obtains clean fault deviation signals using nominal mirror trajectories with identical initial conditions, while the low-fidelity path achieves online real-time residual computation through a multi-step prediction GRU surrogate model. A 1D-CNN classifier performs end-to-end diagnosis of 20 fault classes. An LLM diagnostic report engine enhanced with FMEA knowledge fuses classification results, residual evidence, and domain causal knowledge to generate interpretable natural language reports. Experiments show the paired-mirror residual scheme achieves a Macro-F1 of 96.2% on the 20-class task, while the GRU surrogate scheme achieves 4.3x inference acceleration at only 0.6% performance cost. Comparison across 24 schemes reveals that residual feature quality contributes approximately 5x more to diagnostic performance than classifier architecture, establishing the "residual quality first" design principle.

</details>


### [203] [PExA: Parallel Exploration Agent for Complex Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22934)
*Tanmay Parekh,Ella Hofmann-Coyle,Shuyi Wang,Sachith Sri Ram Kothur,Srivas Prasad,Yunmo Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于软件测试覆盖率的Text-to-SQL框架，通过并行执行原子测试用例来平衡延迟与性能，在Spider 2.0上达到70.2%的执行准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的Text-to-SQL智能体在延迟与性能之间存在权衡困境：提升性能往往以增加延迟为代价，反之亦然。

Method: 引入软件测试覆盖率的视角重新构建Text-to-SQL生成流程。系统为原始查询准备一套包含更简单、原子性SQL的测试用例，这些用例并行执行并共同确保对原始查询的语义覆盖。在迭代测试用例覆盖率后，仅在收集到足够信息时才生成最终SQL，并利用已探索的测试用例SQL作为生成依据。

Result: 在Text-to-SQL领域的SOTA基准测试Spider 2.0上进行了验证，取得了70.2%的执行准确率，达到了新的SOTA水平。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了LLM-based Text-to-SQL智能体的延迟-性能权衡问题，证明了将软件测试覆盖率理念引入SQL生成任务的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: LLM-based agents for text-to-SQL often struggle with latency-performance trade-off, where performance improvements come at the cost of latency or vice versa. We reformulate text-to-SQL generation within the lens of software test coverage where the original query is prepared with a suite of test cases with simpler, atomic SQLs that are executed in parallel and together ensure semantic coverage of the original query. After iterating on test case coverage, the final SQL is generated only when enough information is gathered, leveraging the explored test case SQLs to ground the final generation. We validated our framework on a state-of-the-art benchmark for text-to-SQL, Spider 2.0, achieving a new state-of-the-art with 70.2% execution accuracy.

</details>


### [204] [The Power of Power Law: Asymmetry Enables Compositional Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22951)
*Zixuan Wang,Xingyu Dang,Jason D. Lee,Kaifeng Lyu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在组合推理任务中，幂律分布训练效果优于均匀分布；幂律采样改善了损失景观，使模型能高效学习长尾技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普遍直觉认为对数据进行重加权或策划使其趋向均匀分布有助于模型学习长尾知识和技能，作者旨在挑战这一直觉，探究数据分布对组合推理任务学习效果的真实影响。

Method: 在状态跟踪、多步算术等组合推理任务上进行实验对比，并引入极简技能组合任务进行理论分析，研究幂律分布与均匀分布下的学习表现与数据需求。

Result: 在多种组合推理任务中，幂律分布训练始终优于均匀分布。理论证明幂律采样引入有益的不对称性，改善了病态的损失景观，使模型能先以低数据复杂度学习高频技能组合，进而作为跳板高效学习稀有的长尾技能，显著减少了所需训练数据量。

Conclusion: 幂律分布为模型提供了有效的学习路径，优于直觉上的均匀分布；这为训练模型的有效数据分布提供了新视角，表明保留自然数据的幂律特性可能更有利于长尾技能的学习。

Abstract: Natural language data follows a power-law distribution, with most knowledge and skills appearing at very low frequency. While a common intuition suggests that reweighting or curating data towards a uniform distribution may help models better learn these long-tail skills, we find a counterintuitive result: across a wide range of compositional reasoning tasks, such as state tracking and multi-step arithmetic, training under power-law distributions consistently outperforms training under uniform distributions. To understand this advantage, we introduce a minimalist skill-composition task and show that learning under a power-law distribution provably requires significantly less training data. Our theoretical analysis reveals that power law sampling induces a beneficial asymmetry that improves the pathological loss landscape, which enables models to first acquire high-frequency skill compositions with low data complexity, which in turn serves as a stepping stone to efficiently learn rare long-tailed skills. Our results offer an alternative perspective on what constitutes an effective data distribution for training models.

</details>


### [205] [GSAR: Typed Grounding for Hallucination Detection and Recovery in Multi-Agent LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23366)
*Federico A. Kamelhar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了GSAR框架，用于评估多智能体LLM系统中诊断报告的落地性（groundedness），并支持基于预算的分层恢复决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的落地性评估器（二元分类器、LLM标量评分、自纠正循环）将所有证据视为可互换，且仅输出单一信号，缺乏对下游行动的有原则控制，无法确保诊断报告中的声明真正基于观测证据。

Method: GSAR框架包含四个核心组件：(i) 将声明划分为四类（落地、未落地、矛盾、互补），赋予非冗余替代视角一等地位；(ii) 根据认知强度分配证据类型特定权重；(iii) 计算非对称矛盾惩罚的加权落地性分数；(iv) 将分数耦合到三层决策函数（继续、重新生成、重新规划），在显式计算预算下驱动有界迭代外循环。

Result: 在FEVER数据集上使用四个独立训练的LLM评判器（GPT-5.4、Claude-Sonnet-4-6、Claude-Opus-4-7、Gemini-2.5-Pro）验证了五个设计声明：所有消融实验在所有评判器上方向一致；bootstrap 95%置信区间在rho=0效应上均排除0；在Opus 4.7下无互补消融的CI为[-96,-68]/200；n=1000时三个独立评判器收敛至DeltaS(rho=0)=+0.058。还与Vectara HHEM-2.1-Open进行了对比。

Conclusion: GSAR是首个将证据类型评分与分层恢复机制在显式计算预算下耦合的落地性框架，并通过理论证明和跨模型实验验证了其有效性，为多智能体LLM系统的可信度评估提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous multi-agent LLM systems are increasingly deployed to investigate operational incidents and produce structured diagnostic reports. Their trustworthiness hinges on whether each claim is grounded in observed evidence rather than model-internal inference. Existing groundedness evaluators (binary classifiers, LLM-as-judge scalars, self-correction loops) treat supporting evidence as interchangeable and emit a single signal that offers no principled control over downstream action.
  We present GSAR, a grounding-evaluation and replanning framework that (i) partitions claims into a four-way typology (grounded, ungrounded, contradicted, complementary), giving first-class standing to non-redundant alternative perspectives; (ii) assigns evidence-type-specific weights reflecting epistemic strength; (iii) computes an asymmetric contradiction-penalised weighted groundedness score; and (iv) couples that score to a three-tier decision function (proceed, regenerate, replan) driving a bounded-iteration outer loop under an explicit compute budget.
  We formalise the algorithm, prove six structural properties, and evaluate five design claims on FEVER with gold Wikipedia evidence under four independently-trained LLM judges (gpt-5.4, claude-sonnet-4-6, claude-opus-4-7, gemini-2.5-pro). Every ablation reproduces in the same direction on every judge: bootstrap 95% CIs on the rho=0 effect exclude 0 on all four; the no-complementary ablation under Opus 4.7 has CI [-96,-68] of 200; at n=1000 three independent judges converge to DeltaS(rho=0)=+0.058. A head-to-head against Vectara HHEM-2.1-Open is included. To our knowledge, GSAR is the first published groundedness framework coupling evidence-typed scoring with tiered recovery under an explicit compute budget.

</details>


### [206] [On the Existence of an Inverse Solution for Preference-Based Reductions in Argumentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22958)
*Alessio Zaninotto,Bruno Yun,Nir Oren,Srdjan Vesic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于偏好的论辩框架（PAF）的逆问题：即给定论辩图、标记和语义，判断是否存在偏好关系能产生该标记。在完全语义下，针对四种主流的偏好规约方法，该问题在多数情况下可在多项式时间内求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关注偏好如何影响攻击转化为击败的正向问题，但缺乏反向研究：已知期望的论证标记结果时，是否能推导出产生该结果的偏好关系。该逆问题在偏好 elicitation（偏好获取）和可解释性等领域有重要应用价值，因此需要填补这一研究空白。

Method: 形式化定义了PAF逆问题：以论辩图、标记和语义为输入，判断是否存在偏好关系能生成目标标记。针对完全语义，系统分析了四种最广泛使用的基于偏好的规约方法，对每种方法分别考察逆问题的计算复杂性。

Result: 证明了在四种主流偏好规约方法下，PAF逆问题在大多数情况下可以在多项式时间内解决，给出了具体的算法或证明。

Conclusion: PAF逆问题在实际应用中是计算上可行的，这为偏好获取和论辩结果的可解释性提供了理论基础和计算保障，表明从结果反推偏好关系的任务是高效的。

Abstract: Preference-based argumentation frameworks (PAFs) extend Dung's approach to abstract argumentation (AAFs) by encoding preferences over arguments. Such preferences control the transformation of attacks into defeats, and different approaches to doing so result in different reductions from a PAF to an AAF. In this paper we consider a PAF inverse problem which takes an argumentation graph, a labelling and a semantics as an input, and outputs a ``yes" or ``no" as to whether there is a preference relation between the arguments which can yield the desired labelling. This inverse problem has applications in areas including preference elicitation and explainability. We consider this problem in the context of the four most widely-used preference based reductions under the complete semantics. We show that in most cases, the problem can be answered in polynomial time.

</details>


### [207] [Information-Theoretic Measures in AI: A Practical Decision Guide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23716)
*Nikolaos Al. Papadopoulos,Konstantinos E. Psannis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文为熵、互信息、整合信息等七种信息论度量提供了一个实用的决策框架，包括度量选择流程图和决策表，帮助AI从业者根据数据类型和应用场景正确选择度量方法和估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息论度量在AI中广泛应用，但度量的选择往往与估计器的假设、失效模式和安全的推断声明相脱节。针对这一痛点，作者旨在弥合度量选择与实践应用之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出一个围绕三个规范性问题构建的决策框架：（i）度量回答什么问题及其AI语境；（ii）何种估计器适用于该数据类型和维度；（iii）最危险的误用是什么。该框架通过度量选择流程图和主决策表两个互补组件实现操作化。

Result: 建立了一个涵盖所有七种度量的实用决策框架，包含选择流程图、主决策表以及连接信息论量与认知构念的标准化Bridge Boxes，并通过表征学习、时序影响分析和进化智能体复杂性三个具体场景进行了演示。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效指导AI从业者正确选择和应用信息论度量，避免常见的误用陷阱，确保推断声明的安全性，适用于AI/ML和决策智能体等多个应用领域。

Abstract: Information-theoretic (IT) measures are ubiquitous in artificial intelligence: entropy drives decision-tree splits and uncertainty quantification, cross-entropy is the default classification loss, mutual information underpins representation learning and feature selection, and transfer entropy reveals directed influence in dynamical systems.
  A second, less consolidated family of measures, integrated information (Phi), effective information (EI), and autonomy, has emerged for characterizing agent complexity. Despite wide adoption, measure selection is often decoupled from estimator assumptions, failure modes, and safe inferential claims.
  This paper provides a practical decision framework for all seven measures, organized around three prescriptive questions for each: (i) what question does the measure answer and in which AI context; (ii) which estimator is appropriate for the data type and dimensionality; and (iii) what is the most dangerous misuse. The framework is operationalized in two complementary artifacts: a measure-selection flowchart and a master decision table. We cover both AI/ML and decision-making agent application domains per measure, with standardized Bridge Boxes linking IT quantities to cognitive constructs. Three worked examples illustrate the framework on concrete practitioner scenarios spanning representation learning, temporal influence analysis, and evolved agent complexity.

</details>


### [208] [Towards Causally Interpretable Wi-Fi CSI-Based Human Activity Recognition with Discrete Latent Compression and LTL Rule Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22979)
*Luca Cotti,Luca Lavazza,Marco Cominelli,Liying Han,Gaofeng Dong,Francesco Gringoli,Mani B. Srivastava,Trevor Bihl,Erik P. Blasch,Daniel O. Brigham,Kara Combs,Lance M. Kaplan,Federico Cerutti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CHARL-TRE方法，实现了基于Wi-Fi CSI的人体活动识别的因果可解释性与符号化控制，在保持竞争力的性能的同时提供了完全符号化和确定性的分类器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度神经模型在CSI人体活动识别中虽然性能强大，但依赖连续潜在表示，不透明且难以修改；而纯符号方法无法直接处理原始CSI数据流。因此需要在因果可解释性、符号可控性和直接处理高维原始信号这三个联合需求下寻找解决方案。

Method: 提出一个全自动且严格解耦的流程：使用带有Gumbel-Softmax潜在变量的分类变分自编码器在容量控制目标下压缩CSI幅度窗口，获得紧凑的离散表示；冻结编码器作为确定性映射得到独热潜在轨迹；对这些轨迹执行因果发现以估计类别条件的时间依赖图；将统计支持的滞后依赖关系转化为线性时序逻辑（LTL）规则，产生完全符号化和确定性的分类器。

Result: CHARL-TRE在保持竞争性能的同时保留了显式的时间和因果结构，证明了基于无监督离散潜在表示的确定性符号分类是无线人体活动识别中端到端黑盒模型的可行替代方案。由于规则定义在离散潜在变量上，特定天线的规则集原则上可以在符号层面组合，无需重新训练编码器即可实现结构化多天线融合。

Conclusion: 确定性符号分类结合无监督离散潜在表示能够有效平衡性能与可解释性，为无线人体活动识别领域提供了一种具有因果可解释性和符号可控性的替代方案。

Abstract: We address Human Activity Recognition (HAR) utilizing Wi-Fi Channel State Information (CSI) under the joint requirements of causal interpretability, symbolic controllability, and direct operation on high-dimensional raw signals. Deep neural models achieve strong predictive performance on CSI-based HAR (CHAR), yet rely on continuous latent representations that are opaque and difficult to modify; purely symbolic approaches, in contrast, cannot process raw CSI streams. We propose a fully automatic and strictly decoupled pipeline in which CSI magnitude windows are compressed by a categorical variational autoencoder with Gumbel-Softmax latent variables under a capacity-controlled objective, yielding a compact discrete representation. The encoder is then frozen and used as a deterministic mapping to one-hot latent trajectories. Causal discovery is performed on these trajectories to estimate class-conditional temporal dependency graphs. Statistically supported lagged dependencies are translated into Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) rules, producing a fully symbolic and deterministic classifier based solely on rule evaluation and aggregation, without any learned discriminative head. Because rules are defined over discrete latent variables, antenna-specific rule sets can in principle be combined at the symbolic level, enabling structured multi-antenna fusion without retraining the encoder. Results from CHAR Latent Temporal Rule Extraction (CHARL-TRE) indicate competitive performance while preserving explicit temporal and causal structure, showing that deterministic symbolic classification grounded in unsupervised discrete latent representations constitutes a viable alternative to end-to-end black-box models for wireless HAR.

</details>


### [209] [LLM-Guided Agentic Floor Plan Parsing for Accessible Indoor Navigation of Blind and Low-Vision People](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23970)
*Aydin Ayanzadeh,Tim Oates*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个智能体框架，通过将单张平面图转换为结构化知识库，为视障人士生成安全的室内导航指令，在真实建筑测试中显著优于现有基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的室内导航解决方案依赖于昂贵的建筑基础设施，这对于盲人和低视力（BLV）群体构成了关键的无障碍挑战，因此亟需一种低成本、可扩展的替代方案。

Method: 系统分为两个阶段：首先是多智能体模块，通过带有迭代重试循环和纠正反馈的自纠正流程，将平面图解析为空间知识图谱；其次是路径规划器，用于生成无障碍导航指令，并配备安全评估智能体评估沿途潜在危险。

Result: 在UMBC数学与心理学大楼MP-1楼层，短、中、长路线成功率分别达到92.31%、76.92%和61.54%，优于最强基线的84.62%、69.23%和53.85%；在MP-3楼层分别达到76.92%、61.54%和38.46%，显著优于基线的61.54%、46.15%和23.08%。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，该工作流相比单次调用LLM基线具有一致的改进，证明了其为视障人士提供可扩展的无障碍室内导航解决方案的可行性。

Abstract: Indoor navigation remains a critical accessibility challenge for the blind and low-vision (BLV) individuals, as existing solutions rely on costly per-building infrastructure. We present an agentic framework that converts a single floor plan image into a structured, retrievable knowledge base to generate safe, accessible navigation instructions with lightweight infrastructure. The system has two phases: a multi-agent module that parses the floor plan into a spatial knowledge graph through a self-correcting pipeline with iterative retry loops and corrective feedback; and a Path Planner that generates accessible navigation instructions, with a Safety Evaluator agent assessing potential hazards along each route. We evaluate the system on the real-world UMBC Math and Psychology building (floors MP-1 and MP-3) and on the CVC-FP benchmark. On MP-1, we achieve success rates of 92.31%, 76.92%, and 61.54% for short, medium, and long routes, outperforming the strongest single-call baseline (Claude 3.7 Sonnet) at 84.62%, 69.23%, and 53.85%. On MP-3, we reach 76.92%, 61.54%, and 38.46%, compared to the best baseline at 61.54%, 46.15%, and 23.08%. These results show consistent gains over single-call LLM baselines and demonstrate that our workflow is a scalable solution for accessible indoor navigation for BLV individuals.

</details>


### [210] [FormalScience: Scalable Human-in-the-Loop Autoformalisation of Science with Agentic Code Generation in Lean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23002)
*Jordan Meadows,Lan Zhang,Andre Freitas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为FormalScience的人机交互智能体流程，用于将非形式化的科学推理转化为形式化可验证代码，并构建了包含200道大学物理题目的FormalPhysics数据集，揭示了当前LLM在物理自动形式化中的语义漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将非形式化的数学推理转化为形式化可验证代码对大语言模型而言是一项重大挑战。在物理学等科学领域，领域特定工具（如狄拉克符号、矢量微积分）带来了额外的形式化挑战，而现代LLM和智能体方法尚未解决这些问题。

Method: 提出了FormalScience——一个领域无关的人机交互智能体流程，使单一领域专家（无需深入的形式语言经验）能够以低经济成本产生语法正确且语义对齐的形式化证明。基于此构建了FormalPhysics数据集，包含200道大学物理问题（主要是量子力学和电磁学）及其Lean4形式化表示。通过零样本提示、带有错误反馈的自我改进以及新颖的多阶段智能体方法，评估了开源模型和专有系统在语句自动形式化任务上的表现。

Result: FormalPhysics相比现有形式化数学基准实现了完美的形式有效性，并展现出更高的语句复杂性。研究首次系统性地刻画了物理自动形式化中的语义漂移现象，提出了诸如符号塌陷和抽象提升等概念，揭示了当完全语义保持无法实现时形式语言所验证的内容。

Conclusion: 发布了代码库和交互式基于UI的FormalScience系统，该系统有助于物理学以外科学领域的自动形式化和定理证明，为科学领域的形式化研究提供了新工具和新见解。

Abstract: Formalising informal mathematical reasoning into formally verifiable code is a significant challenge for large language models. In scientific fields such as physics, domain-specific machinery (\textit{e.g.} Dirac notation, vector calculus) imposes additional formalisation challenges that modern LLMs and agentic approaches have yet to tackle. To aid autoformalisation in scientific domains, we present FormalScience; a domain-agnostic human-in-the-loop agentic pipeline that enables a single domain expert (without deep formal language experience) to produce \textit{syntactically correct} and \textit{semantically aligned} formal proofs of informal reasoning for low economic cost. Applying FormalScience to physics, we construct FormalPhysics, a dataset of 200 university-level (LaTeX) physics problems and solutions (primarily quantum mechanics and electromagnetism), along with their Lean4 formal representations. Compared to existing formal math benchmarks, FormalPhysics achieves perfect formal validity and exhibits greater statement complexity. We evaluate open-source models and proprietary systems on a statement autoformalisation task on our dataset via zero-shot prompting, self-refinement with error feedback, and a novel multi-stage agentic approach, and explore autoformalisation limitations in modern LLM-based approaches. We provide the first systematic characterisation of semantic drift in physics autoformalisation in terms of concepts such as notational collapse and abstraction elevation which reveals what formal language verifies when full semantic preservation is unattainable. We release the codebase together with an interactive UI-based FormalScience system which facilitates autoformalisation and theorem proving in scientific domains beyond physics.https://github.com/jmeadows17/formal-science

</details>


### [211] [FastOMOP: A Foundational Architecture for Reliable Agentic Real-World Evidence Generation on OMOP CDM data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24572)
*Niko Moeller-Grell,Shihao Shenzhang,Zhangshu Joshua Jiang,Richard JB Dobson,Vishnu V Chandrabalan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了FastOMOP，一个开源的多智能体架构，通过在流程边界实施确定性治理来确保基于OMOP CDM的真实世界证据（RWE）生成的安全性，实现了高可靠性并完全阻断了对抗性和超范围请求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管OMOP CDM已标准化了大量患者数据，但从生成真实世界证据（RWE）的过程仍依赖人工。现有的多智能体系统虽表现出潜力，但存在突发行为、协调失败和安全风险，缺乏能够确保RWE生成全过程灵活、安全且可审计的基础设施。

Method: 引入FastOMOP多智能体架构，将治理层、可观测层和编排层与可插拔的智能体团队分离。通过流程边界实施独立于智能体推理的确定性验证，确保受损或产生幻觉的智能体无法绕过安全控制，并通过受控的工具暴露来管理智能体团队。研究使用自然语言转SQL的智能体团队在三个OMOP CDM数据集上进行了验证。

Result: FastOMOP在三个数据集上实现了0.84-0.94的可靠性评分，并且对抗性和超范围请求的阻断率达到完美（100%）。这证明流程边界治理能够提供独立于模型选择的安全保障。

Conclusion: RWE部署中的可靠性差距本质上是架构问题而非模型能力问题。FastOMOP作为一种受治理的架构，为渐进式RWE自动化提供了安全保障。

Abstract: The Observational Medical Outcomes Partnership Common Data Model (OMOP CDM), maintained by the Observational Health Data Sciences and Informatics (OHDSI) collaboration, enabled the harmonisation of electronic health records data of nearly one billion patients in 83 countries. Yet generating real-world evidence (RWE) from these repositories remains a manual process requiring clinical, epidemiological and technical expertise. LLMs and multi-agent systems have shown promise for clinical tasks, but RWE automation exposes a fundamental challenge: agentic systems introduce emergent behaviours, coordination failures and safety risks that existing approaches fail to govern. No infrastructure exists to ensure agentic RWE generation is flexible, safe and auditable across the lifecycle. We introduce FastOMOP, an open-source multi-agent architecture that addresses this gap by separating three infrastructure layers, governance, observability and orchestration, from pluggable agent-teams. Governance is enforced at the process boundary through deterministic validation independent of agent reasoning, ensuring no compromised or hallucinating agent can bypass safety controls. Agent teams for phenotyping, study design and statistical analysis inherit these guarantees through controlled tool exposure. We validated FastOMOP using a natural-language-to-SQL agent team across three OMOP CDM datasets: synthetic data from Synthea, MIMIC-IV and a real-world NHS dataset from Lancashire Teaching Hospitals (IDRIL). FastOMOP achieved reliability scores of 0.84-0.94 with perfect adversarial and out-of-scope block rates, demonstrating process-boundary governance delivers safety guarantees independent of model choice. These results indicate that the reliability gap in RWE deployment is architectural rather than model capability, and establish FastOMOP as a governed architecture for progressive RWE automation.

</details>


### [212] [A Systematic Approach for Large Language Models Debugging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23027)
*Basel Shbita,Anna Lisa Gentile,Bing Zhang,Sungeun An,Shailja Thakur,Shubhi Asthana,Yi Zhou,Saptha Surendran,Farhan Ahmed,Rohan Kulkarni,Yuya Jeremy Ong,Chad DeLuca,Hima Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种系统性的LLM调试方法，将模型视为可观测系统，提供从问题检测到模型 refinement 的结构化、模型无关的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在现代AI工作流中至关重要，但由于其不透明和概率性质，以及在多样化任务和设置中诊断错误的困难，调试这些模型仍然是一个持续的挑战。

Method: 提出了一种系统性的LLM调试方法，将模型视为可观测系统；通过统一评估、可解释性和错误分析实践，提供结构化、模型无关的方法，涵盖从问题检测到模型 refinement 的全流程。

Result: 该方法使从业者能够迭代诊断模型弱点，优化 prompt 和模型参数，并适配数据用于 fine-tuning 或评估；同时适用于缺乏标准化基准和评估标准的场景。

Conclusion: 这种结构化方法不仅加速了故障排查，还促进了基于LLM系统部署的可复现性、透明性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become central to modern AI workflows, powering applications from open-ended text generation to complex agent-based reasoning. However, debugging these models remains a persistent challenge due to their opaque and probabilistic nature and the difficulty of diagnosing errors across diverse tasks and settings. This paper introduces a systematic approach for LLM debugging that treats models as observable systems, providing structured, model-agnostic methods from issue detection to model refinement. By unifying evaluation, interpretability, and error-analysis practices, our approach enables practitioners to iteratively diagnose model weaknesses, refine prompts and model parameters, and adapt data for fine-tuning or assessment, while remaining effective in contexts where standardized benchmarks and evaluation criteria are lacking. We argue that such a structured methodology not only accelerates troubleshooting but also fosters reproducibility, transparency, and scalability in the deployment of LLM-based systems.

</details>


### [213] [A Decoupled Human-in-the-Loop System for Controlled Autonomy in Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23049)
*Edward Cheng,Jeshua Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种解耦的人机回环（HITL）系统架构，将人类监督作为独立的系统组件，并通过四维设计框架实现了跨多智能体环境的可复用、一致且可扩展的人类监督集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体在工作流中被广泛部署，对安全和受控自主性的需求日益增长。现有的人机回环（HITL）实现通常嵌入在应用程序逻辑中，限制了其在多智能体环境中的复用性、一致性和可扩展性。

Method: 提出了一种解耦的HITL系统架构，将人类交互管理与应用工作流分离；引入了一个设计框架，从干预条件、角色解析、交互语义和通信通道四个维度形式化HITL集成；支持将其作为协议级别的关注点实现。

Result: 该架构实现了HITL作为独立系统组件，通过明确的接口和结构化执行模型，实现了选择性且上下文感知的人类参与，同时保持了系统级的一致性。

Conclusion: 通过将HITL外部化并结构化其集成，该系统为代理工作流中的可扩展治理和渐进式自主奠定了基础，并与新兴的智能体通信协议保持一致。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly deployed to execute tasks and make decisions within agentic workflows, introducing new requirements for safe and controlled autonomy. Prior work has established the importance of human oversight for ensuring transparency, accountability, and trustworthiness in such systems. However, existing implementations of Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) mechanisms are typically embedded within application logic, limiting reuse, consistency, and scalability across multi-agent environments.
  This paper presents a decoupled HITL system architecture that treats human oversight as an independent system component within the agent operating environment. The proposed design separates human interaction management from application workflows through explicit interfaces and a structured execution model. In addition, a design framework is introduced to formalize HITL integration along four dimensions: intervention conditions, role resolution, interaction semantics, and communication channel. This framework enables selective and context-aware human involvement while maintaining system-level consistency.
  The approach supports alignment with emerging agent communication protocols, allowing HITL to be implemented as a protocol-level concern. By externalizing HITL and structuring its integration, the system provides a foundation for scalable governance and progressive autonomy in agentic workflows.

</details>


### [214] [Don't Make the LLM Read the Graph: Make the Graph Think](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23057)
*Yuqi Sun,Tianqin Meng,George Liu,Yashraj Panwar,Lakshya Chaudhry,Munasib Ilham,Aman Chadha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究显式信念图是否能提升LLM在合作多智能体推理中的表现。发现集成架构决定图的价值：作为提示时对强模型无益，通过动作筛选机制集成时则至关重要。发现"规划器违抗"现象，不同模型家族表现差异显著。最佳性能提升来自智能体间约定，且深层图存在边际效益递减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究显式信念图是否能改善大语言模型在合作多智能体推理任务中的表现，以及不同的集成方式如何影响其有效性。

Method: 在合作卡牌游戏Hanabi中进行3000+次对照实验，覆盖四个LLM家族。对比两种集成架构：作为提示上下文与通过排序候选列表控制动作选择。进行缩放分析并测试不同图深度的效果。

Result: 1）集成架构决定效果：提示式对强模型装饰性、对弱模型有益（二阶心智80% vs 10%, p<0.0001）；候选列表式对强模型至关重要（100% vs 20%, p<0.001）。2）发现"规划器违抗"现象：部分能力下LLM覆盖正确规划器建议（90%覆盖），Gemini几乎无违抗而Llama 70B达90%。3）智能体间约定优于单智能体干预（+128%, p=0.003）。4）深层图边际效益递减，5人游戏中深层ToM图有害（-1.5分, p=0.029）。

Conclusion: 信念图的价值取决于集成架构设计而非简单存在；模型家族特性影响规划建议遵从度；最佳性能提升来自智能体间约定；浅层图提供最佳成本效益比。

Abstract: We investigate whether explicit belief graphs improve LLM performance in cooperative multi-agent reasoning. Through 3,000+ controlled trials across four LLM families in the cooperative card game Hanabi, we establish four findings. First, integration architecture determines whether belief graphs provide value: as prompt context, graphs are decorative for strong models and beneficial only for weak models on 2nd-order Theory of Mind (80% vs 10%, p<0.0001, OR=36.0); when graphs gate action selection through ranked shortlists, they become structurally essential even for strong models (100% vs 20% on 2nd-order ToM, p<0.001). Second, we identify "Planner Defiance," a model-family-specific failure where LLMs override correct planner recommendations at partial competence (90% override, replicated N=20); Gemini models show near-zero defiance while Llama 70B shows 90%, and models distinguish factual context (deferred to) from advisory recommendations (overridden). Third, full-game evidence confirms inter-agent conventions (+128% over baseline, p=0.003) outperform all single-agent interventions, and individual belief-graph components must be combined to produce gains. Fourth, preliminary scaling analysis (N=10/cell, exploratory) suggests graph depth has diminishing returns: shallow graphs provide the best cost-benefit ratio, while deeper ToM graphs appear harmful at larger player counts (-1.5 pts at 5-player, p=0.029).

</details>


### [215] [Analytica: Soft Propositional Reasoning for Robust and Scalable LLM-Driven Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23072)
*Junyan Cheng,Kyle Richardson,Peter Chin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Analytica智能体架构，基于软命题推理(SPR)原理，通过并行分治框架分解问题、使用工具化LLM接地代理减少偏差，结合鲁棒线性模型递归合成以降低方差，在经济金融政治预测任务上平均准确率提升15.84%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型智能体在复杂现实世界分析任务（如金融预测、科学发现）中存在随机不稳定性，且缺乏可验证的组合结构。现有LLM推理过程难以保证稳定性和可解释性。

Method: 提出Analytica架构，基于软命题推理(SPR)原理：1)将复杂分析重构为估计不同结果命题的软真值过程；2)偏差降低：问题分解为子命题树，使用工具化LLM接地代理（包括新型Jupyter Notebook代理）验证和评分事实；3)方差降低：使用鲁棒线性模型递归合成接地叶节点，平均消除随机噪声。

Result: 在经济、金融和政治预测任务上，相比多样化基础模型平均准确率提升15.84%；与Deep Research接地器配合达到71.06%准确率和最低6.02%方差；Jupyter Notebook接地器实现70.11%准确率，成本降低90.35%，时间减少52.85%；随分析深度增加表现出高度噪声抗性和稳定性能增长，具有近线性时间复杂度。

Conclusion: Analytica成功解决了LLM智能体在复杂分析任务中的稳定性和结构化问题，在保持高准确率的同时显著降低了成本和时间开销，展现出良好的可扩展性、适应性（支持开源LLM和科学领域）以及交互式假设分析能力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly tasked with complex real-world analysis (e.g., in financial forecasting, scientific discovery), yet their reasoning suffers from stochastic instability and lacks a verifiable, compositional structure. To address this, we introduce Analytica, a novel agent architecture built on the principle of Soft Propositional Reasoning (SPR). SPR reframes complex analysis as a structured process of estimating the soft truth values of different outcome propositions, allowing us to formally model and minimize the estimation error in terms of its bias and variance. Analytica operationalizes this through a parallel, divide-and-conquer framework that systematically reduces both sources of error. To reduce bias, problems are first decomposed into a tree of subpropositions, and tool-equipped LLM grounder agents are employed, including a novel Jupyter Notebook agent for data-driven analysis, that help to validate and score facts. To reduce variance, Analytica recursively synthesizes these grounded leaves using robust linear models that average out stochastic noise with superior efficiency, scalability, and enable interactive "what-if" scenario analysis. Our theoretical and empirical results on economic, financial, and political forecasting tasks show that Analytica improves 15.84% accuracy on average over diverse base models, achieving 71.06% accuracy with the lowest variance of 6.02% when working with a Deep Research grounder. Our Jupyter Notebook grounder shows strong cost-effectiveness that achieves a close 70.11% accuracy with 90.35% less cost and 52.85% less time. Analytica also exhibits highly noise-resilient and stable performance growth as the analysis depth increases, with a near-linear time complexity, as well as good adaptivity to open-weight LLMs and scientific domains.

</details>


### [216] [Towards Automated Ontology Generation from Unstructured Text: A Multi-Agent LLM Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23090)
*Abid Talukder,Maruf Ahmed Mridul,Oshani Seneviratne*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过对比单智能体与多智能体架构，发现将本体构建分解为多个角色（规划优先、工件驱动）的方案能显著提升本体结构质量并适度改善查询能力，为可扩展的自动化本体工程提供了可审计的新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用大语言模型（LLM）从非结构化自然语言自动生成形式化本体仍面临核心挑战，目前尚不清楚哪些架构设计选择决定了生成质量，以及现有方法为何失败。

Method: 以特定领域的保险合同为对象进行受控实验。首先建立单智能体LLM基线并识别失败模式；随后提出一种多智能体架构，将本体构建分解为四个工件驱动角色：领域专家、管理者、编码者和质量保证者。评估维度包括架构质量（由异构LLM评委组评估）和功能可用性（通过能力问题驱动的SPARQL评估及检索增强生成评估）。

Result: 多智能体方法显著提高了结构质量，并适度增强了可查询性，这些收益主要源于前置规划阶段。

Conclusion: 研究表明，规划优先、工件驱动的生成方式是通往可扩展、可审计的自动化本体工程的一条有前景的路径。

Abstract: Automatically generating formal ontologies from unstructured natural language remains a central challenge in knowledge engineering. While large language models (LLMs) show promise, it remains unclear which architectural design choices drive generation quality and why current approaches fail. We present a controlled experimental study using domain-specific insurance contracts to investigate these questions. We first establish a single-agent LLM baseline, identifying key failure modes such as poor Ontology Design Pattern compliance, structural redundancy, and ineffective iterative repair. We then introduce a multi-agent architecture that decomposes ontology construction into four artifact-driven roles: Domain Expert, Manager, Coder, and Quality Assurer. We evaluate performance across architectural quality (via a panel of heterogeneous LLM judges) and functional usability (via competency question driven SPARQL evaluation with complementary retrieval augmented generation based assessment). Results show that the multi-agent approach significantly improves structural quality and modestly enhances queryability, with gains driven primarily by front-loaded planning. These findings highlight planning-first, artifact-driven generation as a promising and more auditable path toward scalable automated ontology engineering.

</details>


### [217] [PhySE: A Psychological Framework for Real-Time AR-LLM Social Engineering Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23148)
*Tianlong Yu,Yang Yang,Ziyi Zhou,Jiaying Xu,Siwei Li,Tong Guan,Kailong Wang,Ting Bi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PhySE框架，通过VLM预训练实现实时画像生成和引入自适应心理智能体，解决了AR-LLM社会工程攻击中的冷启动延迟和静态策略问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AR-LLM社会工程攻击存在两个瓶颈：一是现有RAG方法在初始交互阶段存在关键延迟，影响实时画像生成；二是现有方法依赖手工设计的静态攻击策略，缺乏心理学理论基础。

Method: 提出PhySE框架，包含两个核心创新：(1)基于VLM的社会语境训练，通过预训练视觉语言模型实现快速实时画像生成；(2)自适应心理智能体，根据目标响应动态部署不同类别的心理策略。

Result: 通过IRB批准的用户研究评估了PhySE，招募60名参与者，收集了涵盖多种社交场景的360个标注对话的新颖数据集。

Conclusion: PhySE框架成功解决了AR-LLM社会工程攻击的实际应用瓶颈，为该领域研究提供了新的数据集和方法论基础。

Abstract: The emerging threat of AR-LLM-based Social Engineering (AR-LLM-SE) attacks (e.g. SEAR) poses a significant risk to real-world social interactions. In such an attack, a malicious actor uses Augmented Reality (AR) glasses to capture a target visual and vocal data. A Large Language Model (LLM) then analyzes this data to identify the individual and generate a detailed social profile. Subsequently, LLM-powered agents employ social engineering strategies, providing real-time conversation suggestions, to gain the target trust and ultimately execute phishing or other malicious acts. Despite its potential, the practical application of AR-LLM-SE faces two major bottlenecks, (1) Cold-start personalization, Current Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) methods introduce critical delays in the earliest turns, slowing initial profile formation and disrupting real-time interaction, (2) Static Attack Strategies, Existing approaches rely on fixed-stage, handcrafted social engineering tactics that lack foundation in established psychological theory.
